     resubscribes anyone, so it goes through at once with a short note. The button waits for a reason (no late "Pick why first.") */
  function dncHtml(c) {
    var id = String(c.id), st = S.dnc && S.dnc.id === id ? S.dnc : {};
    var err = '<span class="w152err" data-w283dncerr id="w283dncerr-' + E(id) + '" role="status">' + E(st.err || '') + '</span>';
    if (c.do_not_contact) {
      return '<div class="w152line">' + E(dncWords(c)) + '. <span class="w283m">No email of any kind, no texts and no WhatsApp. Receipts are still issued and kept, not emailed.</span></div>' +
        '<div class="w283acts"><button class="w152btn sm soft" data-w283dncclear="' + E(id) + '">' + I('x') + ' Clear do not contact</button></div>' +
        '<p class="w283m">Clearing it does not resubscribe them.</p>' + err;
    }
    var r = dncReasonOk(st.reason) ? st.reason : '';
    return (st.note ? '<div class="w283said ok" role="status" data-w283note>' + E(st.note) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w283acts"><select class="w152sel" data-w283dncr="' + E(id) + '" aria-label="Why they are not to be contacted" aria-describedby="w283dnch-' + E(id) + ' w283dncerr-' + E(id) + '"><option value="">Why\u2026</option>' +
      DNC_REASONS.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === r ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<button class="w152btn sm soft" data-w283dnc="' + E(id) + '"' + (r && !st.ask ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + I('lock') + ' Do not contact</button></div>' +
      (st.ask ? '<div class="w283cf" data-w283cf><b>Set do not contact for ' + E(c.full_name) + '?</b> This unsubscribes them from everything. Clearing it later will not resubscribe them.' +
          '<div class="w283acts"><button class="w152btn sm danger" data-w283dncyes="' + E(id) + '">Yes, set do not contact</button><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w283dncno="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div></div>'
        : '<p class="w283m" id="w283dnch-' + E(id) + '">Pick why, then Do not contact. It stops every email (a receipt is still issued and kept, not emailed), texts and WhatsApp, and unsubscribes them.</p>') + err;
  }
  function saveDnc(id, reason) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var sid = String(c.id);
    if (reason !== null && !dncReasonOk(reason)) { S.dnc = { id: sid, err: 'Pick why first.' }; contactSheet(c.id); dncFocus('[data-w283dncr]'); return; }
    S.dnc = { id: sid, err: '', reason: reason || '' };
    var failed = function (m) { S.dnc = { id: sid, err: 'Not saved: ' + String(m).slice(0, 160), reason: reason || '' }; contactSheet(c.id); dncFocus(reason ? '[data-w283dncr]' : '[data-w283dncclear]'); };
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_set_do_not_contact', { p_contact: Number(c.id), p_reason: reason })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) { failed(r.error.message || r.error); return; }
      var before = c.do_not_contact ? dncWords(c) : null, st = lower(c.email_status);
      /* what crm_set_do_not_contact did (wave281): with a reason, the flag and every opt-out; with none, the flag only (never a resubscribe) */
      applyLocal(c.id, reason ? { do_not_contact: true, dnc_reason: reason, email_status: st === 'bounced' || st === 'complained' ? c.email_status : 'unsubscribed', sms_opt_out: true }
        : { do_not_contact: false, dnc_reason: null });
      if (reason) audit(c.id, 'do_not_contact', before, 'do not contact: ' + reason);   /* clearing is logged by the function itself */
      S.dnc = reason ? null : { id: sid, note: 'Cleared. This did not resubscribe them.' };
      toastS(reason ? 'Do not contact is set' : 'Do not contact is cleared');
      contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
      dncFocus(reason ? '[data-w283dncclear]' : '[data-w283dncr]');   /* focus stays where the control was */
    }, function (e) { failed((e && e.message) || e); });
  }
  function dncCancel(id) { S.dnc = { id: String(id), reason: (S.dnc && S.dnc.reason) || '' }; contactSheet(id); dncFocus('[data-w283dnc]'); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w152sheet')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w283dnc]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var id0 = el.getAttribute('data-w283dnc'), sl = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w283dncr]'), r0 = sl && sl.value ? sl.value : '';
      if (!dncReasonOk(r0)) { saveDnc(id0, ''); return; }
      S.dnc = { id: String(id0), ask: true, reason: r0, err: '' }; contactSheet(id0); dncFocus('[data-w283dncno]');   /* the safe choice has the focus */
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w283dncyes]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveDnc(el.getAttribute('data-w283dncyes'), (S.dnc && S.dnc.reason) || ''); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w283dncno]'))) { e.preventDefault(); dncCancel(el.getAttribute('data-w283dncno')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w283dncclear]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveDnc(el.getAttribute('data-w283dncclear'), null); return; }
  });
  /* picking a reason enables the button in place: no repaint, so the focus and the select stay (a closed select on Windows fires change on each arrow key) */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.hasAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-w283dncr')) return;
    var v = dncReasonOk(t.value) ? t.value : '';
    S.dnc = { id: String(t.getAttribute('data-w283dncr')), reason: v, err: '' };
    var b = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w283dnc]'); if (b) b.disabled = !v;
    var er = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w283dncerr]'); if (er) er.textContent = '';
    var nt = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w283note]'); if (nt) nt.remove();
  });
  var css283 = document.createElement('style'); css283.id = 'w283css';
  css283.textContent = ''
    + '.w283dnc{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px;margin:10px 0 0;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(179,64,47,.08);color:' + FLAG + ';font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45;}'
    + '.w283dnc .w152i{width:16px;height:16px;flex:none;margin-top:2px;}.w283dnc>span{min-width:0;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;column-gap:10px;row-gap:2px;}'
    + '.w283dncm{color:#6B6965;font-weight:500;}'
    + '.w283acts{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:4px 0;}.w283acts .w152sel{width:auto;}'
    + '.w283m{color:#6B6965;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.45;}'
    + '.w283cf{background:rgba(179,64,47,.06);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 11px;margin:6px 0;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;animation:w283in 140ms ease-out;}'
    + '.w283said{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;font-size:13px;}.w283said.ok{color:' + GREEN + ';margin:2px 0 4px;}'
    + '.w283first{margin:8px 0 4px;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.45;}'
    + '.w283 button:focus-visible,.w283 select:focus-visible,.w283 input:focus-visible,.w283cf button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GREEN + ';outline-offset:2px;box-shadow:0 0 0 5px rgba(220,165,39,.35);}'
    /* task 10b: the confirmation fades in, reduced motion included (opacity is not movement) */
    + '@keyframes w283in{from{opacity:0}to{opacity:1}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css283);
  function permForm(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var until = c.implied_consent_until ? dayOf(c.implied_consent_until) : (c._last ? addYears(c._last, 2) : '');
    var spam = lower(c.email_status) === 'complained';
    var html = '<h2>Email permission</h2><div class="sub">' + E(c.full_name) + '. Their opt-outs decide what they get; the consent they gave is kept as history.</div><div class="w152form">' +
      field('Permission', sel('email_consent', [['', 'Not recorded'], ['express', 'Express consent: they said yes to our emails'], ['implied', 'Implied consent: an existing relationship'], ['none', 'No consent']], c.email_consent || ''), true) +
      field('Where it came from', inp('consent_source', c.consent_source, 'the website form, a sign-up sheet, an email'), true) +
      field('Date they said yes', inp('consent_at', c.consent_at ? dayOf(c.consent_at) : '', '', 'date'), false, 'express consent') +
      field('Implied consent runs until', inp('implied_consent_until', until, '', 'date'), false, 'two years after the last gift, membership or volunteering') +
      '<label class="chk full"><input type="checkbox" data-f="unsub"' + (c._perm === 'unsub' ? ' checked' : '') + (spam ? ' disabled aria-describedby="w283spam"' : '') + '> They unsubscribed: no newsletters, fundraising or volunteer emails</label>' +
      (spam ? '<div class="note" data-w283spam id="w283spam">They marked one of our emails as spam, so this stays ticked: a spam complaint cannot be cleared here.</div>' : '') +   /* final review M4; task 10b: the box is disabled too */
      '<label class="chk full"><input type="checkbox" data-f="sms_opt_out"' + (c.sms_opt_out ? ' checked' : '') + '> No text messages</label>' +
      (c.do_not_contact ? '<div class="note" data-w281dnc>Do not contact is on. Clear it first to change these.</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="note">' + E(RULE_TEXT) + ' Express and implied consent are kept on the record as history.</div>' +   /* task 10b: Steel's rule of 5 Nov 2026, not the CASL note */
      '</div><div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err" data-w152err></span><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152back="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button>' + (c.do_not_contact ? '' : '<button class="w152btn" data-w152savep="' + E(id) + '">' + I('check') + ' Save</button>') + '</div>';
    if (c.do_not_contact) html = html.replace(/<(input|select) /g, '<$1 disabled ');   /* final review I1: do not contact holds these (the database too), so the form only shows them */
    openSheet(html, 'perm:' + id, true);
  }
  function orgForm(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    load('partners').then(function (ps) {
      if (S.sheet !== 'c:' + id && S.sheet !== 'org:' + id) return;
      var list = (ps || []).filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'declined'; });
      var html = '<h2>Link an organization</h2><div class="sub">' + E(c.full_name) + ' and an organization from Partners: a sponsor\u2019s contact, a school\u2019s club president, a funder\u2019s program officer.</div><div class="w152form">' +
        (list.length ? field('Organization', '<select data-f="partner_id"><option value="">Choose</option>' + list.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.id) + '">' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>', true) +
          field('Their role there', inp('role_title', '', 'President, Program officer, Owner'), true) +
          '<label class="chk full"><input type="checkbox" data-f="is_primary"> Their main organization</label>'
          : '<div class="note">No organization in Partners yet. Add it on the Partners page first.</div>') +
        '</div><div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err" data-w152err></span><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152back="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button>' + (list.length ? '<button class="w152btn" data-w152saveorg="' + E(id) + '">' + I('check') + ' Link</button>' : '') + '</div>';
      openSheet(html, 'org:' + id, true);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- saving ---------- */
  async function audit(id, field, oldV, newV) {
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'crm_contact', entity_id: String(id), field: field, old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV).slice(0, 500), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV).slice(0, 500), changed_by: actor() }); } catch (e) { /* the audit trail is best effort */ }
  }
  /* the row after a save: the database's answer when there is one, else what we sent */
  function applyLocal(id, patch) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return null;
    Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; });
    if (patch.emails) c.primary_email = (patch.emails[0] || '').toLowerCase() || null;
    shape(c);
    return c;
  }
  async function saveContact(id) {
    var v = formVals(), isNew = id === 'new';
    var c = isNew ? null : contact(id);
    if (!v.full_name) { showErr('Give their name.'); return; }
    var emails = uniq(splitList(v.emails).map(function (e) { return e.toLowerCase(); }));
    var bad = emails.filter(function (e) { return !EMAIL_RE.test(e); });
    if (bad.length) { showErr('This does not look like an email: ' + bad[0]); return; }
    var phones = uniq(splitList(v.phones));
    var np = nameParts(v.full_name);
    var fixedKinds = c ? c.kinds.filter(function (k) { return KIND_HAND.indexOf(k) < 0; }) : [];
    var kinds = uniq(fixedKinds.concat(v._kinds.filter(function (k) { return k !== 'volunteer' || isNew; })));
    var row = { full_name: v.full_name, first_name: np.first, last_name: np.last, emails: emails, phones: phones, address: v.address || null, city: v.city || null,
      province: v.province || null, postal_code: v.postal_code ? v.postal_code.toUpperCase() : null, language: v.language || null, kinds: kinds, notes: v.notes || null, job_title: v.job_title || null, updated_by: actor() };
    try {
      if (isNew) {
        /* the same person twice is the most common CRM mistake: stop at an email or a phone we already have */
        var byEmail = (S.contacts || []).filter(function (x) { return x.emails.some(function (e) { return emails.indexOf(lower(e)) >= 0; }); })[0];
        var byPhone = (S.contacts || []).filter(function (x) { return x._digits.some(function (d) { return phones.map(digits).indexOf(d) >= 0; }); })[0];
        var clash = byEmail || byPhone;
        var warn = function (html) {
          showErr('');
          var box = document.querySelector('#w152sheet .w152form'); if (!box) return;
          var old = box.querySelector('.w152warn'); if (old) old.remove();
          box.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<div class="w152warn">' + html + '</div>');
        };
        if (byEmail && kinds.indexOf('volunteer') >= 0) {
          warn('<b>Already in the CRM.</b> ' + E(byEmail.full_name) + ' has this email, and a volunteer is added once. <button class="w152mini" data-w152open="' + E(byEmail.id) + '">Open ' + E(byEmail.full_name) + '</button>');
          return;
        }
        if (clash && S.confirm !== 'dup:' + clash.id) {
          S.confirm = 'dup:' + clash.id;
          warn('<b>Already in the CRM?</b> ' + E(clash.full_name) + ' has the same ' + (byEmail ? 'email' : 'phone') + '. <button class="w152mini" data-w152open="' + E(clash.id) + '">Open ' + E(clash.full_name) + '</button> or press Add them again to add a new contact anyway.');
          return;
        }
        row.source = 'hub'; row.created_by = actor();
        if (v.perm) { row.email_consent = v.perm; row.consent_source = v.perm_source || null; if (v.perm === 'express') row.consent_at = new Date().toISOString(); if (v.perm === 'implied') row.implied_consent_until = addYears(today(), 2); }
        var newId = null;
        if (kinds.indexOf('volunteer') >= 0) {
          /* a new volunteer: their volunteer record first; the database makes (or links) their contact from it */
          var vr = await db.from('volunteer_people').insert({ full_name: row.full_name, emails: emails, phones: phones, address: row.address, roles: [], tags: [] }).select('id');
          if (vr && vr.error) throw vr.error;
          var vid = vr && vr.data && vr.data[0] && vr.data[0].id;
          if (vid != null) {
            var cr = await db.from('crm_contacts').select('id').eq('volunteer_id', vid).limit(1);
            newId = cr && cr.data && cr.data[0] && cr.data[0].id;
            if (newId != null) {
              var ur = await db.from('crm_contacts').update(row).eq('id', newId).select('id');
              if (ur && ur.error) throw ur.error;
            }
          }
          row.volunteer_id = vid != null ? vid : null;
        }
        if (newId == null) {
          var ir = await db.from('crm_contacts').insert(row).select('id');
          if (ir && ir.error) throw ir.error;
          newId = ir && ir.data && ir.data[0] && ir.data[0].id;
        }
        if (newId == null) newId = 'tmp' + Date.now();
        var nc = shape(Object.assign({ id: newId, kinds: [], tags: [], emails: [], phones: [], created_at: new Date().toISOString(), hub_servings: 0, legacy_servings: 0, legacy_hours: 0, donation_total: 0, donation_count: 0, org_names: [], volunteer_roles: [], volunteer_tags: [] }, row));
        S.contacts.push(nc); BY_ID[String(nc.id)] = nc; S.confirm = null;
        audit(newId, 'created', null, row.full_name);
        S.detail[String(newId)] = { ints: [], gifts: [], hist: [], roster: [], events: [], orgs: [], at: Date.now(), chapters: [], birth: '', age: null };
        var xnew = await saveExtras(newId);   /* wave283: chapters and the birth date */
        if (xnew.length) S.note = { id: String(newId), msg: 'Added, but not saved: ' + listJoin(xnew) + '. Open Edit to try again.' };
        toastS(row.full_name + ' is in the CRM');
        try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#crm/c/' + newId); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        contactSheet(newId); repaintBehind();
        return;
      }
      /* wave176: one record: the contact is the person, and the Volunteer Manager reads the same row (no second copy to write) */
      var res = await db.from('crm_contacts').update(row).eq('id', c.id).select('id');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      if (res && Array.isArray(res.data) && !res.data.length) { showErr('Could not save: the CRM is not open to you.'); return; }
      var changed = ['full_name', 'emails', 'phones', 'address', 'city', 'province', 'postal_code', 'language', 'kinds', 'notes', 'job_title'].filter(function (k) { return JSON.stringify(c[k] == null ? null : c[k]) !== JSON.stringify(row[k] == null ? null : row[k]); });
      applyLocal(c.id, row);
      if (changed.length) audit(c.id, 'details', null, changed.join(', '));
      var xerr = await saveExtras(c.id);   /* wave283: chapters and the birth date */
      if (xerr.length) { showErr('Saved the details, but not ' + listJoin(xerr) + '. Try again.'); repaintBehind(); return; }
      toastS('Saved'); contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
    } catch (e) { showErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160)); }
  }
  async function savePerm(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    if (c.do_not_contact) { showErr('Do not contact is on. Clear it first to change these.'); return; }   /* final review I1 */
    var v = formVals();
    var row = { email_consent: v.email_consent || null, consent_source: v.consent_source || null, updated_by: actor(), sms_opt_out: !!v.sms_opt_out };
    if (v.email_consent === 'express') row.consent_at = v.consent_at ? v.consent_at + 'T12:00:00Z' : (c.consent_at || new Date().toISOString());
    else row.consent_at = v.email_consent ? (c.consent_at || null) : null;
    if (v.email_consent === 'implied') { if (!v.implied_consent_until) { showErr('Say until when the implied consent runs (two years after the last gift or volunteering).'); return; } row.implied_consent_until = v.implied_consent_until; }
    else row.implied_consent_until = null;
    var st = lower(c.email_status);
    if (st === 'complained') { /* final review M4: a spam complaint is never changed from here (task 10b: not even into unsubscribed) */ }
    else if (v.unsub) row.email_status = 'unsubscribed';
    else if (st === 'unsubscribed') row.email_status = 'unknown';
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_contacts').update(row).eq('id', c.id).select('id');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      if (res && Array.isArray(res.data) && !res.data.length) { showErr('Could not save: the CRM is not open to you.'); return; }
      var before = c._perm;
      applyLocal(c.id, row);
      audit(c.id, 'email_permission', PERM[before][0], PERM[c._perm][0] + (row.consent_source ? ' (' + row.consent_source + ')' : ''));
      toastS('Saved'); contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
    } catch (e) { showErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160)); }
  }
  async function saveTags(id, tags) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var before = c.tags.slice();
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_contacts').update({ tags: tags, updated_by: actor() }).eq('id', c.id).select('id');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      applyLocal(c.id, { tags: tags });
      audit(c.id, 'tags', before.join(', '), tags.join(', '));
      contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
      var t = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w152tagin]'); if (t) { try { t.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save the tag: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  async function saveLog(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var o = document.getElementById('w152sheet'); if (!o) return;
    var k = (o.querySelector('[data-w152logk]') || {}).value || 'note', when = (o.querySelector('[data-w152logd]') || {}).value || today(), text = String((o.querySelector('[data-w152logt]') || {}).value || '').trim();
    var errEl = o.querySelector('[data-w152logerr]');
    if (!text) { if (errEl) errEl.textContent = 'Write what happened first.'; return; }
    if (when > today()) { if (errEl) errEl.textContent = 'That date is in the future.'; return; }
    var L = LOGS[k] || LOGS.note;
    var row = { contact_id: c.id, kind: L[0], direction: L[1], channel: L[0] === 'note' ? null : L[0], body: text, occurred_at: when === today() ? new Date().toISOString() : when + 'T12:00:00Z', source: 'hub', created_by: actor() };
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_interactions').insert(row).select('*');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      var saved = Object.assign({ id: 'tmp' + Date.now(), created_at: new Date().toISOString() }, row, (res && res.data && res.data[0]) || {});
      var d = S.detail[String(c.id)]; if (d) d.ints.unshift(saved);
      if (L[1] !== 'internal' && (!c.last_activity_at || String(row.occurred_at) > String(c.last_activity_at))) applyLocal(c.id, { last_activity_at: row.occurred_at });
      toastS('Saved to ' + (c.first_name || c.full_name) + '\u2019s timeline'); contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
    } catch (e) { if (errEl) errEl.textContent = 'Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140); }
  }
  async function deleteLog(id, intId) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    if (S.confirm !== 'del:' + intId) { S.confirm = 'del:' + intId; toastS('Press Delete again to remove it from the timeline'); return; }
    S.confirm = null;
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_interactions').delete().eq('id', intId).select('id');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      if (res && Array.isArray(res.data) && !res.data.length) { toastS('Only the person who wrote it (or an admin) can delete it'); return; }
      var d = S.detail[String(c.id)]; if (d) d.ints = d.ints.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(intId); });
      audit(c.id, 'timeline', 'deleted an entry', null);
      toastS('Deleted'); contactSheet(c.id);
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  async function saveOrg(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var v = formVals();
    if (!v.partner_id) { showErr('Choose the organization.'); return; }
    var d = S.detail[String(c.id)];
    if (d && d.orgs.some(function (o) { return String(o.partner_id) === String(v.partner_id); })) { showErr('They are already linked to it.'); return; }
    var row = { contact_id: c.id, partner_id: Number(v.partner_id), role_title: v.role_title || null, is_primary: !!v.is_primary, created_by: actor() };
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_contact_orgs').insert(row).select('*');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      var saved = Object.assign({ id: 'tmp' + Date.now() }, row, (res && res.data && res.data[0]) || {});
      if (d) d.orgs.push(saved);
      var p = partnerOf(v.partner_id);
      if (p && c.org_names.indexOf(p.name) < 0) applyLocal(c.id, { org_names: c.org_names.concat([p.name]) });
      audit(c.id, 'organization', null, p ? p.name : v.partner_id);
      toastS('Linked'); contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
    } catch (e) { showErr('Could not link: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160)); }
  }
  async function unlinkOrg(linkId) {
    var id = String(S.sheet || '').replace(/^c:/, ''); var c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    if (S.confirm !== 'unlink:' + linkId) { S.confirm = 'unlink:' + linkId; toastS('Press \u00d7 again to unlink the organization'); return; }
    S.confirm = null;
    try {
      var res = await db.from('crm_contact_orgs').delete().eq('id', linkId).select('id');
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      var d = S.detail[String(c.id)], gone = null;
      if (d) { gone = d.orgs.filter(function (o) { return String(o.id) === String(linkId); })[0]; d.orgs = d.orgs.filter(function (o) { return String(o.id) !== String(linkId); }); }
      var p = gone ? partnerOf(gone.partner_id) : null;
      if (p) applyLocal(c.id, { org_names: c.org_names.filter(function (n) { return n !== p.name; }) });
      audit(c.id, 'organization', p ? p.name : linkId, null);
      toastS('Unlinked'); contactSheet(c.id); repaintBehind();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not unlink: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  /* ======================= possible duplicates (#crm/dupes) ======================= */
  function sideHtml(c, other) {
    var lines = [];
    if (c.emails.length) lines.push('<div class="ln">' + c.emails.map(function (e) { return other.emails.some(function (x) { return lower(x) === lower(e); }) ? '<b>' + E(e) + '</b>' : E(e); }).join(', ') + '</div>');
    if (c.phones.length) lines.push('<div class="ln">' + c.phones.map(function (p) { var d = digits(p); return d.length >= 7 && other._digits.indexOf(d) >= 0 ? '<b>' + E(p) + '</b>' : E(p); }).join(', ') + '</div>');
    var facts = [];
    if (c._served) facts.push(plural(c._served, 'serving', 'servings'));
    if (Number(c.donation_count)) facts.push(plural(Number(c.donation_count), 'gift', 'gifts'));
    if (c._roles.length) facts.push(c._roles.map(roleName).join(', '));
    lines.push('<div class="ln m">' + E(['From ' + sourceName(c.source)].concat(facts).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>');
    if (c.created_at) lines.push('<div class="ln m">In the CRM since ' + E(fmtDay(c.created_at)) + '</div>');
    return '<div class="w152ds"><div class="top">' + avatar(c) + '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="nm">' + E(c.full_name) + '</div><div class="ks" style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:3px">' + kindsHtml(c) + '</div></div></div>' +
      lines.join('') + '<div class="act"><button class="w152btn sm" data-w152keep="' + E(c.id) + '" data-drop="' + E(other.id) + '">' + I('check') + ' Keep this one</button>' +
      '<button class="w152mini" data-w152open="' + E(c.id) + '">Open</button></div></div>';
  }
  function pairHtml(p) {
    var a = contact(p.a), b = contact(p.b);
    var why = p.on.length > 1 ? 'Same email and phone' : p.on[0] === 'email' ? 'Same email' : 'Same phone';
    var cf = '';
    var m = /^merge:(\d+):(\d+)$/.exec(S.confirm || '');
    if (m && ((m[1] === String(a.id) && m[2] === String(b.id)) || (m[1] === String(b.id) && m[2] === String(a.id)))) {
      var keep = contact(m[1]), drop = contact(m[2]);
      cf = '<div class="w152cf"><span>Merge <b>' + E(drop.full_name) + '</b> into <b>' + E(keep.full_name) + '</b>? Their emails, phones, tags, notes and history move to ' + E(keep.full_name) +
        (keep.volunteer_id && drop.volunteer_id ? ', and their two volunteer records become one (with both rosters)' : '') + '. The Hub cannot undo it.</span>' +
        '<span style="display:flex;gap:6px"><button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w152cancel="1">Cancel</button><button class="w152btn danger sm" data-w152merge="' + E(keep.id) + '" data-drop="' + E(drop.id) + '">' + I('merge') + ' Merge</button></span></div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w152dp" data-pair="' + E(p.a + '-' + p.b) + '"><div class="why">' + I('merge') + E(why) + ' <span>' + E(p.values.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="two">' + sideHtml(a, b) + sideHtml(b, a) + '</div>' + cf +
      '<div class="bot"><button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w152notdup="' + E(p.a + '-' + p.b) + '">' + I('x') + ' Not the same person</button></div></div>';
  }
  function paintDupes(root) {
    var pairs = dupePairs();
    root.innerHTML = tabsHtml() +
      '<div class="w152count" style="max-width:760px;line-height:1.5">Two contacts with the same email or phone. Keep the right one and the other one\u2019s emails, phones, tags, notes and history move to it. Family members often share a phone: mark those as different people and they leave this list.</div>' +
      (S.err.dupes && !S.dupes.length ? '<div class="w152empty">The duplicates could not load (' + E(S.err.dupes) + ').</div>' :
        pairs.length ? '<div class="w152dl">' + pairs.map(pairHtml).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w152empty">' + I('check') + ' No possible duplicates right now.</div>');
  }
  async function merge(keepId, dropId) {
    var keep = contact(keepId), drop = contact(dropId); if (!keep || !drop) return;
    var btn = document.querySelector('[data-w152merge="' + keepId + '"]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('crm_merge_contacts', { p_keep: Number(keepId), p_drop: Number(dropId) });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      /* what the database did, on the page at once; the next visit reloads the numbers */
      applyLocal(keep.id, {
        emails: uniq(keep.emails.concat(drop.emails)), phones: uniq(keep.phones.concat(drop.phones)), tags: uniq(keep.tags.concat(drop.tags)), kinds: uniq(keep.kinds.concat(drop.kinds)),
        hub_servings: (Number(keep.hub_servings) || 0) + (keep.volunteer_id && drop.volunteer_id && keep.volunteer_id !== drop.volunteer_id ? (Number(drop.hub_servings) || 0) : 0),
        legacy_servings: (Number(keep.legacy_servings) || 0) + (Number(drop.legacy_servings) || 0), donation_total: (Number(keep.donation_total) || 0) + (Number(drop.donation_total) || 0),
        donation_count: (Number(keep.donation_count) || 0) + (Number(drop.donation_count) || 0), org_names: uniq(keep.org_names.concat(drop.org_names))
      });
      S.contacts = S.contacts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(dropId); }); delete BY_ID[String(dropId)];
      S.dupes = (S.dupes || []).filter(function (d) { return String(d.contact_id) !== String(dropId) && String(d.duplicate_id) !== String(dropId); });
      delete S.detail[String(keepId)]; delete S.detail[String(dropId)];
      S.at.contacts = 0; S.at.dupes = 0; S.confirm = null;
      toastS(drop.full_name + ' merged into ' + keep.full_name);
      paint();
    } catch (e) { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; toastS('Could not merge: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); }
  }
  async function notDup(key) {
    var ab = String(key).split('-').map(Number); if (ab.length !== 2) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('crm_dupe_dismissals').insert({ a_id: Math.min(ab[0], ab[1]), b_id: Math.max(ab[0], ab[1]), dismissed_by: actor() });
      if (r && r.error && !/duplicate key/i.test(String(r.error.message || ''))) throw r.error;
      S.dupes = (S.dupes || []).filter(function (d) { var a = Math.min(Number(d.contact_id), Number(d.duplicate_id)), b = Math.max(Number(d.contact_id), Number(d.duplicate_id)); return !(a === Math.min(ab[0], ab[1]) && b === Math.max(ab[0], ab[1])); });
      toastS('Marked as different people');
      paint();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); }
  }

  /* ======================= sources (#crm/sources) ======================= */
  function loadCounts() {
    if (S.counts && Date.now() - (S.counts.at || 0) < 120000) return Promise.resolve(S.counts);
    var n = function (p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r && !r.error && typeof r.count === 'number' ? r.count : null; }, function () { return null; }); };
    return Promise.all([
      n(db.from('crm_volunteer_history').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })),
      n(db.from('crm_interactions').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('kind', 'website_message')),
      n(db.from('crm_donations').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }))
    ]).then(function (r) { S.counts = { hist: r[0], msgs: r[1], gifts: r[2], at: Date.now() }; return S.counts; });
  }
  function paintSources(root) {
    var all = S.contacts || [], by = {}, perm = {};
    all.forEach(function (c) { by[c.source || ''] = (by[c.source || ''] || 0) + 1; perm[c._perm] = (perm[c._perm] || 0) + 1; });
    var srcRows = Object.keys(by).sort(function (a, b) { return by[b] - by[a]; }).map(function (s) {
      var d = SOURCES[s] || [s ? String(s).replace(/_/g, ' ') : 'Unknown', ''];
      return '<div class="w152srow"><b>' + by[s] + '</b><div><div class="tt">' + E(d[0]) + '</div>' + (d[1] ? '<p>' + E(d[1]) + '</p>' : '') + '</div><span></span></div>';
    }).join('');
    var k = S.counts || {};
    var st = function (n, none) { return n == null ? '<span class="st next">Checking\u2026</span>' : n > 0 ? '<span class="st on">' + Number(n).toLocaleString('en-CA') + ' in</span>' : '<span class="st next">' + E(none) + '</span>'; };
    var keela = by.keela || 0;
    var next = [
      ['Volunteer history from the old website', 'Every event each volunteer signed up for, was confirmed for and served at, from the Django site.', st(k.hist, 'Next')],
      ['Messages from the website', 'What people write through the contact form on welfareavenue.com, on the sender\u2019s timeline.', st(k.msgs, 'Next')],
      ['Gifts and tax receipts from Keela', 'Every donation with its receipt, on the donor\u2019s timeline and in their total.', st(k.gifts, 'Later')],
      ['The Keela export', 'Donors and supporters from Keela, merged with the people already here.', keela ? '<span class="st on">' + keela + ' in</span>' : '<span class="st next">When it arrives</span>']
    ].map(function (r) { return '<div class="w152srow nx"><div><div class="tt">' + E(r[0]) + '</div><p>' + E(r[1]) + '</p></div>' + r[2] + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var ok = all.filter(function (c) { return c.emails.length && c._perm !== 'unsub' && c._perm !== 'bounced' && !c.do_not_contact; }).length;   /* final review M6: Steel's rule (RULE_TEXT) */
    var pr = [['express', 'said yes to our emails'], ['implied', 'with an implied consent that has not run out'], ['unknown', 'with no consent on file (the rule still emails them)'], ['none', 'recorded as no consent (the rule still emails them)'], ['unsub', 'who unsubscribed'], ['bounced', 'whose address bounces']]
      .filter(function (x) { return perm[x[0]]; }).map(function (x) { return '<div class="w152srow"><b>' + perm[x[0]] + '</b><div><div class="tt">' + permChip(x[0]) + '</div><p>' + E('Contacts ' + x[1] + '.') + '</p></div><span></span></div>'; }).join('');
    var rev = all.filter(function (c) { return c.volunteer_needs_review; }).length, noEm = all.filter(function (c) { return !c.emails.length; }).length, noPh = all.filter(function (c) { return !c.phones.length; }).length, pairs = S.dupes ? dupePairs().length : 0;
    var tidy = [[pairs, 'possible duplicates', 'Same email or phone on two contacts.', '#crm/dupes'], [rev, 'volunteer records to review', 'Flagged in the Volunteer Manager when they were imported.', ''],
      [noEm, 'contacts with no email', '', ''], [noPh, 'contacts with no phone', '', '']].map(function (x) {
      return '<div class="w152srow"><b>' + x[0] + '</b><div><div class="tt">' + E(x[1].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + x[1].slice(1)) + '</div>' + (x[2] ? '<p>' + E(x[2]) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>' + (x[3] && x[0] ? '<button class="w152mini" data-w152go="' + x[3] + '">Check them ' + I('arrow') + '</button>' : '<span></span>') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    root.innerHTML = tabsHtml() + '<div class="w152grid2">' + keelaHtml() +
      '<div class="w152card"><h3>Where they come from <span>' + plural(all.length, 'contact', 'contacts') + '</span></h3>' + srcRows + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w152card"><h3>What joins next</h3>' + next + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w152card"><h3>Email permission <span>' + plural(ok, 'contact', 'contacts') + ' can get a newsletter today</span></h3>' + pr +
      '<p class="w152pnote">Sends follow one rule: ' + E(RULE_TEXT) + '</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w152card"><h3>To tidy</h3>' + tidy + '</div></div>';
    if (!S.counts) loadCounts().then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'sources') paint(); });
    /* wave159a: what Leaving Keela reads */
    var need = ['forms', 'autos', 'kcfg', 'gifts'].filter(function (k) { return !S[k] && !S.loading[k]; });
    if (need.length) Promise.all(need.map(function (k) { return load(k); })).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'sources') paint(); });
  }

  /* ---------- the Gifts tab (wave153): every gift with its donor, campaign, platform and receipt ---------- */
  function fyOf(day) { var d = dayOf(day); if (!d) return ''; var y = Number(d.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(d.slice(5, 7)); return String(m >= 10 ? y + 1 : y); } /* the fiscal year ends September 30 */
  function giftDonor(g) { var c = contact(g.contact_id); return c ? c.full_name : ''; }
  function giftHay(g) { var c = contact(g.contact_id); return fold([giftDonor(g), c ? c.emails.join(' ') : '', g.campaign, g.designation, g.platform, g.receipt_number, g.external_id, g.notes].join(' ')); }
  function giftMatches(g) {
    var u = S.ui;
    if (u.gplat && lower(g.platform) !== u.gplat) return false;
    if (u.gyear && fyOf(g.donated_at) !== u.gyear) return false;
    if (u.gq) { var words = fold(u.gq).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean), hay = giftHay(g); if (!words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; })) return false; }
    return true;
  }
  var RFD = { part: ['Part refunded', '#B9851B'], all: ['Refunded', MUTED] }; /* wave161 */
  function platName(p) { p = lower(p); return { zeffy: 'Zeffy', keela: 'Keela', stripe: 'Stripe', cash: 'Cash', cheque: 'Cheque', hub: 'The Hub', paypal: 'PayPal' }[p] || (p ? p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1) : 'Unknown'); }
  function giftRow(g) {
    var c = contact(g.contact_id), nm = c ? c.full_name : 'Contact ' + g.contact_id;
    var rs = rcptFor(g.id), rule = rs ? [rs.receipt_status, rs.receipt_note] : receiptRule(g);
    var rc = g.receipt_number ? '<span class="rc">' + (g.receipt_url ? '<a href="' + E(g.receipt_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Receipt ' + E(g.receipt_number) + ' ' + I('ext') + '</a>' : 'Receipt ' + E(g.receipt_number)) + (g.receipt_issued_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDay(g.receipt_issued_at)) : '') + '</span>'
      : '<span class="rc">' + gchip(RSTS, rule[0]) + '<div class="m">' + E(rule[0] === 'due' ? 'for ' + cents(eligibleOf(g)) : rule[1]) + '</div></span>';
    var kind = g.kind && g.kind !== 'donation' ? ' ' + gchip(GKIND, g.kind) : '';
    /* task 10b: amounts with their cents, right-aligned in tabular figures; each cell named, so a phone lays the row out as a card (donor and
       amount first) and a long name, email or campaign wraps inside the card instead of running off it */
    return '<div class="w152row w158row" role="listitem" data-w158open="' + E(g.id) + '" tabindex="0" aria-label="Open the gift">' +
      '<span class="wh">' + E(fmtDay(g.donated_at)) + '</span>' +
      '<span class="dn"><a href="#crm/c/' + E(g.contact_id) + '">' + E(nm) + '</a>' + (c && c.emails[0] ? '<div class="m">' + E(c.emails[0]) + '</div>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="amt"><b class="w152num">' + E(cents(g.amount)) + '</b>' + (g.recurring ? '<small> monthly</small>' : '') + kind + (Number(g.refunded_amount) > 0 ? ' <span class="rfd">' + gchip(RFD, Number(g.amount) > 0 ? 'part' : 'all') + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="cp">' + E([g.campaign, g.designation].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') || '\u2013') + (g.tribute_kind ? '<div class="m">' + E((g.tribute_kind === 'memory' ? 'In memory of ' : 'In honour of ') + (g.tribute_name || '')) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="m">' + E(platName(g.platform)) + (g.method ? ' \u00b7 ' + E((GMETH.filter(function (m) { return m[0] === g.method; })[0] || ['', g.method])[1]) : '') + '</div></span>' +
      '<span class="rx">' + rc + '</span></div>';
  }
  function paintGifts(root) {
    var u = S.ui;
    if (!S.gifts || !S.grcpt) { root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + busy('Loading the gifts\u2026'); Promise.all([load('gifts'), load('grcpt')]).then(function () { if (u.tab === 'gifts') paint(); }); return; }
    if (GF.view === 'receipts') {
      if (!GF.cfg || !S.rcpts) { root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + busy('Loading the receipt settings\u2026'); Promise.all([GF.cfg ? null : loadRcfg(), S.rcpts ? null : load('rcpts')]).then(function () { if (u.tab === 'gifts') paint(); }); return; }
      root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + receiptsHtml(); rbSigLoad(); return;
    }
    if (GF.view === 'online') { /* wave161: the donation page, monthly gifts, what Stripe told the Hub */
      if (!S.dcfg || !S.plans || !S.sevents || !S.dtries) { root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + busy('Loading online giving\u2026'); Promise.all([load('dcfg'), load('plans'), load('sevents'), load('dtries')]).then(function () { if (u.tab === 'gifts') paint(); }); return; }
      root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + onlineHtml(); return;
    }
    var all = S.gifts, list = all.filter(giftMatches);
    var total = 0, donors = {}, receipted = 0, years = {}, plats = {};
    all.forEach(function (g) { total += Number(g.amount) || 0; donors[g.contact_id] = 1; if (g.receipt_number) receipted++; var fy = fyOf(g.donated_at); if (fy) years[fy] = 1; plats[lower(g.platform) || ''] = 1; });
    var fyNow = fyOf(today()), thisFy = all.filter(function (g) { return fyOf(g.donated_at) === fyNow; }), thisTot = thisFy.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + (Number(g.amount) || 0); }, 0);
    var sel = list.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + (Number(g.amount) || 0); }, 0);
    var shown = list.slice(0, u.gshow), filtered = u.gq || u.gplat || u.gyear;
    var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.hasAttribute && document.activeElement.hasAttribute('data-w152gq');
    var caret = focus && document.activeElement.selectionStart;
    root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() +
      '<div class="w152stats">' +
      /* task 10b (Steel's decision 15): whole dollars only in these big headline totals; every list and table shows the cents */
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + E(money(Math.round(total))) + '</b><span>given, all time (' + plural(all.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + E(money(Math.round(thisTot))) + '</b><span>this fiscal year (to 30 Sep ' + E(fyNow) + ', ' + plural(thisFy.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + Object.keys(donors).length.toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>' + (Object.keys(donors).length === 1 ? 'donor' : 'donors') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + receipted.toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>with a tax receipt</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w152bar"><label class="w152q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w152gq="1" value="' + E(u.gq) + '" placeholder="Search a donor, campaign, receipt number or platform" aria-label="Search the gifts"></label>' +
      '<select class="w152sel" data-w152gplat aria-label="Platform"><option value="">Every platform</option>' + Object.keys(plats).sort().map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p) + '"' + (u.gplat === p ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(platName(p)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="w152sel" data-w152gyear aria-label="Fiscal year"><option value="">Every year</option>' + Object.keys(years).sort().reverse().map(function (y) { return '<option value="' + E(y) + '"' + (u.gyear === y ? ' selected' : '') + '>FY ' + E(y) + ' (Oct ' + (Number(y) - 1) + ' to Sep ' + E(y) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      (can('gifts_edit') ? '<button class="w152btn" data-w158new="1">' + I('plus') + ' Record a gift</button>' : '') + '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152go="#crm/gifts/receipts">' + I('note') + ' Receipts</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152go="#crm/gifts/online">' + I('card') + ' Online giving</button></div>' + (GF.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(GF.msg) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w152count">' + (filtered ? plural(list.length, 'gift matches', 'gifts match') + ' (' + E(cents(sel)) + ') \u00b7 <button class="w152mini" data-w152gclear="1">' + I('x') + 'Clear the filters</button>' : plural(all.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ', newest first') +
      (shown.length < list.length ? ' \u00b7 showing the first ' + shown.length : '') + '</div>' +
      (shown.length ? '<div class="w152list w153gifts" role="list"><div class="w152lh" aria-hidden="true"><span>When</span><span>Donor</span><span class="amt">Amount</span><span>Campaign and platform</span><span>Receipt</span></div>' + shown.map(giftRow).join('') + '</div>' +
        (shown.length < list.length ? '<button class="w152btn soft w152more" data-w152gmore="1">Show ' + Math.min(60, list.length - shown.length) + ' more</button>' : '')
        : '<div class="w152empty">' + (all.length ? 'No gift matches. Try another word, or <button class="w152mini" data-w152gclear="1">clear the filters</button>.' : 'No gifts in the CRM yet. Zeffy joins next; until then the Keela export is the record.') + '</div>');
    if (focus) { var inp = root.querySelector('[data-w152gq]'); if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  var gqT = null;
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-w152gq')) return;
    var v = t.value; clearTimeout(gqT);
    gqT = setTimeout(function () { S.ui.gq = v; S.ui.gshow = 60; paint(); }, 160);
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w152root')) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152gplat')) S.ui.gplat = t.value;
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152gyear')) S.ui.gyear = t.value;
    else return;
    S.ui.gshow = 60; paint();
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152gclear]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.ui.gq = ''; S.ui.gplat = ''; S.ui.gyear = ''; S.ui.gshow = 60; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152gmore]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.ui.gshow += 60; paint(); return; }
  });

  /* ======================= the Email tab (wave154): sending from welfareavenue.com, the templates, the send log, the CASL rule =======================
     Steel (27/09/26 plan, step 2): Resend from welfareavenue.com, branded templates, a CASL footer, suppression that follows each
     contact's permission, a send log on the timeline, test sends to Steel only. Registered charity (713048502RR0001) confirmed
     28/09/26: implied consent applies (two years after a gift or a serving). The tab talks to the mail-send function; nothing
     goes out in test mode except to the test recipients. */
  var MAIL = { st: null, err: '', busy: '', prev: null, edit: null, form: false, ferr: '', msg: '', sendc: null };
  var MSTATUS = { queued: ['Queued', MUTED], sent: ['Sent', TEAL], delivered: ['Delivered', GREEN], delayed: ['Delayed', '#B9851B'], bounced: ['Bounced', FLAG], complained: ['Complaint', FLAG], failed: ['Failed', FLAG], suppressed: ['Not sent', MUTED] };
  var MKIND = { transactional: ['Confirmation', TEAL], newsletter: ['Newsletter', PURPLE], fundraising: ['Appeal', '#B9851B'], test: ['Test', MUTED] };
  var DSTATUS = { verified: ['Verified', GREEN], pending: ['Waiting for the records', '#B9851B'], not_started: ['Waiting for the records', '#B9851B'], failed: ['Records not found', FLAG], temporary_failure: ['Checking again', '#B9851B'] };
  LOADERS.templates = function () { return db.from('crm_email_templates').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true }); };
  LOADERS.sends = function () { return db.from('crm_email_sends').select('id,contact_id,to_email,template_key,kind,subject,status,is_test,permission_basis,error,sent_at,delivered_at,opened_at,clicked_at,bounced_at,created_at,created_by,meta').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200); };

  /* the function behind the tab; a refused call comes back as {error} with the function's own words */
  function mailFn(action, body) {
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.functions) return Promise.resolve({ error: 'offline' });
    return Promise.resolve(db.functions.invoke('mail-send', { body: Object.assign({ action: action }, body || {}) })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) {
        var ctx = r.error.context;
        if (ctx && typeof ctx.json === 'function') return ctx.json().then(function (j) { return { error: (j && j.error) || r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' }; }, function () { return { error: r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' }; });
        return { error: r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' };
      }
      return (r && r.data) || {};
    }).catch(function (e) { return { error: String((e && e.message) || e) }; });
  }
  function mchip(map, key) { var d = map[key] || [key || '?', MUTED]; return '<span class="w154chip" style="background:' + d[1] + '17;color:' + d[1] + '"><i style="background:' + d[1] + '"></i>' + E(d[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function testList() { var s = MAIL.st && MAIL.st.settings ? MAIL.st.settings.mail_test_recipients : ''; return String(s || '').toLowerCase().split(/[,;\s]+/).filter(Boolean); }
  function canTest() { return testList().indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0; }
  function mailAdmin() { return MAIL.st ? !!MAIL.st.admin : amAdmin(); }
  function tplByKey(k) { return (S.templates || []).filter(function (t) { return t.key === k; })[0] || null; }
  /* what a contact may receive today, by Steel's one rule of 5 Nov 2026 (the server checks again before every send, in crm_email_permission):
     do not contact (said first), no address, a bounce or a spam complaint stop everything; an unsubscribe stops newsletters, fundraising and
     volunteer emails; everyone else gets them. Express and implied consent no longer decide anything (task 10b: they are history on the page). */
  function permWhy(c) {
    var st = lower(c.email_status), has = c.emails.length > 0;
    var stop = c.do_not_contact ? 'do not contact' : !has ? 'no email address' : st === 'bounced' ? 'the address bounces' : st === 'complained' ? 'they marked one of our emails as spam' : '';
    var trans = !stop, news = !stop && st !== 'unsubscribed';
    return { news: news, trans: trans, why: stop || (news ? 'they have not unsubscribed' : 'they unsubscribed'),
      twhy: stop === 'do not contact' ? 'do not contact: receipts are issued and kept, not emailed' : stop || 'a working address' };
  }
  function permLine(c) {
    var w = permWhy(c);
    if (!c.emails.length) return '<div class="w152line m w154perm">No email address on file, so nothing can be sent.</div>';
    var yn = function (ok) { return '<b style="color:' + (ok ? GREEN : FLAG) + '">' + (ok ? 'yes' : 'no') + '</b>'; };
    return '<div class="w154perm"><div class="w152line">Newsletters, fundraising and volunteer emails: ' + yn(w.news) + ' <span class="m">(' + E(w.why) + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152line">Receipts and confirmations: ' + yn(w.trans) + ' <span class="m">(' + E(w.twhy) + ')</span></div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the numbers: who can be emailed today ---------- */
  function permStatsHtml(all) {
    var n = { express: 0, implied: 0, unknown: 0, unsub: 0, bounced: 0 };
    all.forEach(function (c) { if (!c.emails.length) return; var k = c._perm === 'none' ? 'unknown' : c._perm; if (n[k] != null) n[k]++; });
    function st(key, v, label, color) { return '<button class="w152st" data-w154perm="' + key + '"><b style="color:' + color + '">' + Number(v).toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>' + E(label) + '</span></button>'; }
    return '<div class="w152stats w154stats">' +
      st('express', n.express, 'said yes to our emails (express consent)', GREEN) +
      st('implied', n.implied, 'gave or served in the last two years (implied consent)', TEAL) +
      st('unknown', n.unknown, 'with an address and no consent on file (the rule still emails them)', MUTED) +   /* task 10b: Steel's rule of 5 Nov 2026 */
      st('unsub', n.unsub + n.bounced, 'unsubscribed or bouncing: never a newsletter', FLAG) + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the sending card: the domain, the settings, the mode ---------- */
  function hostOf(name) { var d = (MAIL.st && MAIL.st.settings && MAIL.st.settings.mail_domain) || 'welfareavenue.com'; var n = String(name || ''); if (n === d) return '@'; return n.replace(new RegExp('\\.' + d.replace(/\./g, '\\.') + '$'), ''); }
  function dnsHtml(dom) {
    var recs = (dom && dom.records) || [];
    if (!recs.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w154dns"><div class="w154dh"><span>Type</span><span>Host (at Namecheap)</span><span>Value</span><span>Status</span></div>' + recs.map(function (r) {
      var val = String(r.value || ''), st = lower(r.status || 'not_started');
      return '<div class="w154dr"><span><b>' + E(r.type) + '</b>' + (r.priority != null ? '<div class="m">priority ' + E(r.priority) + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="m">' + E(r.record || '') + '</div></span>' +
        '<span><code>' + E(hostOf(r.name)) + '</code> <button class="w152mini" data-w152copy="' + E(hostOf(r.name)) + '" aria-label="Copy the host">' + I('copy') + '</button></span>' +
        '<span><code class="v">' + E(val) + '</code> <button class="w152mini" data-w152copy="' + E(val) + '" aria-label="Copy the value">' + I('copy') + 'Copy</button></span>' +
        '<span>' + mchip(DSTATUS, st) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div><p class="w152pnote">Add each record at Namecheap under Advanced DNS (the host is relative to welfareavenue.com; <code>@</code> means the domain itself). Leave the Google MX, SPF and DKIM records as they are. Then Check the domain; verification takes a few minutes once the records are live.</p>';
  }
  function settingsFormHtml(s) {
    return '<div class="w152form w154form">' +
      field('From name', inp('mail_from_name', s.mail_from_name, 'Welfare Avenue')) + field('From address', inp('mail_from_email', s.mail_from_email, 'info@welfareavenue.com'), false, 'on welfareavenue.com') +
      field('Reply-to', inp('mail_reply_to', s.mail_reply_to, 'info@welfareavenue.com')) +
      field('Mode', sel('mail_mode', [['test', 'Test mode: only the test recipients get email'], ['live', 'Live: any contact the rule allows']], s.mail_mode || 'test'), false, 'switching to live is the go-ahead for real sends') +
      field('Test recipients', inp('mail_test_recipients', s.mail_test_recipients, 'name@welfareavenue.com, other@\u2026'), true, 'separated by commas') +
      field('Postal address in the footer', inp('mail_postal_address', s.mail_postal_address, 'Welfare Avenue, 8200 av Bloomfield, Montreal QC H3N 2H9'), true) +
      '</div><div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err">' + E(MAIL.ferr) + '</span><button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w154cancel="1">Cancel</button><button class="w152btn sm" data-w154savecfg="1">' + I('check') + ' Save the settings</button></div>';
  }
  function sendingCardHtml() {
    var st = MAIL.st, s = (st && st.settings) || {}, adm = mailAdmin();
    var head = '<h3>' + I('mail') + ' Sending from ' + E(s.mail_domain || 'welfareavenue.com') + '</h3>';
    if (!st && !MAIL.err) return '<section class="w154card">' + head + '<div class="w152empty">Checking the engine\u2026</div></section>';
    if (MAIL.err && !st) return '<section class="w154card">' + head + '<div class="w152empty">' + I('alert') + ' The email engine did not answer (' + E(MAIL.err) + '). <button class="w152mini" data-w154retry="1">Try again</button></div></section>';
    var dom = st.domain && !st.domain.error ? st.domain : null;
    var dchip = !st.key_present ? mchip({ k: ['Resend key missing', FLAG] }, 'k') : !st.domain_id ? mchip({ k: ['Not set up yet', MUTED] }, 'k') : dom ? mchip(DSTATUS, lower(dom.status)) : mchip({ k: ['Resend did not answer', FLAG] }, 'k');
    var live = (s.mail_mode || 'test') === 'live';
    var lines = '<div class="w154kv"><span>Domain</span><span>' + dchip + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w154kv"><span>From</span><span>' + E(s.mail_from_name || 'Welfare Avenue') + ' &lt;' + E(s.mail_from_email || '') + '&gt;' + (s.mail_reply_to ? '<div class="m">replies to ' + E(s.mail_reply_to) + '</div>' : '') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w154kv"><span>Mode</span><span>' + (live ? mchip({ k: ['Live', GREEN] }, 'k') + ' <span class="m">any contact the rule allows</span>' : mchip({ k: ['Test mode', '#B9851B'] }, 'k') + ' <span class="m">only to ' + E(s.mail_test_recipients || 'nobody yet') + '</span>') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w154kv"><span>Webhook</span><span>' + (st.webhook_id && st.webhook_secret_present ? mchip({ k: ['Connected', GREEN] }, 'k') + ' <span class="m">deliveries, opens, bounces and complaints come back to the log</span>' : mchip({ k: ['Not connected', MUTED] }, 'k')) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w154kv"><span>Last 30 days</span><span>' + plural(st.last30.sent, 'email sent', 'emails sent') + ', ' + plural(st.last30.tests, 'test', 'tests') + (st.last30.bounced ? ', ' + plural(st.last30.bounced, 'bounce', 'bounces') : '') + (st.last30.suppressed ? ', ' + st.last30.suppressed + ' held back by the rule' : '') + (st.last30.failed ? ', ' + st.last30.failed + ' failed' : '') + '</span></div>';
    var acts = [];
    if (adm) {
      if (st.key_present && !st.domain_id) acts.push('<button class="w152btn sm" data-w154act="domain_setup">' + I('spark') + ' Set up the domain in Resend</button>');
      if (st.domain_id) acts.push('<button class="w152btn soft sm" data-w154act="domain_verify">' + I('check') + ' Check the domain</button>');
      if (st.key_present && !(st.webhook_id && st.webhook_secret_present)) acts.push('<button class="w152btn soft sm" data-w154act="webhook_setup">' + I('link') + ' Connect the webhook</button>');
      acts.push('<button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w154cfg="1">' + I('edit') + ' Change the settings</button>');
      acts.push('<button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w154act="refresh_consent" aria-describedby="w154refwhy">' + I('shield') + ' Refresh implied consent</button>');
    }
    /* task 10b (Steel's decision 13): the button stays, with why it still matters under the one rule */
    return '<section class="w154card">' + head + lines + (MAIL.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(MAIL.msg) + '</div>' : '') +
      (acts.length ? '<div class="w154acts">' + acts.join('') + '</div><p class="w152pnote" id="w154refwhy" data-w154refwhy>Refresh implied consent keeps the reason on each record up to date. It doesn\u2019t change who gets email.</p>' : '') + (MAIL.busy ? '<div class="w152count">' + E(MAIL.busy) + '\u2026</div>' : '') +
      (MAIL.form && adm ? settingsFormHtml(s) : '') + (dom ? dnsHtml(dom) : '') +
      (!st.key_present ? '<p class="w152pnote">The Resend key (RESEND_API_KEY) is missing from the function secrets, so nothing can be sent or set up. An admin adds it in Supabase; the Hub never stores it.</p>' : '') +
      '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- the templates ---------- */
  function templatesCardHtml() {
    var list = S.templates || [], adm = mailAdmin();
    var rows = list.map(function (t) {
      var on = MAIL.prev && MAIL.prev.key === t.key, ed = MAIL.edit && MAIL.edit.key === t.key;
      return '<div class="w154tpl' + (t.active ? '' : ' off') + (on ? ' on' : '') + '"><div class="w154tph"><div><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b> ' + mchip(MKIND, t.kind) + (t.active ? '' : ' <span class="m">switched off</span>') + '<div class="m">' + E(t.subject) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="w154tpa"><button class="w152mini" data-w154prev="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('search') + (on ? 'Hide' : 'Preview') + '</button>' +
        (canTest() ? '<button class="w152mini" data-w154test="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('mail') + 'Send me a test</button>' : '') +
        (adm ? '<button class="w152mini" data-w154edit="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('edit') + (ed ? 'Close' : 'Edit') + '</button>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        (window.w305 ? window.w305.row(t, paint, function () { return load('templates', true); }) : '') +   /* wave305: copies and replies */
        (ed ? editFormHtml(t) : '') +
        (on ? (MAIL.prev.html ? '<div class="w154pv"><div class="m">Subject: <b>' + E(MAIL.prev.subject) + '</b>' + (MAIL.prev.preheader ? ' \u00b7 preview text: ' + E(MAIL.prev.preheader) : '') + '</div><iframe class="w154frame" title="Preview of ' + E(t.name) + '" sandbox="" srcdoc="' + E(MAIL.prev.html) + '"></iframe></div>' : '<div class="w152count">' + E(MAIL.prev.error || 'Rendering the preview\u2026') + '</div>') : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('note') + ' Templates <span class="m">' + plural(list.length, 'template', 'templates') + '</span></h3>' +
      '<p class="w152pnote">Each one is wrapped in the brand layout: the logo on green, cream body, gold button, and a footer with the postal address, the charity number, why they got it and the unsubscribe link. <code>{{first_name}}</code> fills in from the contact; <code>{{name|fallback}}</code> falls back when empty.</p>' +
      (rows || '<div class="w152empty">No templates yet.</div>') + '</section>';
  }
  function editFormHtml(t) {
    return '<div class="w152form w154form w154edit">' +
      field('Name', inp('name', t.name), true) + field('Subject', inp('subject', t.subject), true) + field('Preview text', inp('preheader', t.preheader), true, 'the line inboxes show after the subject') +
      field('Body (HTML: h1, h2, p, ul, li, strong, a class="btn")', area('html_body', t.html_body), true) + field('Plain-text version', area('text_body', t.text_body), true, 'what clients without HTML show') +
      '<label class="full"><input type="checkbox" data-f="active"' + (t.active ? ' checked' : '') + '> In use</label>' +
      '</div><div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err">' + E(MAIL.ferr) + '</span><button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w154cancel="1">Cancel</button><button class="w152btn sm" data-w154savetpl="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('check') + ' Save the template</button></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the send log ---------- */
  function sendRow(s) {
    var c = s.contact_id ? contact(s.contact_id) : null, t = tplByKey(s.template_key);
    var who = c ? '<a href="#crm/c/' + E(c.id) + '">' + E(c.full_name) + '</a><div class="m">' + E(s.to_email) + '</div>' : E(s.to_email);
    var marks = [];
    if (s.opened_at) marks.push('opened'); if (s.clicked_at) marks.push('clicked'); if (s.is_test) marks.push('test');
    return '<div class="w154sr"><span>' + E(fmtDay(s.created_at)) + '<div class="m">' + E(String(s.created_by || '').split('@')[0]) + '</div></span><span>' + who + '</span>' +
      '<span>' + E(t ? t.name : (s.template_key || '')) + (s.subject ? '<div class="m">' + E(clip(s.subject, 70)) + '</div>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span>' + mchip(MSTATUS, s.status) + (marks.length ? '<div class="m">' + E(marks.join(', ')) + '</div>' : '') + (s.status === 'suppressed' || s.status === 'failed' ? '<div class="m">' + E(s.permission_basis || s.error || '') + '</div>' : '') + '</span></div>';
  }
  function sendsCardHtml() {
    var list = S.sends || [];
    return '<section class="w154card wide"><h3>' + I('clock') + ' Recent emails <span class="m">' + (list.length ? 'the last ' + list.length : '') + '</span></h3>' +
      (list.length ? '<div class="w154sl"><div class="w154sh"><span>When</span><span>To</span><span>Template</span><span>What happened</span></div>' + list.slice(0, 60).map(sendRow).join('') + '</div>'
        : '<div class="w152empty">Nothing sent yet. The first message is a test to the test recipients once the domain is verified.</div>') + '</section>';
  }
  function ruleHtml() {
    return '<section class="w154card wide w154rule"><h3>' + I('shield') + ' How the rule works</h3>' +
      '<p>' + E(RULE_TEXT) + ' Nobody marked do not contact gets any email. The Hub applies the rule before every send. Every email carries our postal address and an unsubscribe link, and each one sits on the contact\u2019s timeline with what happened to it.</p></section>';   /* wave283: one rule (Steel, 5 Nov 2026) */
  }
  function paintEmail(root) {
    var u = S.ui;
    if (!S.templates || !S.sends) {
      root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + busy('Loading the email centre\u2026');
      Promise.all([load('templates'), load('sends')]).then(function () { if (u.tab === 'email') paint(); });
      return;
    }
    if (!MAIL.st && !MAIL.busy && !MAIL.err) {
      MAIL.busy = 'Checking the engine';
      mailFn('settings').then(function (r) { MAIL.busy = ''; if (r.error) MAIL.err = r.error; else { MAIL.st = r; MAIL.err = ''; } if (u.tab === 'email') paint(); });
    }
    root.innerHTML = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml() + permStatsHtml(S.contacts || []) +
      '<div class="w154grid">' + sendingCardHtml() + templatesCardHtml() + sendsCardHtml() + ruleHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  function refreshMail(after) {
    return Promise.all([load('sends', true), mailFn('settings')]).then(function (r) { if (!r[1].error) MAIL.st = r[1]; if (after) after(); if (S.ui.tab === 'email') paint(); });
  }
  function mailAct(action) {
    if (MAIL.busy) return;
    MAIL.busy = { domain_setup: 'Setting up the domain in Resend', domain_verify: 'Asking Resend to check the records', webhook_setup: 'Connecting the webhook', refresh_consent: 'Refreshing implied consent' }[action] || 'Working';
    MAIL.msg = ''; paint();
    mailFn(action).then(function (r) {
      MAIL.busy = '';
      if (r.error) { MAIL.msg = r.error; paint(); return; }
      if (action === 'refresh_consent' && r.counts) { MAIL.msg = 'Implied consent refreshed: ' + r.counts.updated + ' contacts updated, ' + r.counts.implied + ' with implied consent today, ' + r.counts.express + ' express.'; load('contacts', true).then(function () { paint(); }); }
      else if (action === 'webhook_setup') MAIL.msg = r.note ? 'The webhook was already connected.' : 'Webhook connected; its secret is in the vault.';
      else if (r.domain) MAIL.msg = 'Domain ' + (r.domain.name || '') + ': ' + ((DSTATUS[lower(r.domain.status)] || [r.domain.status])[0]).toLowerCase() + '.';
      mailFn('settings').then(function (s) { if (!s.error) MAIL.st = s; paint(); });
    });
  }
  function previewTpl(key, contactId) {
    MAIL.prev = { key: key, html: '', subject: '', preheader: '' }; paint();
    mailFn('preview', { template_key: key, contact_id: contactId || undefined }).then(function (r) {
      if (!MAIL.prev || MAIL.prev.key !== key) return;
      if (r.error) MAIL.prev.error = r.error; else { MAIL.prev.html = r.html; MAIL.prev.subject = r.subject; MAIL.prev.preheader = r.preheader; }
      paint();
    });
  }
  function sendTest(key) {
    if (MAIL.busy) return;
    MAIL.busy = 'Sending a test to ' + myEmail(); MAIL.msg = ''; paint();
    mailFn('test', { template_key: key, to: myEmail() }).then(function (r) {
      MAIL.busy = '';
      MAIL.msg = r.error ? 'The test did not go: ' + r.error : 'Test sent to ' + (r.to || myEmail()) + ' (' + (r.status || 'sent') + ').';
      refreshMail();
    });
  }
  function saveSettings() {
    var v = formVals(); MAIL.ferr = '';
    var body = { mail_from_name: v.mail_from_name, mail_from_email: v.mail_from_email, mail_reply_to: v.mail_reply_to, mail_mode: v.mail_mode, mail_test_recipients: v.mail_test_recipients, mail_postal_address: v.mail_postal_address };   /* wave283: no fundraising switch (one rule) */
    MAIL.busy = 'Saving the settings'; paint();
    return mailFn('set_settings', body).then(function (r) {
      MAIL.busy = '';
      if (r.error) { MAIL.ferr = r.error; paint(); return; }
      MAIL.form = false; MAIL.msg = r.changed ? 'Settings saved (' + plural(r.changed, 'change', 'changes') + ').' : 'Nothing changed.';
      mailFn('settings').then(function (s) { if (!s.error) MAIL.st = s; paint(); });
    });
  }
  function saveTemplate(key) {
    var v = formVals(); MAIL.ferr = '';
    if (!String(v.subject || '').trim() || !String(v.html_body || '').trim()) { MAIL.ferr = 'A subject and a body are needed.'; paint(); return Promise.resolve(); }
    MAIL.busy = 'Saving the template'; paint();
    return mailFn('save_template', { key: key, name: v.name, subject: v.subject, preheader: v.preheader, html_body: v.html_body, text_body: v.text_body, active: !!v.active }).then(function (r) {
      MAIL.busy = '';
      if (r.error) { MAIL.ferr = r.error; paint(); return; }
      MAIL.edit = null; MAIL.msg = 'Template saved.';
      if (MAIL.prev && MAIL.prev.key === key) MAIL.prev = null;
      load('templates', true).then(function () { paint(); });
    });
  }
  /* formVals reads [data-f] inside #w152sheet; the Email tab's forms live on the page, so read them there too */
  var __formVals152 = formVals;
  formVals = function () {
    var o = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-f]') ? null : document.querySelector('.w152root .w154form');
    if (!o) return __formVals152();
    var v = {};
    o.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) { v[el.getAttribute('data-f')] = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : String(el.value || '').trim(); });
    v._kinds = [];
    return v;
  };

  /* ---------- a contact's page: send them an email ---------- */
  function mailFormHtml(c) {
    /* wave202: a studio one-off or a template still holding [brackets] is not sent from here; the studio writes one for them */
    var list = (S.templates || []).filter(function (t) { return t.active && t.kind !== 'test' && (window.w202 && window.w202.sendable ? window.w202.sendable(t) : !t.one_off); });
    var cur = MAIL.sendc && MAIL.sendc.id === c.id ? MAIL.sendc : (MAIL.sendc = { id: c.id, key: list.length ? list[0].key : '', html: '', subject: '', err: '', msg: '', busy: '' });
    var t = tplByKey(cur.key), w = permWhy(c), ok = t ? (t.kind === 'transactional' ? w.trans : w.news) : false;
    var mode = MAIL.st && MAIL.st.settings ? MAIL.st.settings.mail_mode : 'test';
    var testOnly = mode !== 'live' && testList().indexOf(lower(c.emails[0])) < 0;
    return '<h2>Send an email</h2><div class="sub">To ' + E(c.full_name) + ' &lt;' + E(c.emails[0] || '') + '&gt;. The Hub checks their permission first and logs the send on their timeline.</div>' +
      '<div class="w152form w154form"><label class="full">Template<select data-f="template_key" data-w154tplsel="' + E(c.id) + '">' + list.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + E(x.key) + '"' + (x.key === cur.key ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + ' (' + E((MKIND[x.kind] || [x.kind])[0].toLowerCase()) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div>' +
      (t ? '<div class="w152line m" style="margin-top:8px">' + (ok ? '<b style="color:' + GREEN + '">Allowed</b>: ' + E(t.kind === 'transactional' ? 'a confirmation or reminder, not a commercial message' : w.why) : '<b style="color:' + FLAG + '">Held back</b>: ' + E(w.why) + '. The Hub will not send a ' + E((MKIND[t.kind] || [t.kind])[0].toLowerCase()) + ' to them.') + '</div>' : '') +
      (testOnly ? '<div class="w152line m">' + I('alert') + ' The Hub is in test mode: this address is not a test recipient, so the send would be refused until an admin switches the Email tab to live.</div>' : '') +
      (cur.html ? '<iframe class="w154frame sheet" title="Preview" sandbox="" srcdoc="' + E(cur.html) + '"></iframe>' : '<div class="w152count">' + E(cur.err || (cur.busy || 'Rendering the preview\u2026')) + '</div>') +
      '<div class="w152line m" style="margin-top:8px">' + I('spark') + ' Something more personal? <a href="#crm/studio/ai/to/' + E(c.id) + '" data-w202fromcontact>Write one for ' + E(String(c.full_name || 'them').split(' ')[0]) + ' in the email studio</a>.</div>' +
      '<div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err">' + E(cur.msg) + '</span><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152back="' + E(c.id) + '">Back</button>' +
      '<button class="w152btn" data-w154go="' + E(c.id) + '"' + (!ok || !t || cur.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('mail') + ' Send now</button></div>';
  }
  function mailForm(id) {
    var c = contact(id); if (!c || !c.emails.length) return;
    var open = function () {
      openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true);
      var cur = MAIL.sendc;
      if (cur && cur.key && !cur.html && !cur.busy) {
        cur.busy = 'Rendering the preview';
        mailFn('preview', { template_key: cur.key, contact_id: c.id }).then(function (r) {
          if (!MAIL.sendc || MAIL.sendc.id !== c.id || MAIL.sendc.key !== cur.key) return;
          cur.busy = ''; if (r.error) cur.err = r.error; else { cur.html = r.html; cur.subject = r.subject; }
          if (S.sheet === 'mail:' + c.id) openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true);
        });
      }
    };
    if (!S.templates) load('templates').then(open); else open();
    if (!MAIL.st) mailFn('settings').then(function (r) { if (!r.error) { MAIL.st = r; if (S.sheet === 'mail:' + c.id) openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true); } });
  }
  function sendNow(id) {
    var c = contact(id), cur = MAIL.sendc; if (!c || !cur || cur.id !== c.id || !cur.key) return Promise.resolve();
    cur.busy = 'Sending'; cur.msg = ''; openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true);
    return mailFn('send', { contact_id: c.id, template_key: cur.key }).then(function (r) {
      cur.busy = '';
      if (r.error) { cur.msg = r.error; openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true); return; }
      if (r.status === 'suppressed') { cur.msg = 'Held back by the rule: ' + (r.basis || ''); openSheet(mailFormHtml(c), 'mail:' + c.id, true); return; }
      toastS('Email sent to ' + (r.to || c.emails[0]));
      MAIL.sendc = null; S.sends = null;
      contactSheet(c.id, true);
    });
  }
