  /* ---------- wave196: wiring ---------- */
  function markDirty() {
    AU.dirty = true;
    var d = document.querySelector('#crm .w196dirty'); if (d) { d.textContent = 'Unsaved changes'; d.classList.add('on'); }
    var sv = document.querySelector('#crm .w196tb [data-w157save]'); if (sv) sv.classList.remove('ghost');
  }
  function liveTitle(i) {
    var a = AU.a; if (!a || !a.steps[i]) return;
    var card = document.querySelector('#crm .w196card[data-w196sel="' + i + '"] .w196tx b'); if (card) card.textContent = stepTitle(a.steps[i]);
  }
  function closeMenus() { var ch = false; if (AU.rowMenu) { AU.rowMenu = null; ch = true; } if (AU.edMenu) { AU.edMenu = false; ch = true; } return ch; }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w152root')) return;
    var a = AU.a;
    /* menus close on any click outside them */
    if (!t.closest('.w196mw') && closeMenus() && !t.closest('button,a,select,input,textarea,label,[data-w196sel]')) { paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196rowmenu]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var rid = el.getAttribute('data-w196rowmenu'); AU.rowMenu = AU.rowMenu === rid ? null : rid; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196edmenu]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.edMenu = !AU.edMenu; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196mv]'))) { e.preventDefault(); moveAutoBy(el.getAttribute('data-w196id'), Number(el.getAttribute('data-w196mv'))); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196dupa]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.edMenu = false; dupAuto(el.getAttribute('data-w196dupa')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196nf]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.newFolder = ''; AU.pendingMove = null; paint(); var inp = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196nfin]'); if (inp) inp.focus(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196nfno]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.newFolder = null; AU.pendingMove = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196nfok]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var nm = String((document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196nfin]') || {}).value || '').trim();
      if (!nm) return;
      AU.newFolder = null;
      if (AU.pendingMove) { var pm = AU.pendingMove; AU.pendingMove = null; setFolder(pm, nm); return; }
      if (autoGroups().map(function (g) { return g.name; }).indexOf(nm) < 0) AU.newFolders = (AU.newFolders || []).concat([nm]);
      AU.msg = 'Folder ' + nm + ' made: drag automations into it.'; paint(); return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196fold]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.closed = AU.closed || {}; var fn = el.getAttribute('data-w196fold'); AU.closed[fn] = !AU.closed[fn]; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196rename]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.renaming = el.getAttribute('data-w196rename'); paint(); var ri = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196renin]'); if (ri) { ri.focus(); ri.select(); } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196renno]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.renaming = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196renok]'))) { e.preventDefault(); renameFolder(el.getAttribute('data-w196renok'), (document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196renin]') || {}).value); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196unfold]'))) { e.preventDefault(); removeFolder(el.getAttribute('data-w196unfold')); return; }
    if (!a) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196saveback]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.confirm = ''; saveAuto().then(function (id) { if (id) { AU.dirty = false; AU.a = null; AU.sel = null; go('#crm/automations'); } }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196leave]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.confirm = ''; AU.dirty = false; AU.a = null; AU.sel = null; AU.prev = null; go('#crm/automations'); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196ins]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var at = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w196ins')); AU.ins = AU.ins === at ? null : at; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196paste]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var c = clip(); if (c) insertStep(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w196paste')), editable(cloneStep(c.step, a))); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196dup]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var di = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w196dup')); insertStep(di + 1, cloneStep(a.steps[di], a)); AU.msg = 'Duplicated: the copy is step ' + (di + 2) + '.'; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196copy]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var ci = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w196copy')); setClip(cleanSteps([a.steps[ci]])[0], a.name); AU.msg = 'Step ' + (ci + 1) + ' copied. Use + anywhere, in this automation or another one, and choose Paste.'; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196close]'))) { e.preventDefault(); AU.sel = null; AU.prev = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w196sel]')) && !t.closest('button:not([data-w196sel]),a,select,input,textarea')) {
      e.preventDefault(); var v = el.getAttribute('data-w196sel'); AU.sel = v === 't' ? 't' : Number(v); AU.prev = null; AU.ins = null; AU.cto = ''; paint(); return;
    }
  }, true);
  /* a pasted step comes in the editor's own shape (tags and people as text) */
  function editable(s) {
    if (s.type === 'tag') { s._add = csv(s.add).join(', '); s._rem = csv(s.remove).join(', '); }
    if (s.type === 'notify') s._to = csv(s.to).join(', ');
    return s;
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#crm')) return;
    var card = t.closest('[data-w196sel]');
    if (card && t === card && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); var v = card.getAttribute('data-w196sel'); AU.sel = v === 't' ? 't' : Number(v); AU.prev = null; paint(); var p = document.querySelector('#crm .w196panel input,#crm .w196panel select,#crm .w196panel textarea'); if (p) p.focus(); return; }
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && AU.a && (AU.ins != null || AU.sel != null || AU.edMenu)) { AU.ins = null; AU.edMenu = false; if (!t.closest('.w196panel')) AU.sel = null; paint(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w152root')) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w196tofolder')) {
      var id = t.getAttribute('data-w196tofolder'), v = t.value; if (v === '__') return;
      AU.rowMenu = null;
      if (v === '__new') { AU.newFolder = ''; AU.pendingMove = id; paint(); var inp = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196nfin]'); if (inp) inp.focus(); return; }
      setFolder(id, v); return;
    }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w196cto') && AU.a && t.value) { copyStepTo(Number(t.getAttribute('data-w196cto')), t.value); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.hasAttribute) return;
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && t.hasAttribute('data-w196nfin')) { e.preventDefault(); var b = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w196nfok]'); if (b) b.click(); }
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && t.hasAttribute('data-w196renin')) { e.preventDefault(); renameFolder(t.getAttribute('data-w196renin'), t.value); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.hasAttribute && t.hasAttribute('data-w196nfin')) AU.newFolder = t.value; });

  /* ---------- wave196: drag and drop (automations in the list, steps in the flow) ---------- */
  var DR = { kind: '', id: null, from: -1, over: null };
  function clearDrop() { Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#crm .dropb,#crm .dropa,#crm .dropin,#crm .w196link.drop,#crm .dragging'), function (x) { x.classList.remove('dropb', 'dropa', 'dropin', 'drop', 'dragging'); }); }
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#crm')) return;
    var row = t.closest('[data-w196row]'), card = t.closest('.w196card[data-w157si]');
    if (row) { DR.kind = 'row'; DR.id = row.getAttribute('data-w196row'); row.classList.add('dragging'); }
    else if (card) { DR.kind = 'step'; DR.from = Number(card.getAttribute('data-w157si')); card.classList.add('dragging'); AU.ins = null; }
    else return;
    try { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', DR.kind + ':' + (DR.id || DR.from)); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
  });
  function rowTarget(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return null;
    var row = t.closest('[data-w196row]');
    if (row && row.getAttribute('data-w196row') !== DR.id) { var r = row.getBoundingClientRect(); return { el: row, beside: row.getAttribute('data-w196row'), after: e.clientY > r.top + r.height / 2 }; }
    var zone = t.closest('[data-w196drop]'); if (zone) return { el: zone, folder: zone.getAttribute('data-w196drop') };
    return null;
  }
  function stepTarget(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return null;
    var link = t.closest('.w196link'); if (link) { var b = link.querySelector('[data-w196ins]'); if (b) return { el: link, at: Number(b.getAttribute('data-w196ins')), link: true }; }
    var card = t.closest('.w196card[data-w157si]');
    if (card) { var i = Number(card.getAttribute('data-w157si')), r = card.getBoundingClientRect(), after = e.clientY > r.top + r.height / 2; return { el: card, at: after ? i + 1 : i, after: after }; }
    if (t.closest('.w196card.trig')) return { el: t.closest('.w196card.trig'), at: 0, after: true };
    return null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) {
    if (!DR.kind) return;
    var tg = DR.kind === 'row' ? rowTarget(e) : stepTarget(e); if (!tg) return;
    e.preventDefault(); try { e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move'; } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    if (DR.over === tg.el && DR.overAfter === tg.after) return;
    clearDrop(); DR.over = tg.el; DR.overAfter = tg.after;
    if (DR.kind === 'step') { var d = document.querySelector('#crm .w196card[data-w157si="' + DR.from + '"]'); if (d) d.classList.add('dragging'); }
    if (tg.link) tg.el.classList.add('drop'); else if (tg.folder != null) tg.el.classList.add('dropin'); else tg.el.classList.add(tg.after ? 'dropa' : 'dropb');
  });
  document.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
    if (!DR.kind) return;
    var tg = DR.kind === 'row' ? rowTarget(e) : stepTarget(e), kind = DR.kind, id = DR.id, from = DR.from;
    DR.kind = ''; DR.over = null; clearDrop();
    if (!tg) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if (kind === 'row') { if (tg.beside != null) moveAuto(id, { beside: tg.beside, after: tg.after }); else moveAuto(id, { folder: tg.folder }); }
    else if (AU.a && tg.at !== from && tg.at !== from + 1) moveStep(from, tg.at);
  });
  document.addEventListener('dragend', function () { DR.kind = ''; DR.over = null; clearDrop(); });
  /* ======================= Gifts by hand and receipts (wave158a, 28/09/26) =======================
     The first part of step 6 (gifts and receipts). Staff record the gifts that come without a platform (cheques,
     e-transfers, cash), say what kind of money it is (a donation, a registration or a ticket with the value the person
     received, a sponsorship, a gift in kind), note a tribute, and see for every gift whether a CRA receipt is done, due
     or not possible and why (crm_gift_receipts_v). The receipt settings (the charity's name and address as on file with
     the CRA, where receipts are issued, the numbering, the signatory) are kept by admins; the Hub shows a sample receipt
     with every field the CRA requires and lists the receipts due for a tax year. Issuing official receipts (the number,
     the PDF, the email) comes in wave158b once the treasurer confirms the settings: receipt_mode stays off. */
  var GKIND = { donation: ['Donation', GREEN], registration: ['Registration', TEAL], ticket: ['Ticket', PURPLE], sponsorship: ['Sponsorship', '#B9851B'], in_kind: ['In kind', '#3A6EA5'], other: ['Other', MUTED] };
  var GMETH = [['', 'Not recorded'], ['cheque', 'Cheque'], ['etransfer', 'E-transfer'], ['cash', 'Cash'], ['card', 'Card'], ['online', 'Online'], ['other', 'Other']];
  var RSTS = { receipted: ['Receipted', GREEN], due: ['Receipt due', '#B9851B'], platform: ['Zeffy receipts it', TEAL], no: ['No receipt', MUTED] };
  var GF = { view: '', cfg: null, cfgErr: '', year: null, msg: '', err: '', sample: null };
  LOADERS.grcpt = function () { return pageAll(function (a, b) { return db.from('crm_gift_receipts_v').select('*').order('gift_id', { ascending: true }).range(a, b); }, function (r) { return String(r.gift_id); }); };
  function rcptFor(id) { return (S.grcpt || []).filter(function (r) { return String(r.gift_id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function giftById(id) { return (S.gifts || []).filter(function (g) { return String(g.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  /* receipts always show the cents */
  function cents(n) { var v = Number(n) || 0; try { return v.toLocaleString('en-CA', { style: 'currency', currency: 'CAD', minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }); } catch (e) { return '$' + v.toFixed(2); } }
  function eligibleOf(g) { return Math.max(0, (Number(g.amount) || 0) - (Number(g.advantage_amount) || 0)); }
  /* the same rule as crm_gift_receipts_v, for the form while it is being filled in */
  function receiptRule(g) {
    var amt = Number(g.amount) || 0, adv = Number(g.advantage_amount) || 0;
    if (g.receipt_number) return ['receipted', 'Receipt ' + g.receipt_number];
    if (!g.contact_id) return ['no', 'Pick the donor'];
    if (g.kind === 'sponsorship') return ['no', 'A sponsorship gets an invoice or a thank-you, not a tax receipt'];
    if (g.kind === 'other') return ['no', 'Not a gift'];
    if (amt <= 0) return ['no', 'No amount'];
    if (adv > 0.8 * amt) return ['no', 'What they received is worth more than 80% of the amount'];
    if (g.kind === 'in_kind' && !String(g.in_kind_desc || '').trim()) return ['no', 'Describe the gift in kind first'];
    if (lower(g.platform) === 'zeffy') return ['platform', 'Zeffy issues the receipt'];
    return ['due', 'A receipt is due for ' + cents(eligibleOf(g))];
  }
  function gchip(map, k) { var d = map[k] || [k || '?', MUTED]; return '<span class="w154chip" style="background:' + d[1] + '17;color:' + d[1] + '"><i style="background:' + d[1] + '"></i>' + E(d[0]) + '</span>'; }

  /* ---------- the gift form (a sheet) ---------- */
  function stripeSub(x) { /* wave161: an online gift from the donation page */
    var link = stripeLink(x), bits = [];
    if (x.fee != null) bits.push('Stripe kept ' + cents(x.fee) + (x.net_amount != null ? ', ' + cents(x.net_amount) + ' reached Welfare Avenue' : ''));
    if (Number(x.refunded_amount) > 0) bits.push(cents(x.refunded_amount) + ' refunded' + (x.refunded_at ? ' on ' + fmtDay(x.refunded_at) : '') + (Number(x.amount) > 0 ? ', ' + cents(x.amount) + ' kept' : ''));
    return (x.recurring ? 'A monthly payment' : 'An online gift') + ' through the Hub\u2019s donation page, paid on Stripe: the amount, the date and the donor stay as Stripe recorded them.' +
      (bits.length ? ' ' + E(bits.join('; ')) + '.' : '') + (link ? ' <a href="' + E(link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it in Stripe ' + I('ext') + '</a>' : '');
  }
  function giftForm(id, contactId) {
    var g = id ? giftById(id) : null, isNew = !g, hub = !g || lower(g.platform) === 'hub';
    var mayEdit = can('gifts_edit');   /* wave283: team leads, admins and finance (wa_my_caps); everyone with the CRM reads gifts */
    if (isNew && !mayEdit) { openSheet('<h2>Record a gift</h2><div class="w152err" role="status">' + E(GIFT_REFUSED) + '</div><div class="w152foot"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152close="1">Close</button></div>', 'gift:new', true); return; }
    var x = g || { contact_id: contactId || null, amount: '', donated_at: today(), kind: 'donation', method: 'cheque', campaign: '', designation: '', advantage_amount: 0, advantage_desc: '', in_kind_desc: '', appraiser: '', tribute_kind: '', tribute_name: '', notes: '', platform: 'hub' };
    var c = x.contact_id ? contact(x.contact_id) : null;
    var donor = c ? '<div class="w158donor">' + avatar(c) + '<b>' + E(c.full_name) + '</b><span class="m">' + E(c.emails[0] || '') + '</span></div>'
      : '<input data-f="donor" list="w158donors" placeholder="Type a name or an email" autocomplete="off"><datalist id="w158donors">' + (S.contacts || []).filter(function (k) { return !/^\(no name\)$/i.test(k.full_name || ''); }).slice(0, 3000).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k.full_name + (k.emails[0] ? ' \u00b7 ' + k.emails[0] : '') + ' (#' + k.id + ')') + '"></option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist>';
    var kinds = Object.keys(GKIND).map(function (k) { return [k, GKIND[k][0]]; });
    var ro = hub ? '' : ' disabled';
    var sub = isNew ? 'A cheque, an e-transfer or cash that came in outside Zeffy and Keela. The thank-you automation starts on its own if it is on.' : hub ? 'Recorded in the Hub' + (x.created_by ? ' by ' + E(x.created_by) : '') + '.' : lower(x.platform) === 'stripe' ? stripeSub(x) : 'From ' + E(platName(x.platform)) + ': the amount, the date and the donor stay as ' + E(platName(x.platform)) + ' recorded them.';
    if (!mayEdit) { giftReadView(x, c, id, sub); return; }   /* task 10b (Steel's decision 7): a read view, not a form of disabled fields */
    var html = '<h2>' + (isNew ? 'Record a gift' : 'A gift of ' + E(cents(x.amount))) + '</h2>' +
      '<div class="sub">' + sub + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w152form" data-w158gift="' + E(id || 'new') + '">' +
      field('Donor', donor, true) +
      field('Amount ($)', '<input data-f="amount" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" value="' + E(x.amount) + '"' + ro + '>') + field('Date received', '<input data-f="donated_at" type="date" value="' + E(dayOf(x.donated_at) || today()) + '"' + ro + '>') +
      field('What kind of money', sel('kind', kinds, x.kind || 'donation')) + field('How it came', sel('method', GMETH, x.method || '')) +
      field('Campaign', inp('campaign', x.campaign, 'Rally Paper 2026, the Sunday meals')) + field('Designation', inp('designation', x.designation, 'Toronto, the kitchen')) +
      '<div class="full w158adv"' + (x.kind === 'registration' || x.kind === 'ticket' || x.kind === 'other' || Number(x.advantage_amount) ? '' : ' hidden') + '>' +
        field('What they received ($)', '<input data-f="advantage_amount" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" value="' + E(Number(x.advantage_amount) || 0) + '">', false, 'the meal, the ticket, playing in the tournament') +
        field('Described as', inp('advantage_desc', x.advantage_desc, 'Dinner at the gala')) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="full w158ink"' + (x.kind === 'in_kind' ? '' : ' hidden') + '>' + field('The gift in kind', inp('in_kind_desc', x.in_kind_desc, '40 winter coats'), true, 'the amount is its fair market value') +
        field('Appraised by (optional)', inp('appraiser', x.appraiser, 'Name and address')) + '</div>' +
      field('In honour or memory of', sel('tribute_kind', [['', 'No'], ['honour', 'In honour of'], ['memory', 'In memory of']], x.tribute_kind || '')) + field('Their name', inp('tribute_name', x.tribute_name, 'Their name')) +
      field('Notes', area('notes', x.notes, 'Cheque number, who dropped it off, anything the treasurer should know'), true) +
      '<div class="note full w158rule" data-w158rule>' + E(receiptRule(x)[1]) + '</div>' +
      '</div><div class="w152foot"><span class="w152err" data-w152err role="status"></span>' +
      (!isNew && hub ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158del="' + E(id) + '">' + I('trash') + ' Delete</button>' : '') +   /* wave283: the delete policy is gifts_edit */
      (!isNew ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158sample="' + E(id) + '">' + I('note') + ' Sample receipt</button>' : '') +
      '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152close="1">Cancel</button><button class="w152btn" data-w158save="' + E(id || 'new') + '">' + I('check') + (isNew ? ' Record it' : ' Save') + '</button></div>';
    openSheet(html, 'gift:' + (id || 'new'), true);
  }
  /* task 10b: someone without gifts_edit reads a gift as text they can select and copy (the Contact details layout), with the note on top,
     no field, no save and no delete (row security refuses the writes anyway) */
  function giftReadView(x, c, id, sub) {
    var kv = function (l, v) { return v === '' || v == null ? '' : '<b>' + E(l) + '</b><span>' + v + '</span>'; };
    var meth = (GMETH.filter(function (m) { return m[0] === x.method; })[0] || ['', x.method || ''])[1];
    var rs = rcptFor(x.id), rnote = rs && rs.receipt_note ? rs.receipt_note : receiptRule(x)[1];
    var rows = kv('Donor', c ? E(c.full_name) + (c.emails[0] ? '<span class="m w158rm">' + E(c.emails[0]) + '</span>' : '') : E('Contact ' + (x.contact_id || ''))) +
      kv('Amount', '<span class="w152num">' + E(cents(x.amount)) + '</span>' + (x.recurring ? ' a month' : '')) +
      kv('Date received', E(fmtDay(x.donated_at))) +
      kv('What kind of money', E((GKIND[x.kind] || [x.kind || 'Donation'])[0])) +
      kv('How it came', E(meth || 'Not recorded')) +
      kv('Campaign', E(x.campaign || '')) + kv('Designation', E(x.designation || '')) +
      (Number(x.advantage_amount) ? kv('What they received', '<span class="w152num">' + E(cents(x.advantage_amount)) + '</span>' + (x.advantage_desc ? ' (' + E(x.advantage_desc) + ')' : '')) : '') +
      (x.kind === 'in_kind' ? kv('The gift in kind', E(x.in_kind_desc || '')) + kv('Appraised by', E(x.appraiser || '')) : '') +
      (x.tribute_kind ? kv(x.tribute_kind === 'memory' ? 'In memory of' : 'In honour of', E(x.tribute_name || '')) : '') +
      kv('Notes', x.notes ? '<span class="w158pre">' + E(x.notes) + '</span>' : '') +
      kv('Receipt', E(rnote));
    openSheet('<h2>A gift of ' + E(cents(x.amount)) + '</h2><div class="sub">' + sub + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w158ronote" data-w283ro role="note">' + E(GIFT_REFUSED) + ' This gift is shown read-only.</div>' +
      '<div class="w152kv w158read" data-w158read="' + E(id) + '">' + rows + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w152foot"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158sample="' + E(id) + '">' + I('note') + ' Sample receipt</button><button class="w152btn" data-w152close="1">Close</button></div>', 'gift:' + id, true);
  }
  function giftFormValues(box) {
    var v = {}; box.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) { v[el.getAttribute('data-f')] = el.value; });
    return v;
  }
  function donorFrom(v, box) {
    var id = box.getAttribute('data-w158gift'), g = id !== 'new' ? giftById(id) : null;
    if (g) return g.contact_id;
    var fixed = box.querySelector('.w158donor') ? GF.formContact : null; if (fixed) return fixed;
    var m = /\(#(\d+)\)\s*$/.exec(String(v.donor || '')); return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;
  }
  function refreshRule(box) {
    var v = giftFormValues(box), id = box.getAttribute('data-w158gift'), g = id !== 'new' ? giftById(id) : {};
    var x = Object.assign({}, g, v, { contact_id: donorFrom(v, box) || (g && g.contact_id) });
    var el = box.querySelector('[data-w158rule]'); if (el) el.textContent = receiptRule(x)[1];
    var adv = box.querySelector('.w158adv'); if (adv) adv.hidden = !(v.kind === 'registration' || v.kind === 'ticket' || v.kind === 'other' || Number(v.advantage_amount));
    var ink = box.querySelector('.w158ink'); if (ink) ink.hidden = v.kind !== 'in_kind';
  }
  function saveGift(btn) {
    var sh = document.getElementById('w152sheet'), box = sh && sh.querySelector('[data-w158gift]'); if (!box) return;
    var id = box.getAttribute('data-w158gift'), g = id !== 'new' ? giftById(id) : null, hub = !g || lower(g.platform) === 'hub';
    var v = giftFormValues(box), err = sh.querySelector('[data-w152err]');
    var say = function (m) { if (err) err.textContent = m; };
    if (!can('gifts_edit')) { say(GIFT_REFUSED); return; }
    var cid = donorFrom(v, box), amt = Number(v.amount), adv = Number(v.advantage_amount) || 0;
    if (!cid) { say('Pick the donor from the list (type a name or an email).'); return; }
    if (hub && !(amt > 0)) { say('The amount must be more than $0.'); return; }
    if (hub && (!v.donated_at || v.donated_at > today())) { say('The date received is today or earlier.'); return; }
    if (adv < 0 || (hub && adv > amt) || (!hub && adv > Number(g.amount))) { say('What they received cannot be more than the amount.'); return; }
    if (v.kind === 'in_kind' && !String(v.in_kind_desc || '').trim()) { say('Describe the gift in kind.'); return; }
    if (v.tribute_kind && !String(v.tribute_name || '').trim()) { say('Whose honour or memory is it in?'); return; }
    var row = { kind: v.kind, method: v.method || null, campaign: String(v.campaign || '').trim() || null, designation: String(v.designation || '').trim() || null,
      advantage_amount: adv, advantage_desc: String(v.advantage_desc || '').trim() || null, in_kind_desc: v.kind === 'in_kind' ? String(v.in_kind_desc || '').trim() : null,
      appraiser: v.kind === 'in_kind' ? (String(v.appraiser || '').trim() || null) : null, tribute_kind: v.tribute_kind || null, tribute_name: v.tribute_kind ? String(v.tribute_name || '').trim() : null,
      notes: String(v.notes || '').trim() || null, updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    if (hub) { row.amount = Math.round(amt * 100) / 100; row.donated_at = v.donated_at + 'T12:00:00-04:00'; row.currency = 'CAD'; }
    if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    var q = g ? db.from('crm_donations').update(row).eq('id', g.id).select().single()
      : db.from('crm_donations').insert(Object.assign({ contact_id: cid, platform: 'hub', created_by: actor(), receipt_eligible: row.kind !== 'sponsorship' && row.kind !== 'other' }, row)).select().single();
    Promise.resolve(q).then(function (r) {
      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
      /* wave283: 0 rows (PGRST116 from .single()) or a refused write (42501) is row security saying no */
      if (r && r.error) { say(r.error.code === 'PGRST116' || r.error.code === '42501' ? GIFT_REFUSED : 'Not saved: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
      GF.msg = g ? 'Saved.' : 'Recorded: ' + cents(row.amount) + ' from ' + ((contact(cid) || {}).full_name || 'the donor') + '.';
      Promise.all([load('gifts', true), load('grcpt', true)]).then(function () { closeSheet(true); if (S.detail[String(cid)]) delete S.detail[String(cid)]; if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); });
    });
  }
  function delGift(id) {
    var say = function (m) { var el = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w152err]'); if (el) el.textContent = m; };
    if (!can('gifts_edit')) { say(GIFT_REFUSED); return; }
    Promise.resolve(db.from('crm_donations').delete().eq('id', Number(id)).select('id')).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) { say(r.error.code === '42501' ? GIFT_REFUSED : 'Not deleted: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
      if (r && Array.isArray(r.data) && !r.data.length) { say(GIFT_REFUSED); return; }   /* wave283: 0 rows deleted: row security said no */
      GF.msg = 'Deleted.';
      Promise.all([load('gifts', true), load('grcpt', true)]).then(function () { closeSheet(true); paint(); });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- a sample receipt ---------- */
  function sampleReceipt(id) {
    openSheet('<h2>Sample receipt</h2>' + busy('Putting it together\u2026'), 'rcpt:' + id);
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_sample', { p_gift_id: Number(id) })).then(function (r) {
      if (S.sheet !== 'rcpt:' + id) return;
      if (!r || r.error || !r.data) { openSheet('<h2>Sample receipt</h2><div class="w152err">' + E((r && r.error && (r.error.message || r.error)) || 'It did not come back.') + '</div>', 'rcpt:' + id); return; }
      openSheet(receiptHtml(r.data), 'rcpt:' + id);
    });
  }
  function receiptHtml(s) {
    var line = function (l, v, warn) { return '<div class="w158rl"><span>' + E(l) + '</span><b' + (warn ? ' class="w158miss"' : '') + '>' + E(v) + '</b></div>'; };
    var ws = (s.warnings || []);
    return '<h2>Sample receipt</h2><div class="sub">What the Hub\u2019s receipt for this gift would say, with every field the CRA asks for. It is not numbered, kept or sent: issuing receipts from the Hub starts once the treasurer confirms the settings.</div>' +
      (ws.length ? '<div class="w155cf test"><b>Before this could be a real receipt:</b><ul class="w157ul">' + ws.map(function (w) { return '<li>' + E(w) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w158rc"><div class="w158wm" aria-hidden="true">SAMPLE</div>' +
      '<div class="w158rh"><b>' + E(s.charity_name) + '</b><span>' + E(s.charity_address || '') + '</span><span>Charitable registration number ' + E(s.charity_number || '') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w158rt">Official receipt for income tax purposes</div>' +
      line('Receipt number', (s.serial || '') + ' (sample)') + line('Issued at', s.location) + line('Date issued', s.issued_date) +
      line('Received from', s.donor_name || 'no name on file', !s.donor_name) + line('Address', s.donor_address || 'no address on file', !s.donor_address) +
      line('Date the gift was received', s.gift_date) +
      (s.kind === 'in_kind' ? line('The gift', s.in_kind_desc || '') + (s.appraiser ? line('Appraised by', s.appraiser) : '') : '') +
      line(s.kind === 'in_kind' ? 'Fair market value of the gift' : 'Total amount received', cents(s.amount)) +
      line('Value of the advantage', cents(s.advantage_amount) + (s.advantage_desc ? ' (' + s.advantage_desc + ')' : '')) +
      line('Eligible amount of the gift for tax purposes', cents(s.eligible_amount)) +
      '<div class="w158sig"><span class="w158line"></span><span>' + E(s.signatory_name ? s.signatory_name + (s.signatory_title ? ', ' + s.signatory_title : '') : 'Authorized signature (no signatory yet)') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w158cra">' + E(s.cra) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="m" data-w158bsay role="status"></div>' +
      '<div class="w152foot"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152close="1">Close</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158bpdfsample="1">' + I('note') + ' Download the sample (PDF)</button><button class="w152btn" data-w158print="1">' + I('note') + ' Print the sample</button></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- receipts: settings, the year's receipts due ---------- */
  function loadRcfg() {
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_config')).then(function (r) { GF.cfg = (r && r.data) || {}; GF.cfgErr = r && r.error ? String(r.error.message || r.error) : ''; }, function () { GF.cfg = {}; });
  }
  function receiptsHtml() {
    var cfg = GF.cfg || {}, admin = !!cfg.is_admin;
    var years = {}; (S.grcpt || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r.tax_year) years[r.tax_year] = 1; });
    var ys = Object.keys(years).sort().reverse(), yr = GF.year || String(new Date(today() + 'T12:00:00').getFullYear());
    if (ys.indexOf(yr) < 0) ys.unshift(yr);
    var due = (S.grcpt || []).filter(function (r) { return String(r.tax_year) === String(yr) && r.receipt_status === 'due'; });
    var by = {}; due.forEach(function (r) { var k = String(r.contact_id); (by[k] = by[k] || { id: r.contact_id, gifts: [], total: 0 }); by[k].gifts.push(r); by[k].total += Number(r.eligible_amount) || 0; });
    var donors = Object.keys(by).map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.total - a.total; });
    var total = due.reduce(function (a, r) { return a + (Number(r.eligible_amount) || 0); }, 0);
    var done = (S.grcpt || []).filter(function (r) { return String(r.tax_year) === String(yr) && r.receipt_status === 'receipted'; }).length;
    var set = function (k, label, ph, hint) { return '<label class="w155lb">' + E(label) + (hint ? ' <span class="m">' + E(hint) + '</span>' : '') + '<input class="w152in" data-w158cfg="' + k + '" value="' + E(cfg[k] || '') + '" placeholder="' + E(ph || '') + '"' + (admin ? '' : ' disabled') + '></label>'; };
    return '<div class="w155grid"><div class="w154card"><h3>' + I('note') + ' Receipt settings</h3>' +
      '<p class="m">What every CRA receipt from the Hub will carry. Keela\u2019s 55 receipts stay the legal copies; the numbering here must not repeat Keela\u2019s KEE numbers.</p>' +
      (GF.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(GF.err) + '</div>' : '') + (GF.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(GF.msg) + '</div>' : '') +
      (RB.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(RB.err) + '</div>' : '') + (RB.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(RB.msg) + '</div>' : '') + rbStatusHtml(cfg) +
      set('receipt_charity_name', 'Charity name', 'Welfare Avenue', 'as on file with the CRA') + set('receipt_charity_address', 'Charity address', '', 'as on file with the CRA') +
      '<div class="w154kv"><span>Registration number</span><span>' + E(cfg.mail_charity_number || '') + '</span></div>' +
      set('receipt_location', 'Where receipts are issued', 'Montreal, Quebec') +
      '<div class="w155two">' + set('receipt_prefix', 'Number prefix', 'WA-2026-') + set('receipt_next_number', 'Next number', '1') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w155two">' + set('receipt_signatory_name', 'Signed by', 'Full name') + set('receipt_signatory_title', 'Their title', 'Treasurer') + '</div>' +
      (admin ? '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w158cfgsave="1">' + I('check') + ' Save the settings</button></div>' : '<p class="m">Only an admin changes these.</p>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w154card"><h3>' + I('heart') + ' Receipts due for ' + E(yr) + ' <select class="w152sel w158yr" data-w158year aria-label="Tax year">' + ys.map(function (y) { return '<option' + (y === yr ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(y) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></h3>' +
      '<p class="m">Receipts follow the donor\u2019s tax year (January to December), not the fiscal year. The year-end run gives each donor one receipt for the year.</p>' +
      /* task 10b (UI review row 4): these tiles take their content's width and wrap, so an amount is never cut ($12,000.00 ran into "1 donor") */
      '<div class="w152stats w158due"><div class="w152st"><b>' + E(cents(total)) + '</b><span>eligible, ' + plural(due.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + '</span></div><div class="w152st"><b>' + donors.length.toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>' + (donors.length === 1 ? 'donor' : 'donors') + ' to receipt</span></div><div class="w152st"><b>' + done.toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>already receipted</span></div></div>' +
      rbRunHtml(donors.length, yr) +
      (donors.length ? '<div class="w156fl">' + donors.map(function (d) {
        var c = contact(d.id), noAddr = c && !String(c.address || '').trim();
        return '<div class="w156fr"><span><a href="#crm/c/' + E(d.id) + '">' + E(c ? c.full_name : 'Contact ' + d.id) + '</a><span class="m"> \u00b7 ' + plural(d.gifts.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + (noAddr ? ' \u00b7 <span class="w156warn">no address on file</span>' : '') + '</span></span><span>' + E(cents(d.total)) + '</span>' +
          '<button class="w152mini" data-w158sample="' + E(d.gifts[0].gift_id) + '">Sample</button>' + rbDueActions(d, yr) + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="m">No receipt is due for ' + E(yr) + '.</p>') +
      '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152go="#crm/gifts">Back to the gifts</button></div></div>' + rbIssuedHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  function saveRcfg() {
    var p = {}; document.querySelectorAll('#crm [data-w158cfg]').forEach(function (el) { p[el.getAttribute('data-w158cfg')] = el.value; });
    GF.err = ''; GF.msg = '';
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_config_set', { p: p })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) GF.err = 'Not saved: ' + (r.error.message || r.error); else { GF.cfg = r.data || GF.cfg; GF.msg = 'Saved.'; }
      paint();
    });
  }

    + '.w158row .amt .m{font-weight:400;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.35;}.w158row{cursor:pointer;}.w158row:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:-2px;}'

    + '.w152stats.w158due{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;}.w158due .w152st{flex:1 1 auto;min-width:max-content;}.w158due .w152st b{white-space:nowrap;}'
    + '.w158ronote{font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;background:rgba(220,165,39,.12);border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin:12px 0 10px;}'

    + '@media print{body *{visibility:hidden !important;}#w152sheet .w158rc,#w152sheet .w158rc *{visibility:visible !important;}#w152sheet .w158rc{position:fixed;left:0;top:0;width:100%;border:none;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css158);

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158new]'))) { e.preventDefault(); GF.formContact = el.getAttribute('data-w158new') === '1' ? null : Number(el.getAttribute('data-w158new')); giftForm(null, GF.formContact); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158open]'))) { if (t.closest('a')) return; e.preventDefault(); GF.formContact = null; giftForm(el.getAttribute('data-w158open')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158save]'))) { e.preventDefault(); saveGift(el); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158del]'))) { e.preventDefault(); if (el.getAttribute('data-sure') === '1') delGift(el.getAttribute('data-w158del')); else { el.setAttribute('data-sure', '1'); el.innerHTML = I('trash') + ' Delete it for good?'; } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158sample]'))) { e.preventDefault(); sampleReceipt(el.getAttribute('data-w158sample')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158print]'))) { e.preventDefault(); try { window.print(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158cfgsave]'))) { e.preventDefault(); saveRcfg(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { var t = e.target; if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && t && t.hasAttribute && t.hasAttribute('data-w158open')) { e.preventDefault(); GF.formContact = null; giftForm(t.getAttribute('data-w158open')); } });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target, box = t && t.closest && t.closest('[data-w158gift]'); if (box) refreshRule(box); });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var box = t.closest('[data-w158gift]'); if (box) { refreshRule(box); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w158year')) { GF.year = t.value; paint(); }
  });
  /* ======================= Official receipts (wave158b, 28/09/26) =======================
     The machinery for CRA receipts from the Hub, built while receipts stay off. In Receipts: whether receipts from the Hub are on,
     what is still missing (the settings and a signature image), the signature (admins upload an image; the Hub keeps it as a PNG
     in the private bucket), the switch for admins (on needs everything; the treasurer and Steel decide), Issue for a donor's due
     gifts of the tax year (one receipt: one gift, or several as the year-end run does), the year-end run (one receipt per donor),
     and the receipts issued, each with its PDF, Replace and Cancel (with the reason). A sample receipt downloads as a PDF drawn by
     receipt-pdf, marked SAMPLE. Issuing and replacing refuse in the database while receipt_mode is off.
     wave185 (30/09/26): receipts by email. Email it beside an issued receipt sends the donor one email that thanks them and carries
     the PDF (mail-send, action receipt; a test copy to a test address while the Hub's email is in test mode), and the receipt
     says when it was emailed. The switch for online gifts (admins, only while receipts are on): every paid gift through the
     donation page gets its receipt issued and emailed by itself (stripe-events). Both switches stay off until an admin decides. */
  var RB = { busy: '', msg: '', err: '', act: null, confirmOn: false, run: null, sigUrl: null, sigAt: 0, row: null };
  LOADERS.rcpts = function () { return db.from('crm_receipts').select('id,serial,contact_id,tax_year,kind,status,gift_ids,amount,advantage_amount,eligible_amount,donor,issued_at,issued_by,replaces_id,replaced_by_id,cancelled_at,cancel_reason,pdf_path,cancelled_pdf_path,emailed_at,email_send_id').order('issued_at', { ascending: false }).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(3000); };
  function rbMail(body) {
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.functions) return Promise.resolve({ error: 'offline' });
    return Promise.resolve(db.functions.invoke('mail-send', { body: body })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) {
        var ctx = r.error.context;
        if (ctx && typeof ctx.json === 'function') return ctx.json().then(function (j) { return { error: (j && j.error) || r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' }; }, function () { return { error: r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' }; });
        return { error: r.error.message || 'The email function refused.' };
      }
      return (r && r.data) || {};
    }).catch(function (e) { return { error: String((e && e.message) || e) }; });
  }
  function rbAuto() { return !!(GF.cfg && GF.cfg.receipt_auto_online === 'on'); }
  function rbFn(body) {
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.functions) return Promise.resolve({ error: 'offline' });
    return Promise.resolve(db.functions.invoke('receipt-pdf', { body: body })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) {
        var ctx = r.error.context;
        if (ctx && typeof ctx.json === 'function') return ctx.json().then(function (j) { return { error: (j && j.error) || r.error.message || 'The receipt function refused.' }; }, function () { return { error: r.error.message || 'The receipt function refused.' }; });
        return { error: r.error.message || 'The receipt function refused.' };
      }
      return (r && r.data) || {};
    }).catch(function (e) { return { error: String((e && e.message) || e) }; });
  }
  function rbOn() { return !!(GF.cfg && GF.cfg.receipt_mode === 'on'); }
  /* wave283: issuing, replacing, cancelling and drawing follow can_issue (team leads, admins and finance: wa_can gifts_edit); is_admin only while the
     database is older than wave283. Email it too (final review M5: mail-send v7 sends a receipt for anyone with gifts_edit). The settings, the mode, the signature and the automatic switch stay with admins (their functions check admin). */
  function rbAdmin() { var c = GF.cfg || {}; return !!(c.can_issue === undefined ? c.is_admin : c.can_issue); }
  function rbSave(bytes, name, type) {
    try {
      var blob = bytes instanceof Blob ? bytes : new Blob([bytes], { type: type || 'application/pdf' }), url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = name; a.rel = 'noopener'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
      setTimeout(function () { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 30000);
      return true;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function rbB64(b64) { var s = atob(b64), u = new Uint8Array(s.length); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u[i] = s.charCodeAt(i); return u; }
  function rbRefresh() { S.at.rcpts = 0; S.at.gifts = 0; S.at.grcpt = 0; return Promise.all([load('rcpts', true), load('gifts', true), load('grcpt', true), loadRcfg()]).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); }); }
  function rbSay(msg, err) { RB.msg = err ? '' : msg; RB.err = err ? msg : ''; RB.busy = ''; if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); }

  /* ---------- the sample as a PDF ---------- */
  function rbSamplePdf(btn) {
    var m = /^rcpt:(\d+)$/.exec(S.sheet || ''); if (!m) return;
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Drawing the PDF\u2026'; }
    rbFn({ action: 'sample', gift_id: Number(m[1]) }).then(function (r) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = I('note') + ' Download the sample (PDF)'; }
      var box = document.querySelector('#w152sheet [data-w158bsay]');
      if (r.error || !r.pdf_b64) { if (box) box.textContent = 'The PDF did not come back: ' + (r.error || 'no file'); return; }
      rbSave(rbB64(r.pdf_b64), 'sample-receipt-gift-' + m[1] + '.pdf');
      if (box) box.textContent = 'Downloaded: sample-receipt-gift-' + m[1] + '.pdf (marked SAMPLE).';
    });
  }

  /* ---------- in Receipts: the switch, the signature ---------- */
  function rbStatusHtml(cfg) {
    var on = cfg.receipt_mode === 'on', admin = !!cfg.is_admin, missing = (cfg.missing || []).slice();
    if (!cfg.signature) missing.push('a signature image');
    var sig = '<div class="w158bsig">' + (cfg.signature ? (RB.sigUrl ? '<img src="' + E(RB.sigUrl) + '" alt="The signature on receipts">' : '<span class="m">A signature image is uploaded.</span>') : '<span class="m">No signature image yet.</span>') +
      (admin ? '<label class="w152btn sm ghost w158bup">' + I('edit') + (cfg.signature ? ' Replace the signature' : ' Upload the signature') + '<input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg" data-w158bsig hidden></label>' : '') + '</div>';
    return '<div class="w158bmode ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off') + '"><b>Receipts from the Hub are ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off') + '.</b> ' +
      (on ? 'Team leads, admins and finance issue, replace and cancel them below; every receipt takes the next number.'
        : missing.length ? 'Before an admin can turn them on: ' + E(listJoin(missing)) + '.' : 'Everything is ready: an admin can turn them on once the treasurer and Steel agree.') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w155lb">The signature on receipts <span class="m">the treasurer\u2019s, or another person the board authorizes; a PNG or JPG on a white or clear background</span></div>' + sig +
      (admin ? (RB.confirmOn ? '<div class="w155cf"><b>Turn receipts from the Hub on?</b> From then on team leads, admins and finance can issue official receipts with the next number (' + E((cfg.receipt_prefix || '') + String(cfg.receipt_next_number || '').padStart(4, '0')) + '), and Keela stops being the only place receipts come from. The treasurer and Steel decide this.' +
          '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w158bmode="on">' + I('check') + ' Turn them on</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158bmodeno="1">Not yet</button></div></div>'
        : '<div class="w154acts">' + (on ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158bmode="off">' + I('lock') + ' Turn receipts off</button>' : '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w158bask="1"' + (missing.length ? ' disabled title="' + E('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) + '"' : '') + '>' + I('check') + ' Turn receipts on\u2026</button>') + '</div>') : '') +
      rbAutoHtml(cfg);
  }
  /* wave185: the switch for online gifts */
  function rbAutoHtml(cfg) {
    var on = cfg.receipt_mode === 'on', auto = cfg.receipt_auto_online === 'on', admin = !!cfg.is_admin;
    return '<div class="w185auto ' + (auto && on ? 'on' : 'off') + '" data-w185auto-state="' + (auto && on ? 'on' : 'off') + '"><b>Online gifts: ' + (auto && on ? 'each paid gift through the donation page gets its receipt issued and emailed by itself.' : 'receipts are issued by hand (Issue below, then Email it).') + '</b> ' +
      (on ? 'With this on, every gift the donation page takes is receipted with the next number the moment Stripe confirms it, and the donor gets one email: the thank-you with the official receipt attached as a PDF, in the language they gave in. A gift that cannot be receipted by itself (no address, a number already used) stays in the list below for an admin.'
        : 'Receipts from the Hub must be on first.') +
      (admin ? '<div class="w154acts">' + (on ? (auto ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w185auto="off">' + I('lock') + ' Stop the automatic receipts</button>' : '<button class="w152btn" data-w185auto="on">' + I('send') + ' Email receipts for online gifts automatically</button>') : '') +
        '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w185sample="en"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('note') + ' Email me a sample</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w185sample="fr"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('note') + ' En fran\u00e7ais</button></div>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  }
  function rbAutoSet(mode) {
    RB.busy = 'auto';
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_auto_set', { p_on: mode })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) { rbSay('Not changed: ' + (r.error.message || r.error), true); return; }
      GF.cfg = r.data || GF.cfg; rbSay(mode === 'on' ? 'Online gifts now get their receipt by email automatically.' : 'Online gifts are receipted by hand again.');
    });
  }
  /* wave185: the receipt email with a SAMPLE receipt attached, to the admin's own test address */
  function rbSample(lang) {
    RB.busy = 'sample'; paint();
    rbMail({ action: 'receipt_sample', lang: lang }).then(function (r) {
      if (r.error || r.status !== 'sent') { rbSay('The sample was not sent: ' + (r.error || r.status || 'no answer'), true); return; }
      rbSay('A sample receipt email went to ' + r.to + ' (the attached receipt is marked SAMPLE).');
    });
  }
  /* wave185: Email it */
  function rbEmail(id) {
    RB.busy = 'mail:' + id; RB.row = null; paint();
    rbMail({ action: 'receipt', receipt_id: Number(id) }).then(function (r) {
      /* wave283: a do-not-contact donor: the receipt is kept, not emailed, and the row says so */
      if (/do not contact/i.test(String(r.basis || r.error || ''))) { RB.row = { id: String(id), msg: 'Receipt not emailed: do not contact' }; RB.busy = ''; if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); return; }
      if (r.error) { rbSay('Not emailed: ' + r.error, true); return; }
      if (r.status === 'suppressed') { rbSay('Not emailed: ' + (r.basis || 'the address does not work') + '. Download the PDF and send it another way.', true); return; }
      if (r.status !== 'sent') { rbSay('Not emailed: ' + (r.error || r.status || 'no answer'), true); return; }
      rbSay(r.test_copy ? 'The Hub\u2019s email is in test mode: a copy went to ' + r.to + ' instead of ' + (r.donor_email || 'the donor') + '. The receipt is not marked as emailed.' : 'Emailed to ' + r.to + ' with the PDF attached.');
      rbRefresh();
    });
  }
  function rbSigLoad() {
    if (!GF.cfg || !GF.cfg.signature || RB.sigUrl || Date.now() - RB.sigAt < 60000 || !db.storage) return;
    RB.sigAt = Date.now();
    Promise.resolve(db.storage.from('receipts').createSignedUrl('settings/signature.png', 600)).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.data && r.data.signedUrl) { RB.sigUrl = r.data.signedUrl; if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts' && GF.view === 'receipts') paint(); }
    }, function () { /* fine */ });
  }
  function rbSigUpload(input) {
    var f = input.files && input.files[0]; if (!f) return;
    if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg)$/.test(f.type)) { rbSay('A PNG or JPG, please.', true); return; }
    RB.busy = 'sig'; RB.msg = 'Uploading the signature\u2026'; paint();
    var img = new Image(), url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
    img.onload = function () {
      /* kept as a PNG no wider than 900 px, so the PDF can use it */
      var sc = Math.min(1, 900 / img.naturalWidth), c = document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalWidth * sc)); c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.naturalHeight * sc));
      c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
      try { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      c.toBlob(function (blob) {
        if (!blob) { rbSay('That image could not be read.', true); return; }
        Promise.resolve(db.storage.from('receipts').upload('settings/signature.png', blob, { upsert: true, contentType: 'image/png' })).then(function (r) {
          if (r && r.error) { rbSay('Not uploaded: ' + (r.error.message || r.error), true); return; }
          RB.sigUrl = null; RB.sigAt = 0;
          try { db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'receipts', entity_id: 'settings', field: 'signature', old_value: null, new_value: 'uploaded', changed_by: actor() }); } catch (e) { /* the upload is what counts */ }
          loadRcfg().then(function () { rbSay('The signature is saved.'); });
        });
      }, 'image/png');
    };
    img.onerror = function () { rbSay('That image could not be read.', true); };
    img.src = url;
  }
  function rbModeSet(mode) {
    RB.busy = 'mode'; RB.confirmOn = false;
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_mode_set', { p_mode: mode })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) { rbSay('Not changed: ' + (r.error.message || r.error), true); return; }
      GF.cfg = r.data || GF.cfg; rbSay(mode === 'on' ? 'Receipts from the Hub are on.' : 'Receipts from the Hub are off.');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- issuing ---------- */
  function rbIssue(giftIds, quiet) {
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_receipt_issue', { p_gift_ids: giftIds.map(Number) })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) return { error: r.error.message || String(r.error) };
      var id = r && r.data; if (id && typeof id === 'object') id = id.crm_receipt_issue != null ? id.crm_receipt_issue : id.id;
      return rbFn({ action: 'render', receipt_id: Number(id) }).then(function (p) { return { id: id, pdf: p && !p.error, pdfErr: p && p.error }; });
    });
  }
  function rbIssueDonor(cid, year) {
    var gifts = (S.grcpt || []).filter(function (r) { return String(r.contact_id) === String(cid) && String(r.tax_year) === String(year) && r.receipt_status === 'due'; }).map(function (r) { return r.gift_id; });
    if (!gifts.length) return;
    RB.busy = 'issue:' + cid; paint();
    rbIssue(gifts).then(function (x) {
      if (x.error) { rbSay('Not issued: ' + x.error, true); return; }
      rbSay('Receipt issued' + (x.pdf ? ' and its PDF kept.' : '; the PDF did not come back (' + (x.pdfErr || '?') + '): Make the PDF below.'));
      rbRefresh();
    });
  }
  function rbYearRun(year) {
    var by = {};
    (S.grcpt || []).forEach(function (r) { if (String(r.tax_year) === String(year) && r.receipt_status === 'due') (by[r.contact_id] = by[r.contact_id] || []).push(r.gift_id); });
    var ids = Object.keys(by), done = 0, bad = [];
    if (!ids.length) return;
    RB.busy = 'run'; RB.run = { total: ids.length, done: 0 }; paint();
    var step = function (i) {
      if (i >= ids.length) { RB.run = null; rbSay(plural(done, 'receipt', 'receipts') + ' issued for ' + year + (bad.length ? '; not issued: ' + bad.join('; ') : '.'), !!bad.length && !done); rbRefresh(); return; }
      rbIssue(by[ids[i]]).then(function (x) {
        if (x.error) { var c = contact(ids[i]); bad.push((c ? c.full_name : 'contact ' + ids[i]) + ' (' + x.error + ')'); } else done++;
        RB.run.done = i + 1; if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint();
        step(i + 1);
      });
    };
    step(0);
  }
  function rbDueActions(d, year) {
    if (!rbAdmin() || !rbOn()) return '';
    return '<button class="w152mini" data-w158bissue="' + E(d.id) + '" data-year="' + E(year) + '"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (RB.busy === 'issue:' + d.id ? 'Issuing\u2026' : 'Issue') + '</button>';
  }
  function rbRunHtml(n, year) {
    if (!n) return '';
    if (RB.run) return '<div class="w154msg">Issuing ' + RB.run.done + ' of ' + RB.run.total + '\u2026</div>';
    if (!rbOn()) return '<p class="m">' + I('lock') + ' Issuing waits until receipts from the Hub are on.</p>';
    if (!rbAdmin()) return '<p class="m">Team leads, admins and finance issue the receipts.</p>';
    return '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w158brun="' + E(year) + '"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('send') + ' Issue one receipt per donor (' + n + ')</button></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the receipts issued ---------- */
  function rbPdf(id, which) {
    var r = (S.rcpts || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!r) return;
    var path = which === 'cancelled' ? r.cancelled_pdf_path : r.pdf_path;
    if (!path || !db.storage) return;
    Promise.resolve(db.storage.from('receipts').createSignedUrl(path, 120)).then(function (s) {
      if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) { try { window.open(s.data.signedUrl, '_blank', 'noopener'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      else rbSay('The PDF could not be opened' + (s && s.error ? ': ' + (s.error.message || s.error) : '.'), true);
    });
  }
  function rbMake(id) {
    RB.busy = 'pdf:' + id; paint();
    rbFn({ action: 'render', receipt_id: Number(id) }).then(function (p) {
      if (p.error) { rbSay('The PDF was not made: ' + p.error, true); return; }
      rbSay('The PDF is kept.'); rbRefresh();
    });
  }
  function rbAct(id, kind) {
    var box = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w158breason="' + id + '"]'), why = box ? String(box.value || '').trim() : '';
    if (why.length < 3) { rbSay('Say why, in a few words.', true); return; }
    RB.busy = kind + ':' + id; paint();
    var q = kind === 'replace' ? db.rpc('crm_receipt_replace', { p_id: Number(id), p_reason: why }) : db.rpc('crm_receipt_cancel', { p_id: Number(id), p_reason: why });
    Promise.resolve(q).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) { rbSay((kind === 'replace' ? 'Not replaced: ' : 'Not cancelled: ') + (r.error.message || r.error), true); return; }
      RB.act = null;
      var newId = kind === 'replace' ? (r && r.data && typeof r.data === 'object' ? r.data.crm_receipt_replace : r && r.data) : null;
      /* the kept copy marked cancelled, and the replacement's own PDF */
      rbFn({ action: 'render', receipt_id: Number(id) }).then(function () {
        return newId ? rbFn({ action: 'render', receipt_id: Number(newId) }) : null;
      }).then(function () {
        rbSay(kind === 'replace' ? 'Replaced: the original is kept, marked cancelled.' : 'Cancelled: the receipt is kept, marked cancelled, and its gifts follow the receipt rule again.');
        rbRefresh();
      });
    });
  }
  function rbIssuedHtml() {
    var list = S.rcpts || [], admin = rbAdmin(), mailer = rbAdmin(), on = rbOn();   /* final review M5: Email it follows can_issue */
    var bySerial = {}; list.forEach(function (r) { bySerial[r.id] = r.serial; });
    var rows = list.slice(0, 200).map(function (r) {
      var c = contact(r.contact_id), who = c ? '<a href="#crm/c/' + E(c.id) + '">' + E((r.donor && r.donor.name) || c.full_name) + '</a>' : E((r.donor && r.donor.name) || 'Contact ' + r.contact_id);
      var st = r.status === 'cancelled' ? (r.replaced_by_id ? 'Replaced by ' + (bySerial[r.replaced_by_id] || 'a new receipt') : 'Cancelled') : 'Issued';
      var acts = [];
      if (r.pdf_path) acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w158bpdf="' + E(r.id) + '">' + I('note') + ' PDF</button>');
      else if (admin) acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w158bmake="' + E(r.id) + '"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Make the PDF</button>');
      if (r.cancelled_pdf_path) acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w158bpdf="' + E(r.id) + '" data-which="cancelled">Cancelled copy</button>');
      if (admin && r.status === 'issued') {
        if (mailer) acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w185mail="' + E(r.id) + '"' + (RB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (RB.busy === 'mail:' + r.id ? 'Emailing\u2026' : I('send') + (r.emailed_at ? ' Email it again' : ' Email it')) + '</button>');
        if (on) acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w158bdo="replace" data-id="' + E(r.id) + '">Replace</button>');
        acts.push('<button class="w152mini" data-w158bdo="cancel" data-id="' + E(r.id) + '">Cancel</button>');
      }
      var mailed = r.status === 'issued' ? (r.emailed_at ? '<span class="w185mailed on" title="' + E('Emailed to the donor on ' + fmtDay(r.emailed_at)) + '">' + I('send') + ' Emailed ' + E(fmtDay(r.emailed_at)) + '</span>' : '<span class="w185mailed">Not emailed</span>') : '';
      var said = RB.row && RB.row.id === String(r.id) ? '<div class="w158bwhy w283said" role="status">' + E(RB.row.msg) + '</div>' : '';
      var form = RB.act && String(RB.act.id) === String(r.id) ? '<div class="w158bwhy"><input class="w152in" data-w158breason="' + E(r.id) + '" placeholder="' + (RB.act.kind === 'replace' ? 'Why it is replaced (the address changed, a typo\u2026)' : 'Why it is cancelled (issued in error\u2026)') + '">' +
        '<button class="w152btn sm" data-w158bgo="' + E(RB.act.kind) + '" data-id="' + E(r.id) + '">' + (RB.act.kind === 'replace' ? 'Replace it' : 'Cancel it') + '</button><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w158bstop="1">Keep it</button></div>' : '';
      return '<div class="w158brow' + (r.status === 'cancelled' ? ' off' : '') + '"><span class="s"><b>' + E(r.serial) + '</b><span class="m">' + E(fmtDay(r.issued_at)) + (r.kind === 'annual' ? ' \u00b7 ' + plural((r.gift_ids || []).length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ' in ' + E(r.tax_year) : '') + '</span></span>' +
        '<span class="w">' + who + '</span><span class="a">' + E(cents(r.eligible_amount)) + '</span><span class="t">' + gchip({ issued: ['Issued', GREEN], cancelled: [st, MUTED] }, r.status) + (r.cancel_reason ? '<span class="m"> ' + E(String(r.cancel_reason).replace(/^Replaced:\s*/, '')) + '</span>' : '') + mailed + '</span>' +
        '<span class="x">' + acts.join('') + '</span>' + said + form + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w154card wide"><h3>' + I('note') + ' Receipts issued by the Hub <span class="m">' + plural(list.length, 'receipt', 'receipts') + '</span></h3>' +
      (list.length ? '<div class="w158brows">' + rows + '</div>' : '<p class="m">None yet. Keela\u2019s 55 receipts are the legal copies until receipts from the Hub are on; then each one issued here is listed with its PDF.</p>') + '</div>';
  }
