    + '.w185mailed{display:inline-block;margin-left:8px;font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';}.w185mailed.on{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css158b);

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    if (!t.closest('.w152root') && !t.closest('#w152sheet')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bpdfsample]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbSamplePdf(el); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bask]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RB.confirmOn = true; RB.msg = ''; RB.err = ''; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bmodeno]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RB.confirmOn = false; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bmode]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbModeSet(el.getAttribute('data-w158bmode')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w185auto]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbAutoSet(el.getAttribute('data-w185auto')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w185mail]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbEmail(el.getAttribute('data-w185mail')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w185sample]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbSample(el.getAttribute('data-w185sample')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bissue]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbIssueDonor(el.getAttribute('data-w158bissue'), el.getAttribute('data-year')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158brun]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbYearRun(el.getAttribute('data-w158brun')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bpdf]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbPdf(el.getAttribute('data-w158bpdf'), el.getAttribute('data-which')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bmake]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbMake(el.getAttribute('data-w158bmake')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bdo]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RB.act = { id: el.getAttribute('data-id'), kind: el.getAttribute('data-w158bdo') }; RB.msg = ''; RB.err = ''; paint(); var inp = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w158breason]'); if (inp) inp.focus(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bstop]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RB.act = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w158bgo]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rbAct(el.getAttribute('data-id'), el.getAttribute('data-w158bgo')); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.hasAttribute && t.hasAttribute('data-w158bsig')) rbSigUpload(t); });
  /* ======================= Reports (wave160, 28/09/26) =======================
     The CRM's Reports tab (#crm/reports, or #crm/reports/<the year a fiscal year starts>): one fiscal year (1 October to
     30 September) from crm_report(), which runs with the reader's rights. The year in a few lines for the board (copy it or
     print the page), the headline numbers against the year before (at the same point while the year is still running), gifts
     by month, kind, campaign, source and size, donors (new, retained, recaptured, lapsed, the retention rate; who gave last
     year and not yet this year, made into a fixed list in one click; the largest donors), volunteering (servings by month, new
     and returning volunteers, retention, roles, the most servings, the application funnel, the hours and meals where they were
     recorded), email permission today, and the emails and form answers of the year. Read-only apart from Make a list. */
  P.chart = '<path d="M4 4v16h16"/><path d="M8.5 16v-4.5M12.5 16V8M16.5 16v-6"/>';
  P.print = '<path d="M7 9V4h10v5"/><rect x="4" y="9" width="16" height="7.5" rx="2"/><path d="M7 14h10v6H7z"/>';
  P.refresh = '<path d="M19.5 12a7.5 7.5 0 1 1-2.2-5.3M19.5 4.5v4h-4"/>';
  var RP = { fy: null, data: {}, busy: false, err: '', msg: '', showLapsed: false, listing: false };
  function rpGet() { return RP.data[RP.fy == null ? 'cur' : String(RP.fy)] || null; }
  function rpLabel(y) { y = Number(y); return y + '-' + String(y + 1).slice(2); }
  function rpPct(a, b) { a = Number(a) || 0; b = Number(b) || 0; return b ? Math.round(a * 100 / b) + '%' : '\u2013'; }
  function rpCap(s) { s = String(s || ''); return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1); }
  function rpNum(n) { return (Number(n) || 0).toLocaleString('en-CA'); }
  function rpMonth(m) { return MON[Number(String(m).slice(5, 7)) - 1] || ''; }
  function rpLoad(fy, force) {
    var key = fy == null ? 'cur' : String(fy);
    if (RP.data[key] && !force) return Promise.resolve(RP.data[key]);
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.rpc) return Promise.resolve(null);
    RP.busy = true; RP.err = '';
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_report', { p_fy: fy == null ? null : Number(fy) })).then(function (r) {
      RP.busy = false;
      if (r && r.error) { RP.err = String(r.error.message || r.error); return null; }
      var d = r && r.data; if (typeof d === 'string') { try { d = JSON.parse(d); } catch (e) { d = null; } }
      if (!d || d.fy == null) { RP.err = 'The report did not come back.'; return null; }
      RP.data[String(d.fy)] = d; if (fy == null) RP.data.cur = d;
      return d;
    }, function (e) { RP.busy = false; RP.err = String((e && e.message) || e); return null; });
  }
  function openReports(parts) {
    var y = parts[2] && /^\d{4}$/.test(parts[2]) ? Number(parts[2]) : null;
    if (y !== RP.fy) { RP.showLapsed = false; RP.msg = ''; }
    RP.fy = y; RP.err = '';
  }

  /* ---------- the year in a few lines (for the board) ---------- */
  function rpSpan(d) { return d.label + ' (' + fmtDay(d.from) + ' to ' + fmtDay(d.in_progress ? d.through : d.to) + ')'; }
  function rpSummary(d) {
    var g = d.gifts || {}, dn = d.donors || {}, v = d.volunteers || {}, out = [];
    var pg = (d.in_progress ? g.prev_to_date : g.prev) || {}, pv = (d.in_progress ? v.prev_to_date : v.prev) || {};
    var pwhen = d.in_progress ? 'at the same point in ' + d.prev_label : 'in ' + d.prev_label;
    if (g.count) out.push('In ' + rpSpan(d) + ', ' + plural(g.donors, 'person', 'people') + ' gave ' + money(g.total) + ' in ' + plural(g.count, 'gift', 'gifts') + ' (' + pwhen + ': ' + money(pg.total || 0) + ' from ' + plural(pg.donors || 0, 'person', 'people') + ').');
    else out.push('No gift is recorded in ' + rpSpan(d) + (pg.count ? ' (' + pwhen + ': ' + money(pg.total) + ')' : '') + '.');
    var bits = [];
    if (dn.new) bits.push(plural(dn.new, 'person', 'people') + ' gave for the first time');
    if (dn.last_year) bits.push(rpNum(dn.retained) + ' of the ' + plural(dn.last_year, 'donor', 'donors') + ' of ' + d.prev_label + ' gave again (' + rpPct(dn.retained, dn.last_year) + ' retention)');
    if (dn.recaptured) bits.push(plural(dn.recaptured, 'person', 'people') + ' came back after a year or more without giving');
    if (dn.lapsed) bits.push(rpNum(dn.lapsed) + ' who gave in ' + d.prev_label + ' have not given ' + (d.in_progress ? 'yet ' : '') + 'in ' + d.label);
    if (bits.length) out.push(rpCap(listJoin(bits)) + '.');
    if (v.servings) {
      var vb = [plural(v.people, 'volunteer', 'volunteers') + ' served ' + plural(v.servings, 'time', 'times') + ' at ' + plural(v.events, 'event', 'events') + ' (' + pwhen + ': ' + plural(pv.people || 0, 'volunteer', 'volunteers') + ', ' + plural(pv.servings || 0, 'serving', 'servings') + ').'];
      var nb = [];
      if (v.new) nb.push(plural(v.new, 'person', 'people') + ' served for the first time');
      if (v.prev && v.prev.people) nb.push(rpNum(v.retained) + ' of the ' + plural(v.prev.people, 'volunteer', 'volunteers') + ' of ' + d.prev_label + ' came back (' + rpPct(v.retained, v.prev.people) + ')');
      if (nb.length) vb.push(rpCap(listJoin(nb)) + '.');
      if (v.hours && Number(v.hours.recorded) > 0) vb.push('They gave ' + plural(Math.round(Number(v.hours.recorded)), 'hour', 'hours') + ' where hours were recorded.');
      if (v.meals && Number(v.meals.served) > 0) vb.push(plural(v.meals.served, 'meal was', 'meals were') + ' served at the events that recorded it.');
      out.push(vb.join(' '));
    } else out.push('No serving is recorded in ' + rpSpan(d) + '.');
    var ap = v.applied || {};
    if (ap.applied) out.push(plural(ap.applied, 'person', 'people') + ' applied to volunteer, and ' + rpNum(ap.served1) + ' of them ' + (ap.served1 === 1 ? 'has' : 'have') + ' served since.');
    var em = d.email || {};
    if (em.sent) out.push('The Hub sent ' + plural(em.sent, 'email', 'emails') + (em.campaigns ? ' (' + plural(em.campaigns, 'campaign', 'campaigns') + ')' : '') + '; ' + rpNum(em.opened) + ' were opened.');
    var fa = (d.forms || []).reduce(function (a, x) { return a + (Number(x.answers) || 0); }, 0);
    if (fa) out.push('The CRM\u2019s forms were answered ' + plural(fa, 'time', 'times') + '.');
    return out;
  }

  /* ---------- email permission today (the CRM's rule, from the contacts) ---------- */
  function rpPerm() {
    var o = { reach: 0, express: 0, implied: 0, ending: 0, unsub: 0, bounced: 0, none: 0, noemail: 0, total: 0 };
    (S.contacts || []).forEach(function (c) {
      o.total++;
      if (!c.emails.length) { o.noemail++; return; }
      var p = c._perm;
      /* task 10b: Steel's rule of 5 Nov 2026: everyone with a working address who has not unsubscribed and is not do not contact can get a newsletter */
      if (p !== 'unsub' && p !== 'bounced' && !c.do_not_contact) o.reach++;
      if (p === 'express' || p === 'implied') o[p]++;
      else if (p === 'unsub') o.unsub++;
      else if (p === 'bounced') o.bounced++;
      else o.none++;
      if (p === 'implied') { var n = daysSince(c.implied_consent_until); if (n != null && n <= 0 && n >= -90) o.ending++; }
    });
    return o;
  }

  /* ---------- pieces ---------- */
  function rpBars(rows, cur, prev, fmt, label, labels) {
    var max = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { max = Math.max(max, Number(r[cur]) || 0, prev ? Number(r[prev]) || 0 : 0); });
    var h = function (v) { return max && v ? Math.max(3, Math.round(v * 100 / max)) : 0; };
    var aria = label + ': ' + rows.map(function (r) { return rpMonth(r.month) + ' ' + fmt(Number(r[cur]) || 0); }).join(', ');
    return '<div class="w160ch" role="img" aria-label="' + E(aria) + '">' + rows.map(function (r) {
      var a = Number(r[cur]) || 0, b = prev ? Number(r[prev]) || 0 : 0, mo = rpMonth(r.month);
      return '<div class="w160col" title="' + E(mo + ' ' + String(r.month).slice(0, 4) + ': ' + fmt(a) + (prev ? ' (the year before: ' + fmt(b) + ')' : '')) + '"><div class="w160bars">' +
        (prev ? '<i class="p" style="height:' + h(b) + '%"></i>' : '') + '<i class="c" style="height:' + h(a) + '%"></i></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div><div class="w160ml" aria-hidden="true">' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<span>' + E(rpMonth(r.month)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (labels ? '<div class="w160lg"><span><i class="c"></i>' + E(labels[0]) + '</span>' + (prev ? '<span><i class="p"></i>' + E(labels[1]) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '');
  }
  /* rows: [label html, value text, share 0..1, extra text] */
  function rpRows(list, empty) {
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="m">' + E(empty || 'Nothing to show.') + '</p>';
    return '<div class="w160rows">' + list.map(function (x) {
      return '<div class="w160r"><span class="k">' + x[0] + (x[3] ? '<span class="m"> \u00b7 ' + E(x[3]) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="bar" aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:' + Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x[2] || 0)) * 100) + '%"></i></span><span class="v">' + E(x[1]) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function rpWho(id, name) {
    var c = id != null ? contact(id) : null, n = (c && c.full_name) || name || 'Someone not in the CRM';
    return c ? '<a href="#crm/c/' + E(c.id) + '">' + E(n) + '</a>' : E(n);
  }
  function rpBox(n, label) { return '<div><b>' + E(n) + '</b><span>' + E(label) + '</span></div>'; }
  function rpMethod(m) { var x = GMETH.filter(function (o) { return o[0] === m; })[0]; return x && m ? x[1] : ''; }

  function rpGiftsHtml(d) {
    var g = d.gifts || {}, months = g.by_month || [], tot = Number(g.total) || 0;
    var best = months.reduce(function (a, r) { return Number(r.total) > Number(a.total || 0) ? r : a; }, {});
    var kinds = (g.by_kind || []).map(function (k) { var nm = (GKIND[k.kind] || [rpCap(k.kind)])[0]; return [E(nm), cents(k.total), tot ? k.total / tot : 0, plural(k.count, 'gift', 'gifts')]; });
    var camps = (g.by_campaign || []).slice(0, 10).map(function (k) { return [E(k.campaign || 'No campaign'), cents(k.total), tot ? k.total / tot : 0, plural(k.count, 'gift', 'gifts') + ', ' + plural(k.donors, 'donor', 'donors')]; });
    var srcs = (g.by_source || []).map(function (k) { var m = rpMethod(k.method); return [E(platName(k.platform) + (m ? ' (' + m + ')' : '')), cents(k.total), tot ? k.total / tot : 0, plural(k.count, 'gift', 'gifts')]; });
    var rc = g.receipts || {}, rcl = Object.keys(RSTS).filter(function (k) { return rc[k]; }).map(function (k) { return plural(rc[k], 'gift', 'gifts') + ' ' + RSTS[k][0].toLowerCase(); });
    var allKeela = (g.by_source || []).length && (g.by_source || []).every(function (k) { return k.platform === 'keela'; });
    return '<section class="w154card wide"><h3>' + I('heart') + ' Gifts by month <span class="m">' + E(money(tot)) + ' in ' + E(d.label) + (g.prev ? ', ' + E(money(g.prev.total)) + ' in ' + E(d.prev_label) : '') + '</span></h3>' +
      rpBars(months, 'total', 'prev_total', money, 'Gifts by month in ' + d.label, [d.label, d.prev_label]) +
      (best.month && Number(best.total) > 0 ? '<p class="w160note">The best month: ' + E(rpMonth(best.month) + ' ' + best.month.slice(0, 4)) + ' (' + E(money(best.total)) + ', ' + E(plural(best.count, 'gift', 'gifts')) + ').</p>' : '') + '</section>' +
      '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('db') + ' Where it came from</h3>' +
      '<div class="w160h4">What kind of money</div>' + rpRows(kinds, 'No gift this year.') +
      '<div class="w160h4">Campaigns</div>' + rpRows(camps, 'No gift this year.') +
      '<div class="w160h4">How it came</div>' + rpRows(srcs, 'No gift this year.') +
      (rcl.length ? '<p class="w160note">Receipts: ' + E(listJoin(rcl)) + '. <a href="#crm/gifts/receipts">Receipts</a></p>' : '') +
      (allKeela ? '<p class="w160note">Keela\u2019s gifts came in as donations: registrations and tickets among them count as donations until someone sets their kind in Gifts.</p>' : '') + '</section>';
  }
  function rpSizesHtml(d) {
    var g = d.gifts || {}, dn = d.donors || {}, bands = g.bands || [], most = 0;
    bands.forEach(function (b) { most = Math.max(most, Number(b.count) || 0); });
    var rows = bands.map(function (b) { return [E(b.band), plural(b.count, 'gift', 'gifts'), most ? b.count / most : 0, Number(b.total) ? cents(b.total) : '']; });
    var top = (dn.top || []).map(function (x, i) { return [rpWho(x.contact_id, x.name), cents(x.total), dn.top[0] && Number(dn.top[0].total) ? x.total / dn.top[0].total : 0, plural(x.count, 'gift', 'gifts')]; });
    return '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('spark') + ' Gift sizes <span class="m">' + (g.count ? 'average ' + E(money(g.average)) + ', middle ' + E(money(g.median)) + ', largest ' + E(money(g.largest)) : '') + '</span></h3>' +
      rpRows(rows, 'No gift this year.') + '<div class="w160h4">The largest donors of ' + E(d.label) + '</div>' + rpRows(top, 'No donor this year.') + '</section>';
  }
  function rpDonorsHtml(d) {
    var dn = d.donors || {}, lap = dn.lapsed_list || [], shown = RP.showLapsed ? lap : lap.slice(0, 8);
    var rows = shown.map(function (x) { return [rpWho(x.contact_id, x.name), cents(x.prev_total), 0, 'last gave ' + fmtDay(x.last_day) + ', ' + plural(x.gifts_ever, 'gift', 'gifts') + ' in all']; });
    return '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('people') + ' Donors <span class="m">' + E(plural(dn.this_year || 0, 'donor', 'donors')) + ' in ' + E(d.label) + '</span></h3>' +
      '<div class="w160ret">' + rpBox(rpNum(dn.new), 'gave for the first time') + rpBox(rpNum(dn.retained), 'gave again after ' + d.prev_label) +
      rpBox(rpNum(dn.recaptured), 'came back after a gap') + rpBox(rpNum(dn.lapsed), 'gave in ' + d.prev_label + ', not ' + (d.in_progress ? 'yet ' : '') + 'in ' + d.label) + '</div>' +
      '<p>Donor retention: <b>' + E(rpPct(dn.retained, dn.last_year)) + '</b> of the ' + E(plural(dn.last_year || 0, 'donor', 'donors')) + ' of ' + E(d.prev_label) + ' gave again in ' + E(d.label) + (d.in_progress ? ' so far' : '') + '.</p>' +
      (lap.length ? '<div class="w160h4">Gave in ' + E(d.prev_label) + ', not ' + (d.in_progress ? 'yet ' : '') + 'in ' + E(d.label) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w160rows w160lap">' + rows.map(function (x) { return '<div class="w160r"><span class="k">' + x[0] + '<span class="m"> \u00b7 ' + E(x[3]) + '</span></span><span></span><span class="v">' + E(x[1]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w154acts w160noprint">' + (lap.length > shown.length ? '<button class="w152mini" data-w160lapsed="1">Show all ' + lap.length + '</button>' : '') +
        '<button class="w152btn sm" data-w160list="1"' + (RP.listing ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('list') + ' Make a list of them</button></div>' : '') +
      (RP.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(RP.msg) + (/saved/.test(RP.msg) ? ' <a href="#crm/lists">Lists</a>' : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</section>';
  }
  function rpVolHtml(d) {
    var v = d.volunteers || {}, months = v.by_month || [], pv = v.prev || {};
    var roles = v.by_role || [], most = roles.reduce(function (a, r) { return Math.max(a, Number(r.servings) || 0); }, 0);
    var rrows = roles.map(function (r) { return [E(roleName(r.role)), plural(r.servings, 'serving', 'servings'), most ? r.servings / most : 0, plural(r.people, 'person', 'people')]; });
    var top = (v.top || []).map(function (x) { return [rpWho(x.contact_id, x.name), plural(x.servings, 'serving', 'servings'), v.top[0] && v.top[0].servings ? x.servings / v.top[0].servings : 0, '']; });
    var ap = v.applied || {}, steps = [['Applied in ' + d.label, ap.applied], ['Served at least once since', ap.served1], ['Twice or more', ap.served2], ['Three times or more', ap.served3]];
    var funnel = ap.applied ? rpRows(steps.map(function (s) { return [E(s[0]), rpNum(s[1]), ap.applied ? (Number(s[1]) || 0) / ap.applied : 0, '']; })) : '<p class="m">No application is dated in ' + E(d.label) + '.</p>';
    var hrs = v.hours || {}, meals = v.meals || {};
    var notes = [];
    if (Number(hrs.recorded) > 0) notes.push(plural(Math.round(Number(hrs.recorded)), 'hour', 'hours') + ' recorded for ' + plural(hrs.servings_with_hours, 'serving', 'servings') + ' (the old website).');
    else notes.push('Hours are not recorded for servings in the Hub' + (hrs.last_recorded ? '; the old website recorded them until ' + fmtDay(hrs.last_recorded) : '') + '.');
    if (Number(meals.served) > 0) notes.push(plural(meals.served, 'meal', 'meals') + ' served at ' + plural(meals.events, 'event', 'events') + ' that recorded it.');
    else notes.push('Meals served are not recorded' + (meals.last_recorded ? ' since ' + fmtDay(meals.last_recorded) + ' (the old website)' : '') + '.');
    return '<section class="w154card wide"><h3>' + I('hand') + ' Volunteering by month <span class="m">' + E(plural(v.servings || 0, 'serving', 'servings')) + ' in ' + E(d.label) + ', ' + E(plural(pv.servings || 0, 'serving', 'servings')) + ' in ' + E(d.prev_label) + '</span></h3>' +
      rpBars(months, 'servings', 'prev_servings', function (n) { return plural(n, 'serving', 'servings'); }, 'Servings by month in ' + d.label, [d.label, d.prev_label]) + '</section>' +
      '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('people') + ' Volunteers <span class="m">' + E(plural(v.people || 0, 'person', 'people')) + ' at ' + E(plural(v.events || 0, 'event', 'events')) + '</span></h3>' +
      '<div class="w160ret">' + rpBox(rpNum(v.people), 'served in ' + d.label) + rpBox(rpNum(v.new), 'for the first time') +
      rpBox(rpNum(v.retained), 'of ' + rpNum(pv.people) + ' from ' + d.prev_label + ' came back') + rpBox(rpPct(v.retained, pv.people), 'volunteer retention') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w160h4">Roles</div>' + rpRows(rrows, 'No serving this year.') +
      '<div class="w160h4">The most servings</div>' + rpRows(top, 'No serving this year.') + '</section>' +
      '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('arrow') + ' From applying to serving</h3>' + funnel +
      '<p class="w160note">From the volunteer record: the date of someone\u2019s latest application, and the servings on or after it.</p>' +
      '<div class="w160h4">Hours and meals</div><p class="w160note">' + E(notes.join(' ')) + '</p></section>';
  }
  function rpMailHtml(d) {
    var p = rpPerm(), em = d.email || {}, forms = d.forms || [];
    var frows = forms.map(function (x) { return [E(x.title || x.slug || 'A form'), plural(x.answers, 'answer', 'answers'), 0, '']; });
    return '<section class="w154card"><h3>' + I('mail') + ' Email <span class="m">permission today</span></h3>' +
      '<div class="w160ret">' + rpBox(rpNum(p.reach), 'can get a newsletter') + rpBox(rpNum(p.express), 'said yes (express)') + rpBox(rpNum(p.implied), 'implied: gave or served') + rpBox(rpNum(p.ending), 'implied ends within 90 days') + '</div>' +
      '<p class="w160note">' + E(plural(p.unsub, 'person has', 'people have') + ' unsubscribed, ' + plural(p.bounced, 'address bounces', 'addresses bounce') + ', ' + plural(p.none, 'person has', 'people have') + ' no consent on file (the rule still emails them), and ' + plural(p.noemail, 'contact has', 'contacts have') + ' no email address.') + '</p>' +
      '<div class="w160h4">Sent in ' + E(d.label) + '</div>' +
      (em.sent ? rpRows([['Emails sent', rpNum(em.sent), 1, ''], ['Delivered', rpNum(em.delivered), em.delivered / em.sent, ''], ['Opened', rpNum(em.opened), em.opened / em.sent, rpPct(em.opened, em.sent)],
        ['Clicked', rpNum(em.clicked), em.clicked / em.sent, rpPct(em.clicked, em.sent)], ['Unsubscribed', rpNum(em.unsubscribed), em.unsubscribed / em.sent, '']]) +
        '<p class="w160note">' + E(plural(em.from_campaigns, 'email', 'emails') + ' from ' + plural(em.campaigns, 'campaign', 'campaigns') + ', ' + plural(em.from_automations, 'email', 'emails') + ' from automations.') + '</p>'
        : '<p class="m">No email has gone out from the Hub in ' + E(d.label) + ' (test emails are left out).</p>') +
      '<div class="w160h4">Form answers in ' + E(d.label) + '</div>' + rpRows(frows, 'No form answer yet (the CRM\u2019s forms are new in wave156).') + '</section>';
  }
  function rpNotesHtml(d) {
    return '<section class="w154card wide"><h3>' + I('note') + ' How it is counted</h3><p class="w160note">' + E([
      'A fiscal year runs from 1 October to 30 September; ' + d.label + ' began on ' + fmtDay(d.from) + '.',
      d.in_progress ? 'While a year is running, it is compared with the year before at the same date (' + fmtDay(((d.gifts || {}).prev_to_date || {}).through) + ').' : '',
      'A gift counts on the day it was received. A serving is someone confirmed on the roster of a completed event in the Volunteer Manager, the old website\u2019s events included.',
      'A first-time donor gave for the first time in the year; a retained donor gave in both years; a donor who came back after a gap last gave before the year before.',
      'Someone on two volunteer records counts twice until the records are merged in Possible duplicates.',
      'The numbers follow what the CRM holds today: a gift or a roster added later changes them.'].filter(Boolean).join(' ')) + '</p></section>';
  }

  function paintReports(root) {
    var head = statsHtml(S.contacts || []) + tabsHtml();
    var d = rpGet();
    if (!d) {
      if (RP.err) { root.innerHTML = head + '<div class="w152empty">' + I('alert') + ' The report could not be made (' + E(RP.err) + '). <button class="w152mini" data-w160reload="1">Try again</button>' + (RP.fy != null ? ' <button class="w152mini" data-w152go="#crm/reports">This year</button>' : '') + '</div>'; return; }
      root.innerHTML = head + busy('Working out the report\u2026');
      if (!RP.busy) rpLoad(RP.fy).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'reports') paint(); });
      return;
    }
    var g = d.gifts || {}, dn = d.donors || {}, v = d.volunteers || {};
    var pg = (d.in_progress ? g.prev_to_date : g.prev) || {}, pv = (d.in_progress ? v.prev_to_date : v.prev) || {};
    var pwhen = d.in_progress ? d.prev_label + ' at this point' : d.prev_label;
    var years = (d.years || [d.fy]).map(Number);
    var sum = rpSummary(d);
    root.innerHTML = head +
      '<div class="w160rep" data-w160fyshown="' + E(d.fy) + '">' +
      '<div class="w152bar w160top w160noprint"><select class="w152sel" data-w160fy aria-label="Fiscal year">' + years.map(function (y) {
        return '<option value="' + y + '"' + (y === Number(d.fy) ? ' selected' : '') + '>Fiscal year ' + E(rpLabel(y)) + (y === years[0] ? ' (this year)' : '') + '</option>';
      }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<button class="w152btn" data-w160copy="1">' + I('copy') + ' Copy the summary</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w160print="1">' + I('print') + ' Print</button>' +
      '<button class="w152mini" data-w160reload="1" title="Work the numbers out again">' + I('refresh') + ' Refresh</button>' +
      '<span class="w160asof">Figures as of ' + E(fmtDay(d.today)) + (d.at ? ', ' + E(new Date(d.at).toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' })) : '') + '</span></div>' +
      '<h2 class="w160title">Fiscal year ' + E(d.label) + (d.in_progress ? ' <span>so far, to ' + E(fmtDay(d.through)) + '</span>' : '') + '</h2>' +
      '<div class="w152stats w160stats">' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + E(money(g.total || 0)) + '</b><span>raised in ' + E(plural(g.count || 0, 'gift', 'gifts')) + ' (' + E(pwhen) + ': ' + E(money(pg.total || 0)) + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + rpNum(dn.this_year) + '</b><span>donors, ' + rpNum(dn.new) + ' new (' + E(pwhen) + ': ' + rpNum(pg.donors) + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + rpNum(v.people) + '</b><span>volunteers served, ' + rpNum(v.new) + ' for the first time (' + E(pwhen) + ': ' + rpNum(pv.people) + ')</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w152st"><b>' + rpNum(v.servings) + '</b><span>servings at ' + E(plural(v.events || 0, 'event', 'events')) + ' (' + E(pwhen) + ': ' + rpNum(pv.servings) + ')</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w154grid w160grid">' +
      '<section class="w154card wide w160sum"><h3>' + I('note') + ' The year in a few lines <span class="m">ready for the board</span></h3>' + sum.map(function (s) { return '<p>' + E(s) + '</p>'; }).join('') + '</section>' +
      rpGiftsHtml(d) + rpSizesHtml(d) + rpDonorsHtml(d) + rpMailHtml(d) + rpVolHtml(d) + rpNotesHtml(d) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function rpMakeList() {
    var d = rpGet(); if (!d || RP.listing) return;
    var ids = ((d.donors || {}).lapsed_list || []).map(function (x) { return Number(x.contact_id); }).filter(function (x) { return x && contact(x); });
    if (!ids.length) { RP.msg = 'Nobody to put on a list.'; paint(); return; }
    var name = 'Gave in ' + d.prev_label + ', not ' + (d.in_progress ? 'yet ' : '') + 'in ' + d.label;
    var row = { name: name, description: 'Donors who gave in ' + d.prev_label + ' and had not given in ' + d.label + ' on ' + fmtDay(d.today) + ' (made in Reports).', kind: 'static',
      filter: { groups: [] }, member_ids: ids, created_by: actor(), updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    RP.listing = true; RP.msg = ''; paint();
    Promise.resolve(db.from('crm_lists').insert(row).select().single()).then(function (r) {
      RP.listing = false;
      if (r && r.error) RP.msg = 'Not saved: ' + (r.error.message || r.error);
      else { RP.msg = '\u201c' + name + '\u201d saved as a fixed list of ' + plural(ids.length, 'person', 'people') + '.'; S.at.lists = 0; }
      if (S.ui.tab === 'reports') paint();
    }, function (e) { RP.listing = false; RP.msg = 'Not saved: ' + ((e && e.message) || e); if (S.ui.tab === 'reports') paint(); });
  }

    + '.w160ret b{display:block;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:28px;font-weight:600;color:var(--wa-green,' + GREEN + ');line-height:1.05;}.w160ret span{display:block;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.3;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');}'

    + '.w160r .k{min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}.w160r .k a{color:inherit;font-weight:600;}.w160r .bar{height:8px;border-radius:4px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);overflow:hidden;}.w160r .bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + TEAL + ';border-radius:4px;}'
    + '.w160r .v{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;white-space:nowrap;font-weight:600;}.w160lap .w160r{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 0 auto;}'
    + '.w160note{font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin:10px 0 0;}.w160note a{color:inherit;font-weight:600;}'
    + '@media (max-width:700px){.w160ret{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}.w160ch{height:120px;gap:3px;}.w160ml{gap:3px;font-size:10px;}.w160asof{margin-left:0;flex:1 1 100%;}.w160title{font-size:24px;}.w160r{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.5fr) minmax(30px,.6fr) auto;}}'
    + '@media print{body *{visibility:hidden !important;}.w160rep,.w160rep *{visibility:visible !important;}.w160rep{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;}.w160noprint{display:none !important;}.w160rep .w154card{box-shadow:none;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);break-inside:avoid;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css160);

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w160rep, .w152root')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w160copy]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var d = rpGet(); if (d) copy('Fiscal year ' + d.label + (d.in_progress ? ', to ' + fmtDay(d.through) : '') + '\n\n' + rpSummary(d).join('\n\n')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w160print]'))) { e.preventDefault(); try { window.print(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w160reload]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RP.err = ''; RP.msg = ''; rpLoad(RP.fy, true).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'reports') paint(); }); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w160lapsed]'))) { e.preventDefault(); RP.showLapsed = true; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w160list]'))) { e.preventDefault(); rpMakeList(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.hasAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-w160fy')) return;
    var y = Number(t.value), d = rpGet(), cur = d && d.years && d.years.length ? Number(d.years[0]) : null;
    go(y === cur ? '#crm/reports' : '#crm/reports/' + y);
  });
  /* ======================= Leaving Keela (wave159a, 28/09/26) =======================
     The first part of step 7 of the plan (wave159, leaving Keela): a live checklist at the top of Sources. Each thing Keela does
     today, what the Hub has in its place, whose step is next, and the date that matters: Keela bills quarterly (the next charge in
     app_config keela_next_charge) and cancelling needs keela_notice_days of notice, so every step aims to be done a week before
     that. Read from the Hub: the contacts and gifts from Keela, the forms and automations (drafts, live, on), the email mode, the
     receipt mode and settings. The final export and import, and the donation form, are Steel's steps and read as such. */
  LOADERS.kcfg = function () { return db.from('app_config').select('key,value').in('key', ['mail_mode', 'receipt_mode', 'receipt_signatory_name', 'receipt_next_number', 'keela_next_charge', 'keela_notice_days', 'keela_quarterly_cost', 'keela_donation_form', 'donate_mode', 'donate_webhook']); };
  function kcfg(k) { var r = (S.kcfg || []).filter(function (x) { return x.key === k; })[0]; return r ? String(r.value == null ? '' : r.value) : ''; }
  function kDays(day) { var n = daysSince(day); return n == null ? null : -n; }
  function kChip(state) {
    var d = { done: ['Done', GREEN], doing: ['In progress', TEAL], waiting: ['Waiting on a decision', '#B9851B'], todo: ['To do', FLAG] }[state] || ['?', MUTED];
    return '<span class="w154chip" style="background:' + d[1] + '17;color:' + d[1] + '"><i style="background:' + d[1] + '"></i>' + E(d[0]) + '</span>';
  }
  function keelaSteps() {
    var contacts = (S.contacts || []).filter(function (c) { return c.source === 'keela' || (c.legacy_refs && c.legacy_refs.keela_contact_id); }).length;
    var kg = (S.gifts || []).filter(function (g) { return lower(g.platform) === 'keela'; });
    var lastKg = kg.map(function (g) { return dayOf(g.donated_at); }).sort().pop() || '';
    var forms = S.forms || [], live = forms.filter(function (f) { return f.status === 'published'; });   /* wave283: a form that is out is published */
    var autos = S.autos || [], on = autos.filter(function (a) { return a.status === 'on'; });
    var paste = autos.filter(function (a) { return /\[Paste/i.test(JSON.stringify(a.steps || [])); });
    var mail = kcfg('mail_mode') || 'test', rmode = kcfg('receipt_mode') || 'off';
    var rMissing = []; if (!kcfg('receipt_signatory_name')) rMissing.push('who signs'); if (!/^\d+$/.test(kcfg('receipt_next_number'))) rMissing.push('the numbering');
    var donation = kcfg('keela_donation_form'), page = kcfg('donate_mode') === 'live', built = !!kcfg('donate_webhook');
    return [
      { t: 'Contacts and gifts', k: 'Keela holds the donors, supporters and gifts.', s: S.gifts ? 'doing' : 'doing',
        h: plural(contacts, 'contact', 'contacts') + ' and ' + plural(kg.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ' from Keela are in the Hub' + (lastKg ? ' (the latest gift from ' + fmtDay(lastKg) + ')' : '') + '.',
        n: 'Last: a final export from Keela (contacts with their subscription status and custom fields, the transactions with the receipts, the form answers that prove consent), imported and checked against Keela\u2019s counts. Steel downloads; the assistant imports.' },
      { t: 'Forms', k: 'Keela\u2019s volunteer, Toronto and newsletter forms on the website.', s: forms.length && live.length === forms.length ? 'done' : live.length ? 'doing' : 'todo',
        h: forms.length ? plural(live.length, 'form is', 'forms are') + ' live of ' + forms.length + ' in the Hub' + (live.length < forms.length ? ' (the others are drafts)' : '') + '.' : 'No form in the Hub yet.',
        n: live.length === forms.length && forms.length ? 'Close the matching Keela forms.' : 'Steel reads the drafts and puts them live in Forms, then the website\u2019s buttons point to hub.welfareavenue.com/f/ and the Keela forms close.' },
      { t: 'Automations', k: 'Keela\u2019s welcome series, donor follow-up and rally follow-up.', s: autos.length && on.length === autos.length ? 'done' : on.length ? 'doing' : 'todo',
        h: plural(on.length, 'automation is', 'automations are') + ' on of ' + autos.length + (paste.length ? '; ' + plural(paste.length, 'draft still needs', 'drafts still need') + ' the words of Keela\u2019s emails' : '') + '.',
        n: paste.length ? 'Paste the words of Keela\u2019s welcome emails into the drafts (or have the assistant copy them from Keela), try each on yourself, then turn them on once the forms are live.' : 'Try each on yourself, then turn them on once the forms are live.' },
      { t: 'Email', k: 'Keela sends the newsletters and campaigns.', s: mail === 'live' ? 'done' : 'todo',
        h: 'The Hub\u2019s email is in ' + (mail === 'live' ? 'live mode.' : 'test mode: only the test addresses get email.'),
        n: mail === 'live' ? 'Send the next newsletter from Sends.' : 'Steel switches the mode to live in Email when real sends should start (Resend\u2019s free plan sends up to 100 a day).' },
      { t: 'Tax receipts', k: 'Keela issued 55 receipts (KEE numbers).', s: rmode === 'on' ? 'done' : rMissing.length ? 'waiting' : 'todo',
        h: 'Receipts from the Hub are ' + (rmode === 'on' ? 'on.' : 'off' + (rMissing.length ? ' (missing: ' + listJoin(rMissing) + ').' : ', and ready to turn on.')),
        n: rmode === 'on' ? 'Issue from Receipts; the year-end run in January.' : 'The treasurer reviews a sample PDF and fills the settings with the signature; the treasurer and Steel decide to turn them on.' },
      { t: 'Online donations', k: 'Keela\u2019s donation form on the website.', s: page || donation ? 'done' : built ? 'doing' : 'waiting',
        h: page ? 'The Hub\u2019s donation page is live (hub.welfareavenue.com/give, paid on Stripe).' : donation ? 'Donations go through ' + donation + '.' :
          built ? 'The Hub\u2019s donation page is built (Stripe: once or monthly, where it goes, tributes) and off.' : 'Not decided: Zeffy, if its embedded forms can be fully customized, or another option.',
        n: page || donation ? 'Point the website\u2019s Donate buttons at the new page and take Keela\u2019s form off the website.' :
          built ? 'Steel reads the page and puts it live in Gifts, Online giving; then the website\u2019s Donate buttons point to hub.welfareavenue.com/give.' : 'Steel\u2019s decision (Zeffy); until then Keela\u2019s form stays on the website.' }
    ];
  }
  function keelaHtml() {
    var steps = keelaSteps(), done = steps.filter(function (x) { return x.s === 'done'; }).length;
    var charge = kcfg('keela_next_charge'), notice = Number(kcfg('keela_notice_days')) || 7, cost = kcfg('keela_quarterly_cost');
    var by = charge ? (function () { var t = Date.parse(charge + 'T12:00:00Z') - notice * 86400000; return new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10); })() : '';
    var aim = by ? (function () { var t = Date.parse(by + 'T12:00:00Z') - 7 * 86400000; return new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10); })() : '';
    var left = by ? kDays(by) : null;
    return '<div class="w152card w159k"><h3>Leaving Keela <span>' + done + ' of ' + steps.length + ' done</span></h3>' +
      (charge ? '<p class="w159kd"><b>' + (left != null && left >= 0 ? plural(left, 'day', 'days') + ' to give notice' : 'The notice date has passed') + '</b> Keela\u2019s next charge is on ' + E(fmtDay(charge)) + (cost ? ' ($' + E(cost) + ' a quarter)' : '') +
        '; cancelling needs ' + plural(notice, 'day', 'days') + '\u2019 notice, so by ' + E(fmtDay(by)) + '. The aim is every step below done by ' + E(fmtDay(aim)) + '.</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w159ks">' + steps.map(function (x) {
        return '<div class="w159kr"><div class="h"><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b>' + kChip(x.s) + '</div><p class="m">' + E(x.k) + '</p><p>' + E(x.h) + '</p><p class="nx">' + I('arrow') + ' ' + E(x.n) + '</p></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  var css159 = document.createElement('style'); css159.id = 'w159css';
  css159.textContent = ''
    + '.w159k{grid-column:1/-1;}.w159kd{font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;margin:0 0 12px;}.w159kd b{color:' + GREEN + ';margin-right:6px;}'
    + '.w159ks{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:12px;}.w159kr{background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;min-width:0;}'
    + '.w159kr .h{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px;margin-bottom:4px;}.w159kr p{margin:4px 0;font-size:13px;line-height:1.45;}.w159kr .m{color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '.w159kr .nx{color:' + INK + ';font-weight:500;}.w159kr .nx .w152i{width:13px;height:13px;vertical-align:-2px;color:' + TEAL + ';}'
    + '@media (max-width:980px){.w159ks{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}}@media (max-width:700px){.w159ks{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css159);

  /* ======================= Online giving (wave161, 28/09/26) =======================
     The CRM side of the Hub's donation page, hub.welfareavenue.com/give (paid on Welfare Avenue's Stripe account), at
     #crm/gifts/online (the Online giving button in Gifts):
     - The page: live or off, a link to see it (a preview that takes no gift while it is off), and what it says and offers
       (the title and words, the amounts for once and monthly, the smallest and largest gift, where gifts can go, the card-fee
       option, the news box's words, the thank-you, receipt and monthly notes). Admins save them (donate_* settings, which
       the database refuses to anyone else). Put it live asks first and goes through crm_donate_mode_set (admins, logged,
       only once Stripe is set up); Take it down stops new gifts at once.
     - This fiscal year online: the gifts, their total, what Stripe kept in fees, the monthly gifts going, and (admins) how
       many checkouts were started.
     - Monthly gifts: each with its donor, amount, state (active, payment failed, ending, stopped), where it goes, since when,
       the last and next payment, and what it has given so far, with a link to it in Stripe.
     - For admins, what Stripe told the Hub lately (crm_stripe_events): each event, what the Hub did, and any error. */
  P.card = '<rect x="3" y="5.5" width="18" height="13" rx="2.5"/><path d="M3 10h18M7 15h4"/>';
  var DG = { confirm: false, busy: '', msg: '', err: '', draft: {} }; /* draft: what an admin typed and has not saved, kept across repaints */
  var GIVE_URL = 'https://hub.welfareavenue.com/give';
  var DSET = [ /* [setting, label, hint, kind] */
    ['donate_title', 'Title', '', 'text'],
    ['donate_intro', 'The words under the title', '', 'area'],
    ['donate_amounts_once', 'Amounts to give once', 'dollars, separated by commas', 'text'],
    ['donate_amounts_monthly', 'Amounts to give monthly', 'dollars, separated by commas', 'text'],
    ['donate_min', 'The smallest gift ($)', '', 'num'],
    ['donate_max', 'The largest gift online ($)', 'above it, donors are asked to write to you', 'num'],
    ['donate_designations', 'Where gifts can go', 'one per line; the first is chosen unless the donor picks another; with one line donors are not asked', 'area'],
    ['donate_fee_cover', 'Offer to cover the card fee', '', 'onoff'],
    ['donate_consent_label', 'The news box', 'the words the donor ticks to get your emails: their express consent', 'area'],
    ['donate_thanks', 'The thank-you', 'shown after the payment', 'area'],
    ['donate_receipt_note', 'The receipt note', '{charity} becomes the charity number', 'area'],
    ['donate_monthly_note', 'The note for monthly donors', '{email} becomes your reply-to address', 'area']
  ];
  LOADERS.dcfg = function () { return db.from('app_config').select('key,value').in('key', DSET.map(function (d) { return d[0]; }).concat(['donate_mode', 'donate_product', 'donate_webhook', 'donate_fee_percent', 'donate_fee_fixed'])); };
  LOADERS.plans = function () { return db.from('crm_gift_plans').select('id,external_id,contact_id,amount,currency,status,designation,tribute_kind,tribute_name,started_at,next_payment_at,last_paid_at,last_failed_at,ends_at,cancelled_at,cancel_reason').order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(2000); };
  LOADERS.sevents = function () { return amAdmin() ? db.from('crm_stripe_events').select('id,type,status,received_at,attempts,result,error').order('received_at', { ascending: false }).limit(25) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }); };
  LOADERS.dtries = function () { return amAdmin() ? db.from('crm_donate_attempts').select('id,created_at,status,frequency,amount_cents').gte('created_at', dgFyStart() + 'T04:00:00Z').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3000) : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }); };
  function dgFyStart() { var y = Number(fyOf(today())); return (y - 1) + '-10-01'; }
  function dget(k) { var r = (S.dcfg || []).filter(function (x) { return x.key === k; })[0]; return r ? String(r.value == null ? '' : r.value) : ''; }
  function dgChip(d) { return '<span class="w154chip" style="background:' + d[1] + '17;color:' + d[1] + '"><i style="background:' + d[1] + '"></i>' + E(d[0]) + '</span>'; }
  var PLAN_ST = { active: ['Active', GREEN], past_due: ['Payment failed', FLAG], paused: ['Paused', '#B9851B'], incomplete: ['Not started', MUTED], cancelled: ['Stopped', MUTED] };
  function planState(p) { return p.status === 'active' && p.ends_at ? ['Ending ' + fmtDay(p.ends_at), '#B9851B'] : PLAN_ST[p.status] || [p.status || '?', MUTED]; }
  function whyStopped(s) {
    var m = { cancellation_requested: 'they asked', payment_failed: 'payments failed', payment_disputed: 'a disputed payment', too_expensive: 'too expensive', unused: 'not using it',
      switched_service: 'gave elsewhere', low_quality: 'not happy', customer_service: 'service', too_complex: 'too complicated', missing_features: 'missing something', other: 'other' };
    return String(s || '').split(/:\s*/).filter(Boolean).map(function (x) { return m[x] || x.replace(/_/g, ' '); }).join(': ');
  }
  function stripeLink(g) {
    var x = String(g.external_id || '');
    return /^pi_/.test(x) ? 'https://dashboard.stripe.com/payments/' + x : /^in_/.test(x) ? 'https://dashboard.stripe.com/invoices/' + x : '';
  }
  function dgWhen(s) {
    if (!s) return '';
    try { return fmtDay(s) + ', ' + new Date(s).toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return fmtDay(s); }
  }
  var EVT = { 'checkout.session.completed': 'A checkout finished', 'checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded': 'A bank payment cleared', 'checkout.session.async_payment_failed': 'A bank payment failed',
    'invoice.paid': 'A monthly payment', 'invoice.payment_failed': 'A monthly payment failed', 'charge.refunded': 'A refund', 'customer.subscription.created': 'A monthly gift started',
    'customer.subscription.updated': 'A monthly gift changed', 'customer.subscription.deleted': 'A monthly gift stopped' };

  function onlineHtml() {
    var live = dget('donate_mode') === 'live', admin = amAdmin(), ready = !!dget('donate_product') && !!dget('donate_webhook');
    var sum = function (a, k) { return a.reduce(function (t, g) { return t + (Number(g[k]) || 0); }, 0); };
    var gifts = (S.gifts || []).filter(function (g) { return lower(g.platform) === 'stripe' && !(Number(g.refunded_amount) > 0 && !(Number(g.amount) > 0)); }); /* a gift refunded in full counts nowhere */
    var fy = fyOf(today()), cur = gifts.filter(function (g) { return fyOf(g.donated_at) === fy; });
    var plans = S.plans || [], going = plans.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'active' || p.status === 'past_due'; });
    var tries = (S.dtries || []).filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'session'; });
    var st = function (b, s) { return '<div class="w152st"><b>' + b + '</b><span>' + s + '</span></div>'; };

    var page = '<div class="w154card w161card"><h3>' + I('heart') + ' The donation page ' + dgChip(live ? ['Live', GREEN] : ['Off', MUTED]) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="w161p">' + (live
        ? 'Live at <a href="' + GIVE_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">hub.welfareavenue.com/give</a>. Donors give once or monthly on Stripe\u2019s secure page, and each gift lands in Gifts on its own: the donor, where it goes, a tribute, the fee, the news box if they ticked it, and any refund.'
        : 'Off: the page tells visitors that online giving opens soon. <a href="' + GIVE_URL + '?preview=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener">See it as donors will</a> (a preview that takes no gift).') + '</p>' +
      '<ul class="w161ready"><li class="' + (ready ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + I(ready ? 'check' : 'alert') + '<span>' + (ready ? 'Welfare Avenue\u2019s Stripe account is connected: the gift donors see and the Hub\u2019s webhook are set up.' : 'Stripe is not set up yet (the build does it).') + '</span></li>' +
      '<li class="' + (live ? 'ok' : 'todo') + '">' + I(live ? 'check' : 'arrow') + '<span>' + (live ? 'The website\u2019s Donate buttons should point here; then Keela\u2019s donation form can close.' : 'Once it is live: point the website\u2019s Donate buttons at hub.welfareavenue.com/give, then close Keela\u2019s donation form.') + '</span></li></ul>' +
      (admin ? (DG.confirm
          ? '<div class="w155cf"><b>Put the donation page live?</b> From then on anyone with the link can give, paid into Welfare Avenue\u2019s Stripe account, and each gift is recorded here. Monthly gifts carry on until the donor or the team stops them.' +
            '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w161mode="live"' + (DG.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('check') + ' Put it live</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w161no="1">Not yet</button></div></div>'
          : '<div class="w154acts">' + (live ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w161mode="off"' + (DG.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('x') + ' Take it down</button>'
              : '<button class="w152btn" data-w161ask="1"' + (ready ? '' : ' disabled title="Stripe is not set up yet"') + '>' + I('check') + ' Put it live\u2026</button>') + '</div>')
        : '<p class="m">An admin puts the page live or takes it down.</p>') +
      (DG.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(DG.msg) + '</div>' : '') + (DG.err ? '<div class="w154msg err" role="alert">' + E(DG.err) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';

    var stats = '<div class="w152stats w161stats">' +
      st(E(money(sum(cur, 'amount'))), 'given online this fiscal year (' + plural(cur.length, 'gift', 'gifts') + ')') +
      st(E(cents(sum(cur, 'fee'))), 'kept by Stripe in fees') +
      st(E(money(going.reduce(function (t, p) { return t + (Number(p.amount) || 0); }, 0))), 'a month from ' + plural(going.length, 'monthly gift', 'monthly gifts')) +
      (admin ? st(tries.length.toLocaleString('en-CA'), 'checkouts started this year') : '') + '</div>';

    var planRow = function (p) {
      var c = contact(p.contact_id), nm = c ? c.full_name : p.contact_id ? 'Contact ' + p.contact_id : 'Donor not known yet';
      var paid = (S.gifts || []).filter(function (g) { return String(g.plan_id) === String(p.id); });
      var note = p.status === 'cancelled' ? 'Stopped ' + fmtDay(p.cancelled_at) + (p.cancel_reason ? ' (' + whyStopped(p.cancel_reason) + ')' : '')
        : p.status === 'past_due' && p.last_failed_at ? 'Failed ' + fmtDay(p.last_failed_at) + '; Stripe tries again' : p.next_payment_at ? 'Next ' + fmtDay(p.next_payment_at) : '';
      return '<div class="w152row w161prow" role="listitem">' +
        '<span>' + (c ? '<a href="#crm/c/' + E(c.id) + '">' + E(nm) + '</a>' : E(nm)) + (c && c.emails[0] ? '<div class="m">' + E(c.emails[0]) + '</div>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="amt">' + E(cents(p.amount)) + '<small> a month</small>' + (p.tribute_kind ? '<div class="m">' + E((p.tribute_kind === 'memory' ? 'In memory of ' : 'In honour of ') + (p.tribute_name || '')) + '</div>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span>' + dgChip(planState(p)) + (note ? '<div class="m">' + E(note) + '</div>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span>' + E(p.designation || '\u2013') + '<div class="m">Since ' + E(fmtDay(p.started_at)) + '</div></span>' +
        '<span>' + E(cents(sum(paid, 'amount'))) + '<div class="m">' + plural(paid.length, 'payment', 'payments') + (p.last_paid_at ? ', the last ' + E(fmtDay(p.last_paid_at)) : '') + '</div></span>' +
        '<span><a class="w152mini" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/subscriptions/' + E(p.external_id) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' Stripe</a></span></div>';
    };
    var monthly = '<div class="w154card w161plans"><h3>' + I('refresh') + ' Monthly gifts <span class="m">' + plural(going.length, 'going', 'going') + ' of ' + plans.length + '</span></h3>' +
      (plans.length ? '<div class="w152list" role="list"><div class="w152lh" aria-hidden="true"><span>Donor</span><span>Amount</span><span>State</span><span>Where it goes</span><span>Given so far</span><span></span></div>' + plans.map(planRow).join('') + '</div>'
        : '<p class="m">No monthly gift yet. Donors start one on the page (Give monthly); each month\u2019s payment then comes in as a gift.</p>') +
      '<p class="m w161foot">A donor who wants to change or stop a monthly gift writes to you; stop or change it in Stripe (the link on the row), and the Hub follows.</p></div>';

    var evs = S.sevents || [];
    var events = admin ? '<div class="w154card w161events"><h3>' + I('bell') + ' What Stripe told the Hub lately</h3>' +
      (evs.length ? '<div class="w161evl">' + evs.map(function (x) {
          var s = { done: ['Recorded', GREEN], ignored: ['Not the page\u2019s', MUTED], error: ['Error: Stripe sends it again', FLAG], received: ['Received', TEAL] }[x.status] || [x.status, MUTED];
          var why = x.error ? x.error : x.result && x.result.ignored ? x.result.ignored : x.result && x.result.waiting ? x.result.waiting : x.result && x.result.receipt_needs_attention ? 'Refunded after receipt ' + x.result.receipt_needs_attention + ': cancel or replace it' : '';
          return '<div class="w161ev"><span>' + E(dgWhen(x.received_at)) + '</span><span>' + E(EVT[x.type] || x.type) + (x.attempts > 1 ? ' <span class="m">(' + x.attempts + ' tries)</span>' : '') + '</span><span>' + dgChip(s) + (why ? '<div class="m">' + E(why) + '</div>' : '') + '</span></div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="m">Nothing yet. Each payment, monthly payment, refund and change to a monthly gift shows here as Stripe sends it.</p>') + '</div>' : '';

    var setv = function (d) {
      var k = d[0], v = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(DG.draft, k) ? DG.draft[k] : dget(k), dis = admin ? '' : ' disabled';
      var input = d[3] === 'area' ? '<textarea class="w152in" rows="' + (k === 'donate_designations' ? 3 : 2) + '" data-w161set="' + k + '"' + dis + '>' + E(v) + '</textarea>'
        : d[3] === 'onoff' ? '<select class="w152sel" data-w161set="' + k + '"' + dis + '><option value="on"' + (v !== 'off' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Yes, donors can add it</option><option value="off"' + (v === 'off' ? ' selected' : '') + '>No</option></select>'
        : '<input class="w152in"' + (d[3] === 'num' ? ' type="number" min="1" step="1"' : '') + ' data-w161set="' + k + '" value="' + E(v) + '"' + dis + '>';
      return '<label class="w155lb' + (d[3] === 'area' ? ' full' : '') + '">' + E(d[1]) + (d[2] ? ' <span class="m">' + E(d[2]) + '</span>' : '') + input + '</label>';
    };
    var settings = '<div class="w154card w161set"><h3>' + I('edit') + ' What the page says and offers</h3>' + (admin ? '' : '<p class="m">Only an admin changes these.</p>') +
      '<div class="w161grid">' + DSET.map(setv).join('') + '</div>' +
      (admin ? '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w161save="1"' + (DG.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('check') + ' Save</button><a class="w152btn ghost" href="' + GIVE_URL + '?preview=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' See the page</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';

    return '<div class="w161">' + page + stats + monthly + settings + events +
      '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w152go="#crm/gifts">Back to the gifts</button></div></div>';
  }

  function dgSave() {
    var rows = [];
    document.querySelectorAll('#crm [data-w161set]').forEach(function (el) { rows.push({ key: el.getAttribute('data-w161set'), value: String(el.value || '').trim() }); });
    var get = function (k) { return (rows.filter(function (r) { return r.key === k; })[0] || {}).value || ''; };
    var set = function (k, v) { rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.key === k) r.value = v; }); };
    var nums = function (s) { return String(s).split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean); };
    var bad = '';
    ['donate_amounts_once', 'donate_amounts_monthly'].forEach(function (k) {
      var n = nums(get(k));
      if (!n.length || n.length > 6 || n.some(function (x) { return !(Number(x) > 0) || Math.abs(Number(x) * 100 - Math.round(Number(x) * 100)) > 0.001; })) bad = bad || 'The amounts: up to six numbers in dollars, separated by commas.';
      else set(k, n.map(Number).join(', '));
    });
    if (!get('donate_title')) bad = 'The page needs a title.';
    else if (!(Number(get('donate_min')) >= 1)) bad = 'The smallest gift is at least $1.';
    else if (!(Number(get('donate_max')) > Number(get('donate_min')))) bad = 'The largest gift must be more than the smallest.';
    else if (!get('donate_designations').split(/\n/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).length) bad = 'Where gifts can go: at least one line.';
    else if (!get('donate_consent_label')) bad = 'The news box needs its words: they are the donor\u2019s consent.';
    if (bad) { DG.err = bad; DG.msg = ''; paint(); return; }
    DG.busy = 'save'; DG.err = ''; DG.msg = ''; paint();
    var at = new Date().toISOString();
    Promise.resolve(db.from('app_config').upsert(rows.map(function (r) { return { key: r.key, value: r.value, updated_at: at }; }), { onConflict: 'key' })).then(function (r) {
      DG.busy = '';
      if (r && r.error) { DG.err = 'Not saved: ' + String(r.error.message || r.error); paint(); return; }
      DG.msg = 'Saved. The page shows it to the next visitor.'; DG.draft = {};
      load('dcfg', true).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); });
    }, function (e) { DG.busy = ''; DG.err = 'Not saved: ' + String((e && e.message) || e); paint(); });
  }
  function dgMode(mode) {
    DG.confirm = false; DG.busy = 'mode'; DG.msg = ''; DG.err = ''; paint();
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('crm_donate_mode_set', { p_mode: mode })).then(function (r) {
      DG.busy = '';
      if (r && r.error) { DG.err = String(r.error.message || r.error); paint(); return; }
      DG.msg = mode === 'live' ? 'The donation page is live.' : 'The donation page is off: it now says online giving opens soon.';
      Promise.all([load('dcfg', true), load('kcfg', true)]).then(function () { if (S.ui.tab === 'gifts') paint(); });
    }, function (e) { DG.busy = ''; DG.err = String((e && e.message) || e); paint(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target, el; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#crm')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w161ask]'))) { e.preventDefault(); DG.confirm = true; DG.msg = ''; DG.err = ''; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w161no]'))) { e.preventDefault(); DG.confirm = false; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w161mode]'))) { e.preventDefault(); dgMode(el.getAttribute('data-w161mode')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w161save]'))) { e.preventDefault(); dgSave(); return; }
  });
  function dgKeep(e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.hasAttribute && t.hasAttribute('data-w161set')) DG.draft[t.getAttribute('data-w161set')] = t.value; }
  document.addEventListener('input', dgKeep);
  document.addEventListener('change', dgKeep);
  var css161 = document.createElement('style'); css161.id = 'w161css';
  css161.textContent = ''
    + '.w161{display:grid;gap:14px;}.w161 .w154card h3{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.w161 .w154card h3 .m{font-weight:500;font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w161p{margin:6px 0 10px;line-height:1.5;}.w161ready{list-style:none;margin:0 0 6px;padding:0;display:grid;gap:6px;}.w161ready li{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45;}'
    + '.w161ready li .w152i{width:16px;height:16px;flex:none;margin-top:2px;}.w161ready li.ok .w152i{color:' + GREEN + ';}.w161ready li.no .w152i{color:' + FLAG + ';}.w161ready li.todo .w152i{color:' + TEAL + ';}'
    + '.w161stats{margin:0;}'
    + '.w161plans .w152lh,.w161plans .w161prow{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.5fr) minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1.3fr) minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr) auto;}'
    + '.w161foot{margin:10px 0 0;}'

    + '.w161evl{display:grid;gap:0;}.w161ev{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1.3fr) minmax(0,1.5fr);gap:10px;padding:8px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w154msg.err{background:rgba(179,64,47,.1);color:#7e2b1f;}'

  document.head.appendChild(css161);
  /* ======================= wiring ======================= */
  function go(h) { if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h; }
  function inOurs(t) { return t.closest && (t.closest('.w152root') || t.closest('#w152sheet')); }
  function sheetId() { var m = /^(?:c|edit|perm|org):(\d+|tmp\d+)$/.exec(S.sheet || ''); return m ? m[1] : null; }
  function openContact(id) {
    S.confirm = null;
    if (/^#crm(\/|$)/.test(location.hash)) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#crm/c/' + id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } contactSheet(id); }
    else go('#crm/c/' + id);
  }
  function setFilter(o) { var u = S.ui; u.kind = o.kind || ''; u.role = o.role || ''; u.act = o.act || ''; u.perm = o.perm || ''; if (o.q !== undefined) u.q = o.q; u.show = 60; }
  function addTag(input) {
    var id = input.getAttribute('data-w152tagin'), c = contact(id); if (!c) return;
    var t = String(input.value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 40);
    if (!t) return;
    if (c.tags.map(lower).indexOf(t) >= 0) { input.value = ''; return; }
    saveTags(c.id, c.tags.concat([t]));
  }
  function copy(text) {
    var done = function () { toastS('Copied'); };
    try { if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, function () { fallback(); }); return; } } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    fallback();
    function fallback() { try { var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); done(); } catch (e) { toastS(text); } }
  }
  var qT = null;
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !inOurs(t)) return;
    var el, v;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152close]'))) { e.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152open]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openContact(el.getAttribute('data-w152open')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152go]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); v = el.getAttribute('data-w152go');
      if (S.sheet) closeSheet(true);
      go(v);
      /* wave176: from a contact, open that person's editor in the Volunteer Manager once its list has loaded */
      var vid = el.getAttribute('data-w176vid');
      if (vid) {
        var tries = 0, tick = function () {
          var list = (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_PEOPLE) || [];
          if (list.some(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(vid); }) && typeof wa26PersonModal === 'function') { try { wa26PersonModal(vid); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
          if (++tries < 40) setTimeout(tick, 250);
        };
        setTimeout(tick, 250);
      }
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152set]'))) {
      v = el.getAttribute('data-w152set');
      setFilter(v === 'volunteer' ? { kind: 'volunteer' } : v === 'served' ? { act: 'served' } : v === 'donor' ? { kind: 'donor' } : { q: '' });
      paint(); return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152kind]'))) { S.ui.kind = el.getAttribute('data-w152kind'); S.ui.show = 60; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152clear]'))) { e.preventDefault(); setFilter({ q: '' }); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152more]'))) { S.ui.show += 60; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152add]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.confirm = null; editForm(null); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152edit]'))) { e.preventDefault(); editForm(el.getAttribute('data-w152edit')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152back]'))) { e.preventDefault(); v = el.getAttribute('data-w152back'); S.confirm = null; if (v && contact(v)) contactSheet(v); else closeSheet(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152save]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveContact(el.getAttribute('data-w152save')).then(function () { el.disabled = false; }, function () { el.disabled = false; }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152perm-edit]'))) { e.preventDefault(); permForm(el.getAttribute('data-w152perm-edit')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152savep]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; savePerm(el.getAttribute('data-w152savep')).then(function () { el.disabled = false; }, function () { el.disabled = false; }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152linkorg]'))) { e.preventDefault(); orgForm(el.getAttribute('data-w152linkorg')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152saveorg]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveOrg(el.getAttribute('data-w152saveorg')).then(function () { el.disabled = false; }, function () { el.disabled = false; }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152unlink]'))) { e.preventDefault(); unlinkOrg(el.getAttribute('data-w152unlink')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152untag]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var cid = sheetId(), c = contact(cid); if (!c) return;
      v = el.getAttribute('data-w152untag');
      saveTags(c.id, c.tags.filter(function (x) { return x !== v; })); return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152log]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveLog(el.getAttribute('data-w152log')).then(function () { el.disabled = false; }, function () { el.disabled = false; }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152delint]'))) { e.preventDefault(); deleteLog(sheetId(), el.getAttribute('data-w152delint')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152copy]'))) { e.preventDefault(); copy(el.getAttribute('data-w152copy')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152keep]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.confirm = 'merge:' + el.getAttribute('data-w152keep') + ':' + el.getAttribute('data-drop'); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152cancel]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.confirm = null; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152merge]'))) { e.preventDefault(); merge(el.getAttribute('data-w152merge'), el.getAttribute('data-drop')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w152notdup]'))) { e.preventDefault(); notDup(el.getAttribute('data-w152notdup')); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152tagin') && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ',')) { e.preventDefault(); addTag(t); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-w152q')) return;
    var v = t.value;
    clearTimeout(qT);
    qT = setTimeout(function () { S.ui.q = v; S.ui.show = 60; paint(); }, 160);
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w152root')) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152role')) S.ui.role = t.value;
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152act')) S.ui.act = t.value;
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152perm')) S.ui.perm = t.value;
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w152sort')) S.ui.sort = t.value;
    else return;
    S.ui.show = 60; paint();
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if (!S.sheet) return;
    if (!/^#crm(\/|$)/.test(location.hash)) closeSheet(true);
  });

  }

        if (root === 'crm') { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave152 boot', e); }
      return out;
    };
  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v153 the apps: a 9-dot launcher, an app frame with its own rail, and the CRM as the first app (27/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (wave149): "the app launcher (9-dot, bottom left: Admin centre, HR centre, Finance with the
   finances pages only, then the CRM)". Steel (wave152): "remember the 9 dots for the Finances module, HR
   center module, and CRM module? ... the CRM will need space both at the top for menu and search options,
   as well as on the side for all the options like email marketing, fundraising, etc". And: "Okay go for it
   step 1 first".

     nothing is rebuilt, the rail and the bar are a frame around them.
   - Steel (27/09/26, after the first cut): "all the pages that are shown on the apps now, they should just

  var ORDER = ['hub', 'strategy', 'volunteer', 'programs', 'crm', 'fund', 'studio', 'hr', 'finance', 'admin'];   /* wave225: the Content Studio after Fundraising */   /* wave193: the Fundraising app, before HR and Finance */   /* wave274 (one app): Toronto is a city of the Volunteer Manager, no longer a tile of its own */
  /* wave274 (one app): every app that frames pages, in the launcher's order with each app's cities in its place
     (hub, strategy, volunteer, toronto, crm, ...): who owns a page, and the body class each one sets */
  var FRAMES = []; ORDER.forEach(function (id) { (APPS[id].cities || [id]).forEach(function (c) { if (APPS[c] && FRAMES.indexOf(c) < 0) FRAMES.push(c); }); });
  /* 01/10/26: Steel: "I'd like to add an app in the dashboard that links to our website home page" and "but it opens up in a
     seperate tab". A link, not an app: it frames no pages, so it sits under the apps (the launcher and the phone menu's
     tiles) for everyone who has them, and opens welfareavenue.com in a new tab while the Hub stays where it was */

  /* ---------- who can open what: the menu already knows (a hidden tab is a page this person cannot open) ---------- */
  function tab(id) { var b = document.getElementById('tabBar'); return b ? b.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]') : null; }
  function allowed(id) { var t = tab(id); return !!t && t.style.display !== 'none' && !!document.getElementById(id); }
  /* wave262 (Steel, 20/10/26: "allow them to look through the apps that are available to them"): the apps that belong to a
     person's team come first as Your apps; the other apps they can open follow under More apps. Nothing is taken away.
     Admins and the Executive profile have every app as theirs.
     wave279b (Steel, 05/11/26: "Yes, next release"): Toronto staff could already open the CRM, but their Your apps was empty;
     the CRM is now theirs.
     wave283 (CRM slice 1, Phase 3): Marketing also has the CRM, and Toronto also has the Volunteer Manager. */
  var TEAM_APPS = { marketing: ['studio', 'crm'], fundraising: ['fund', 'crm'], opsmtl: ['volunteer'], hr: ['hr'], progdev: ['strategy'], opsto: ['volunteer', 'crm'], board: [] };   /* wave274 (one app): the Toronto team's apps are the Volunteer Manager (it opens Toronto for them) and wave279b's CRM, as the CRM rebuild (wave283) also gives them */
  /* wave284: the CRM rail's Imports item, for import_admin (admins) only, as the CRM page knows it (wa_my_caps through window.w152.can) */
  function importsOk() { try { return !!(window.w152 && window.w152.can && window.w152.can('import_admin')); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function myProfile() {
    try { var r = (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || null; if (r && r.role === 'admin') return 'admin'; if (r && r.profile) return String(r.profile); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { return (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function yours() {
    var can = ORDER.filter(function (id) { return id !== 'hub' && appOk(id); });
    var p = myProfile(), mine = TEAM_APPS[p];
    if (!mine || p === 'admin' || p === 'executive' || p === 'exec') return { yours: can, more: [], all: true };
    var y = can.filter(function (id) { return mine.indexOf(id) >= 0; });
    return { yours: y, more: can.filter(function (id) { return y.indexOf(id) < 0; }), all: false };
  }
  function appOk(id) {
    if (boardMode() || !staffOk()) return false;
    var a = APPS[id]; if (!a) return false;
    if (id === 'hub') return true;
    if (a.cities) return a.cities.some(ownOk);   /* wave274 (one app): the Volunteer Manager is there when either city is */
    return ownOk(id);
  }
  /* an app's (or a city's) own pages, the launcher's rule before wave274 (one app) */
  function ownOk(id) {
    if (boardMode() || !staffOk()) return false;
    var a = APPS[id]; if (!a) return false;
    if (id === 'hub') return true;
    if (a.need) return allowed(a.need);   /* wave193: the Fundraising app needs its own page; the Events calendar alone (finance staff, wave124) opens no app */
    return a.pages.some(allowed);
  }
  /* ---------- wave274 (one app): the cities of an app ----------
     A city is offered when the person may open its page (the menu's rule: wa40Allowed and wave127's profiles decide). With two
     the rail and the phone's menu show the switch; with one there is no switch and the tile opens it. */
  var KEY_CITY = 'wa274.city';   /* the last city this person used (a convenience of this browser only) */
  function tileOf(id) { var a = APPS[id]; return a && a.of && APPS[a.of] ? a.of : id; }
  function citiesOf(id) { var t = APPS[tileOf(id)]; return t ? (t.cities || [t.id]) : []; }
  function cities(id) { return citiesOf(id).filter(ownOk); }
  /* where the tile opens: the last city used, else the profile's own city (opsto: Toronto), else the first (Montreal) */
  function cityFor(id) {
    var cs = cities(id); if (cs.length < 2) return cs[0] || id;
    var last = read(KEY_CITY); if (last && cs.indexOf(last) >= 0) return last;
    var p = myProfile(), mine = cs.filter(function (c) { return APPS[c].team && APPS[c].team === p; })[0];
    return mine || cs[0];
  }
  function items(app) {
    var a = APPS[app]; if (!a || !a.groups) return [];
    var gs = a.groups().map(function (g) { return { label: g.label, solo: !!g.solo, items: g.items.filter(function (it) { return allowed(it.page); }) }; }).filter(function (g) { return g.items.length; });
    /* wave164: an app with one group named after itself (Finance, the HR centre) shows no group heading: it only repeated the app's name */
    if (gs.length === 1 && fold(gs[0].label).replace(/s$/, '') === fold(a.label).replace(/s$/, '')) gs[0].solo = true;
    return gs;
  }
  function firstHash(app) { var gs = items(app); return gs.length ? gs[0].items[0].h : (APPS[app] && APPS[app].home) || '#mypage'; }

  /* ---------- where we are ---------- */
  function hashNow() { try { return decodeURIComponent(location.hash || ''); } catch (e) { return location.hash || ''; } }
  function pageOf(h) { return String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/')[0]; }
  /* the app that owns a page (every app page opens its app's frame; the Hub keeps the rest) */
  function ownerOf(pg) { for (var i = 0; i < FRAMES.length; i++) { var a = APPS[FRAMES[i]]; if (a.pages.indexOf(pg) >= 0) return a.id; } return null; }   /* wave274 (one app): the cities too (Toronto owns #toronto) */
  function current() {
    var pg = pageOf(hashNow());
    if (boardMode() || !staffOk()) return null;
    var id = ownerOf(pg);
    return id && ownOk(id) ? id : null;   /* wave274 (one app): the city's own pages, not its tile's (Toronto alone never frames #volunteering) */
  }
