  /* ---------- the dock, the launcher and the rail ---------- */
  var S = { app: null, open: false, drawn: 0 };
  function nav() { return document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav'); }
  function ensure() {
    var n = nav(); if (!n) return false;
    if (!document.getElementById('w153rail')) {
      var r = document.createElement('div'); r.id = 'w153rail'; r.setAttribute('aria-label', 'App menu');
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (bar && bar.parentNode === n) bar.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', r); else n.appendChild(r);
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w153dock')) {
      var d = document.createElement('div'); d.id = 'w153dock';
      d.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="w153grid" aria-label="Apps" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false" data-w153grid="1">' + I('grid') + '<span>Apps</span></button>' +
        '<button type="button" class="w153back" data-w153back="1">' + I('back') + ' Back to the Hub</button>';
      n.appendChild(d);
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w153pop')) {
      var p = document.createElement('div'); p.id = 'w153pop'; p.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); p.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Apps');
      document.body.appendChild(p);
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w153bar')) {
      var main = document.querySelector('main');
      if (main) { var b = document.createElement('div'); b.id = 'w153bar'; main.insertBefore(b, main.firstChild); }
    }
    return true;
  }
  function tileHtml(a, on) {
    return '<button type="button" class="w153tile' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w153app="' + E(a.id) + '" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '"><span class="w153ai" style="background:' + a.color + '">' + I(a.ic) + '</span><b>' + E(a.label) + '</b><small>' + E(a.blurb) + '</small></button>';
  }
  /* 01/10/26: a link tile (the website): a real link, so it opens in a new tab and the Hub stays put */
  function linkHtml(l) {
    return '<a class="w153tile w153ext" href="' + E(l.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-w153ext="' + E(l.id) + '"><span class="w153ai" style="background:' + l.color + '">' + I(l.ic) + '</span><b>' + E(l.label) + I('ext', 'w153xi') + '</b><small>' + E(l.blurb) + '</small></a>';
  }
  function paintPop() {
    var p = document.getElementById('w153pop'); if (!p) return;
    var cur = tileOf(S.app || 'hub');   /* wave274 (one app): in Toronto the Volunteer Manager's tile is the current one */
    /* wave262: Your apps first, then More apps (the other apps this person can open) */
    var Y = yours(), tiles = function (ids) { return ids.map(function (id) { return tileHtml(APPS[id], id === cur); }).join(''); };
    p.innerHTML = '<div class="w153ph">' + (Y.all ? 'Apps' : 'Your apps') + '</div><div class="w153tiles">' + tileHtml(APPS.hub, cur === 'hub') + tiles(Y.yours) +
      (Y.more.length ? '</div><div class="w153ph w262ph">More apps</div><div class="w153tiles">' + tiles(Y.more) : '') +
      (LINKS.length ? '<div class="w153sep" role="presentation"></div>' + LINKS.map(linkHtml).join('') : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function openPop() { var p = document.getElementById('w153pop'), g = document.querySelector('#w153dock .w153grid'); if (!p) return; paintPop(); p.classList.add('open'); S.open = true; if (g) g.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true'); var f = p.querySelector('.w153tile.on') || p.querySelector('.w153tile'); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  function closePop() { var p = document.getElementById('w153pop'), g = document.querySelector('#w153dock .w153grid'); if (p) p.classList.remove('open'); S.open = false; if (g) g.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
  function togglePop() { if (S.open) closePop(); else openPop(); }

  /* the rail: the app's pages and sections, the current one marked */
  /* pages with sections switched inside the page: how to read the current one, and the button that opens one */
  var SEC = {
    finances: { now: function () { var t = document.querySelector('#finances .w94tab.on'); return t ? t.getAttribute('data-w94tab') : 'overview'; }, btn: function (k) { return document.querySelector('#finances .w94tab[data-w94tab="' + k + '"]'); }, count: function () { return document.querySelectorAll('#finances .w94tab').length; } },
    hrcentre: { now: function () { var t = document.querySelector('#hrcentre .w47tab.on'); return t ? t.getAttribute('data-w47v') : null; }, btn: function (k) { return document.querySelector('#hrcentre .w47tab[data-w47v="' + k + '"]'); }, count: function () { return document.querySelectorAll('#hrcentre .w47tab').length; } }
  };
  function secNow(page) { return SEC[page] ? SEC[page].now() : null; }
  function subOf(h) { return String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/')[1] || ''; }
  function firstOfPage(app, pg) { var gs = items(app); for (var i = 0; i < gs.length; i++) for (var j = 0; j < gs[i].items.length; j++) if (pageOf(gs[i].items[j].h) === pg) return gs[i].items[j]; return null; }
  function isOn(app, it) {
    var h = hashNow(), pg = pageOf(h), sub = subOf(h), ip = pageOf(it.h), isub = subOf(it.h);
    var hp = String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('?')[0], inAlso = function (x) { return (x.also || []).some(function (a) { a = String(a).replace(/^#/, ''); return hp === a || hp.indexOf(a + '/') === 0; }); };   /* wave306 */
    var owner = null; items(app).forEach(function (g) { g.items.forEach(function (x) { if (!owner && inAlso(x)) owner = x; }); }); if (owner) return owner.h === it.h;
    if (app === 'strategy' && pg === 'project') return ip === 'projects';   /* wave163: a project's own page marks Projects */
    if (ip !== pg) return false;
    if (app === 'fund' && ip === 'fund') {   /* wave193: #fund/<section>[/<view>|<id>]: the view is the third part; a grant's id marks the Pipeline */
      var fs = h.replace(/^#/, '').split('/'), is = it.h.replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
      if ((fs[1] || '') !== (is[1] || '')) return false;
      if ((fs[1] || '') !== 'grants') return true;
      var v = String(fs[2] || '').split('?')[0]; if (!/^(found|funders|write|playbook|facts)$/.test(v)) v = '';
      return v === String(is[2] || '');
    }
    if (it.sec) return secNow(ip) === it.sec;
    if (app === 'crm' && ip === 'crm') return (isub || 'contacts') === (sub === 'c' || !sub ? 'contacts' : sub);
    if (!sub) return isub ? firstOfPage(app, ip) === it : true;   /* the page alone: its first section */
    if (isub) return isub === sub;
    /* the page's own item while the address names a section the rail does not list */
    return !items(app).some(function (g) { return g.items.some(function (x) { return pageOf(x.h) === ip && subOf(x.h) === sub; }); });
  }
  /* wave274 (one app): the city switch, Montreal | Toronto: two real buttons, the current city pressed in its own colour. Shown
     only when this person may open both; a press goes to that city's home (#volunteering, #toronto), a page change, not an animation */
  function switchHtml(app) {
    app = app || S.app; if (!app || !APPS[app]) return '';
    var cs = cities(app); if (cs.length < 2) return '';
    return '<div class="w153city" role="group" aria-label="City">' + cs.map(function (c) {
      var on = c === app;
      return '<button type="button" data-w153city="' + E(c) + '" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '" style="--w153cc:' + APPS[c].color + '">' + E(APPS[c].city || APPS[c].label) + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function toCity(c) {
    var a = APPS[c]; if (!a || !ownOk(c)) return;
    if (c === S.app) return;   /* the city you are in: a pressed button stays pressed */
    var sw = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.closest ? document.activeElement.closest('.w153city') : null;
    S.cityFocus = sw && sw.closest('#w153rail') ? { c: c, until: Date.now() + 3000 } : null;   /* the keyboard keeps its place: the new city's button */
    go(a.home || firstHash(c));
  }
  function paintRail() {
    var r = document.getElementById('w153rail'); if (!r) return;
    var app = S.app; if (!app) { r.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    var a = APPS[app], gs = items(app);
    var fc = document.activeElement && r.contains(document.activeElement) ? document.activeElement.getAttribute('data-w153city') : null;   /* wave274 (one app) */
    var sw = switchHtml(app);   /* wave274 (one app): the city's own line, then the switch, under the app's name */
    r.innerHTML = '<div class="w153ah' + (sw ? ' w153ahc' : '') + '"><span class="w153ai" style="background:' + a.color + '">' + I(a.ic) + '</span><div><b>' + E(a.label) + '</b><small>' + E(a.line || a.blurb) + '</small></div>' + sw + '</div>' +
      gs.map(function (g) {
        return '<div class="w153sec">' + (g.solo ? '' : '<div class="w153sh">' + E(g.label) + '</div>') + g.items.map(function (it) {
          var on = isOn(app, it);
          return '<a class="w153it' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" href="' + E(it.h) + '" data-w153go="' + E(it.h) + '"' + (it.sec ? ' data-w153sec="' + E(it.sec) + '"' : '') + (on ? ' aria-current="page"' : '') + '>' + I(it.ic) + '<span>' + E(it.l) + '</span>' + (it.n ? '<small class="w153n">' + E(it.n) + '</small>' : '') + '</a>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
      }).join('') +
      (a.next ? '<div class="w153next"><b>Next in the ' + E(a.label) + '</b>' + a.next.map(function (n) { return '<span>' + I(n[0]) + E(n[1]) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    /* wave274 (one app): a redraw keeps the focus on the switch; after a switch by keyboard it lands on the new city's button */
    var cf = S.cityFocus && S.cityFocus.until > Date.now() && S.cityFocus.c === app ? app : null;
    if (cf) S.cityFocus = null;
    var want = cf || fc, fb = want ? r.querySelector('[data-w153city="' + want + '"]') : null;
    if (fb) { try { fb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* the app bar: the app, where you are, its quick actions */
  function whereNow(app) {
    var gs = items(app), found = null;
    gs.forEach(function (g) { g.items.forEach(function (it) { if (!found && isOn(app, it)) found = it; }); });
    if (found) return found.l;
    var h = hashNow(), pg = pageOf(h);
    var t = tab(pg); return t ? (t.textContent || '').trim() : '';
  }
  function paintBar() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w153bar'); if (!b) return;
    var app = S.app; if (!app) { b.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    var a = APPS[app], where = whereNow(app);
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="w153bc"><span class="w153ai" style="background:' + a.color + ';width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px">' + I(a.ic) + '</span><b>' + E(a.label) + '</b>' + (where ? '<span>' + E(where) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w153acts"><button type="button" class="w153menu" data-w153menu="1">' + I('menu') + ' Menu</button>' +
      (a.actions || []).filter(function (x) { return !x.page || x.page === pageOf(hashNow()); }).map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" class="w153btn" data-w153act="' + E(x.k) + '">' + I(x.ic) + ' ' + E(x.l) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function sig() { var h = hashNow(), pg = pageOf(h); return h + '|' + (SEC[pg] ? SEC[pg].now() + '/' + SEC[pg].count() : '') + '|' + (S.app || ''); }
  function paint() {
    if (!ensure()) return;
    S.sig = sig();
    var hn = hashNow(), pn = pageOf(hn), sn = subOf(hn);
    if (hn !== S.lastHash) { S.lastHash = hn; S.settled = null; }   /* a fresh address gets its section settled once */
    if (SEC[pn] && sn && S.settled !== hn && SEC[pn].now() !== sn) { S.settled = hn; settle(pn, sn); }
    var was = S.app; S.app = current(); S.sig = sig();
    document.body.classList.toggle('w153app', !!S.app);
    FRAMES.forEach(function (id) { document.body.classList.toggle('w153' + id, S.app === id); });   /* wave274 (one app): each city keeps its class (w153volunteer, w153toronto) */
    var dock = document.getElementById('w153dock');
    var any = ORDER.some(function (id) { return id !== 'hub' && appOk(id); });
    if (dock) dock.style.display = any ? '' : 'none';
    if (!S.app) { var h = hashNow(); if (h && pageOf(h) && !ownerOf(pageOf(h))) store(KEY_LAST, h); }
    else if (citiesOf(S.app).length > 1 && read(KEY_CITY) !== S.app) store(KEY_CITY, S.app);   /* wave274 (one app): the city in use, for the tile next time */
    paintRail(); paintBar(); if (S.open) paintPop();
    if (was !== S.app) try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa153app', { detail: { app: S.app } })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    S.drawn++;
  }

  /* ---------- open and close ---------- */
  function open(id) {
    closePop();
    if (id === 'hub' || !APPS[id]) return close();
    if (!appOk(id)) return;
    var pg = pageOf(hashNow());
    /* wave274 (one app): the Volunteer Manager stays in the city you are in, else opens the last one used, else the profile's */
    if (APPS[id].cities) { var inCity = ownerOf(pg); id = inCity && tileOf(inCity) === id && ownOk(inCity) ? inCity : cityFor(id); }   /* (a city named itself, open('toronto'), opens that city) */
    if (APPS[id].pages.indexOf(pg) < 0) { if (APPS[id].home) go(APPS[id].home); else { var gs = items(id), first = gs.length ? gs[0].items[0] : null; if (first) go(first.h, first.sec); else location.hash = '#mypage'; } } else paint();
    try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* back to the Hub: the page you left (an app's page always opens its app, so leaving means going somewhere else) */
  function close() {
    closePop();
    var last = read(KEY_LAST), lp = last ? pageOf(last) : '';
    var to = last && lp && !ownerOf(lp) ? last : '#mypage';
    if (location.hash !== to) location.hash = to; else paint();
    try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* a section named in the address: the page may still be drawing (some rebuild their tabs), so keep asking until it is on */
  function settle(pg, sec) {
    if (!SEC[pg] || !sec) return;
    if (S.settle) clearInterval(S.settle);
    var tries = 0, clicks = 0;
    S.settle = setInterval(function () {
      tries++;
      var sub = subOf(hashNow());
      if (pageOf(hashNow()) !== pg || (sub && sub !== sec)) { clearInterval(S.settle); S.settle = null; return; }
      if (SEC[pg].now() === sec) { clearInterval(S.settle); S.settle = null; setTimeout(paint, 60); return; }
      var t = SEC[pg].btn(sec);
      if (t && !t.classList.contains('on') && clicks < 3) { clicks++; t.click(); }
      if (tries > 30) { clearInterval(S.settle); S.settle = null; }
    }, 150);
  }
  function go(h, sec) {
    var pg = pageOf(h);
    if (location.hash !== h) location.hash = h; else { try { if (typeof applyHash === 'function') applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    if (sec) settle(pg, sec);
    try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function act(k) {
    if (S.app === 'studio' && k === 'newpost') { try { if (pageOf(hashNow()) === 'contentcal' && window.w121 && window.w121.compose) window.w121.compose(); else location.hash = '#contentcal/new'; } catch (e) { location.hash = '#contentcal/new'; } return; }   /* wave225 */
    if (S.app === 'volunteer' && k === 'text') { try { if (typeof waTxtModal === 'function') waTxtModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (S.app === 'toronto' && k === 'newevent') { try { if (window.w274 && window.w274.newEvent) window.w274.newEvent(); else location.hash = '#toronto'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }   /* wave274: New event opens its sheet */
    if (S.app === 'crm' && k === 'add') {
      if (pageOf(hashNow()) !== 'crm' || (hashNow().split('/')[1] || '') !== '') location.hash = '#crm';
      var tries = 0, iv = setInterval(function () { tries++; var b = document.querySelector('#crm [data-w152add]'); if (b) { clearInterval(iv); b.click(); } else if (tries > 40) clearInterval(iv); }, 100);
    }
  }

  /* ---------- Search everything finds contacts (staff with the CRM), first inside the CRM ---------- */
  var SEARCH_PH = 'Search everything: tasks, people, goals, meetings...';
  var CRM_PH = 'Search contacts: a name, email, phone, organization or tag';
  var loading = false;
  function crmContacts() { try { var st = window.w152 && window.w152.state && window.w152.state(); return st && st.contacts ? st.contacts : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function loadContacts() {
    if (loading || crmContacts()) return Promise.resolve(crmContacts());
    if (!window.w152 || !window.w152.load) return Promise.resolve(null);
    loading = true;
    return Promise.resolve(window.w152.load('contacts')).then(function () { loading = false; return crmContacts(); }, function () { loading = false; return null; });
  }
  function contactHits(q) {
    var list = crmContacts(); if (!list) return [];
    var fq = fold(q), words = fq.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean), digits = q.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!words.length && !digits) return [];
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length && out.length < 6; i++) {
      var c = list[i], hay = c._hay || fold([c.full_name, (c.emails || []).join(' '), (c.phones || []).join(' ')].join(' '));
      var ok = words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; }) || (digits.length >= 4 && (c._digits || '').indexOf(digits) >= 0);
      if (ok) out.push(c);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function kindWord(c) { var k = (c.kinds || [])[0] || ''; return { volunteer: 'Volunteer', donor: 'Donor', partner: 'Partner contact', supporter: 'Supporter', staff: 'Staff', alumni: 'Alumni', board: 'Board' }[k] || 'Contact'; }
  function rowFor(c) {

    });

  }
  function addHits(q) {
    var pop = document.getElementById('searchResults'); if (!pop || pop.style.display === 'none') return;
    if (pop.querySelector('[data-w153c]')) return;
    var hits = contactHits(q); if (!hits.length) return;

    if (empty && (empty.textContent || '').indexOf('No matches') >= 0) empty.remove();
    var first = S.app === 'crm';
    hits.forEach(function (c) { var row = rowFor(c); if (first) pop.insertBefore(row, pop.firstChild); else pop.appendChild(row); });
    if (first) { /* keep the contacts in the order found (each insert went to the top) */
      var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(pop.querySelectorAll('[data-w153c]')).reverse();
      rows.forEach(function (r) { pop.insertBefore(r, pop.firstChild); });
    }
  }
  var tS = null;
  if (typeof renderSearch === 'function') {

/* =====================================================================
   WA v162 the Loom pull (28/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I want to make sure that the hub pulls from loom automatically every monday, wednesday, and friday without the
   need for claude to be open". The loom-pull edge function reads Loom through the Atlassian Rovo MCP server (read tools
   only) on Monday, Wednesday and Friday at 7:30 in Toronto and files each new meeting as a transcript: the weekly team
   meetings for everyone, leadership meetings for the leaders, everything else confidential to the people in it and the
   leaders. Board meetings, anything that may be sensitive and meetings whose team is unclear wait for an admin.
   This segment: the Loom card at the top of the Transcript archive, for admins only (loom_pull_status()): the last pull
   and the next one, what came in, what needs a look (File it with a team and who sees it, or Skip), what was skipped,
   and Pull now. A stop in the Transcript archive tour.
   ===================================================================== */

  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }

  function load(force) {
    if (!force && S.ok && Date.now() - S.at < 3 * 60000) return Promise.resolve(S);
    if (S.busy) return S.busy;
    S.busy = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.rpc('loom_pull_status'); if (r && !r.error && r.data) { S.data = r.data; S.ok = true; S.at = Date.now(); } else S.ok = !!S.data; }
      catch (e) { S.ok = !!S.data; }
      S.busy = null; return S;
    })();
    return S.busy;
  }
  function when(iso, withTime) {
    if (!iso) return '';
    try {
      var d = new Date(iso); var o = { timeZone: TZ, weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' };
      var s = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', o);
      if (withTime) s += ' at ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
      return s;
    } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); }
  }
  function day(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; try { return new Date(String(iso).length === 10 ? iso + 'T12:00:00Z' : iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: String(iso).length === 10 ? 'UTC' : TZ, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(5, 10); } }
  function mins(s) { s = Number(s); if (!s) return ''; var m = Math.round(s / 60); return m < 60 ? m + ' min' : Math.floor(m / 60) + ' h ' + (m % 60 ? (m % 60) + ' min' : ''); }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : many); }
  function chip(v) {
    if (v.status === 'filed') {
      if (v.visibility === 'leadership') return '<span class="chip lead">Leaders</span>';
      if (v.visibility === 'speakers') return '<span class="chip priv">Only its speakers</span>'; /* wave165 */
      if (v.visibility === 'private') return '<span class="chip priv">Confidential' + (Array.isArray(v.allowed_people) ? ' &middot; ' + v.allowed_people.length : '') + '</span>';
      return '<span class="chip all">Everyone</span>';
    }
    if (v.status === 'needs_look') return '<span class="chip look">Needs a look</span>';
    if (v.status === 'waiting') return '<span class="chip off">Waiting</span>';
    if (v.status === 'error') return '<span class="chip priv">Could not read</span>';
    return '<span class="chip off">Skipped</span>';
  }
  function headline(d) {
    var last = d.last, next = d.next_run;
    var nextTxt = d.scheduled ? ' Next pull: <b>' + E(when(next, true)) + '</b>.' : ' The schedule is not on yet.';
    if (S.pulling || (last && last.status === 'running')) return { cls: '', t: 'Pulling from Loom now. This takes a minute or two.' };
    if (!last) return { cls: '', t: 'Meetings recorded in Loom come in by themselves on Monday, Wednesday and Friday at 7:30. No pull has run yet.' + nextTxt };
    if (last.status === 'no_key') return { cls: 'bad', t: 'Loom is not connected yet. Press Connect Loom, sign in to Atlassian as hub@welfareavenue.com and approve reading Loom.' + nextTxt };
    if (last.status === 'refused') return { cls: 'bad', t: 'Atlassian refused the Loom connection on ' + E(when(last.started_at, true)) + (last.note ? ' (' + E(last.note) + ')' : '') + '. Press Connect Loom and approve it again as hub@welfareavenue.com.' + nextTxt };
    var did = [];
    if (last.filed) did.push(plural(last.filed, 'meeting filed', 'meetings filed'));
    if (last.needs_look) did.push(plural(last.needs_look, 'needs a look', 'need a look'));
    if (last.waiting) did.push(plural(last.waiting, 'waiting for its transcript', 'waiting for their transcripts'));
    if (last.skipped) did.push(plural(last.skipped, 'skipped', 'skipped'));
    var what = did.length ? did.join(', ') : 'nothing new';
    if (last.status === 'error') return { cls: 'bad', t: 'The last pull (' + E(when(last.started_at, true)) + ') hit a problem: ' + E(last.note || 'unknown') + '. It tries again at the next pull.' + nextTxt };
    return { cls: '', t: 'Last pull <b>' + E(when(last.started_at, true)) + '</b>' + (last.why && last.why !== 'schedule' ? ' (' + E(last.why) + ')' : '') + ': ' + E(what) + '.' + (last.status === 'partial' && last.note ? ' ' + E(last.note) + '.' : '') + nextTxt };
  }

  function row(v, canFile) {
    var bits = [day(v.meeting_date || v.recorded_at), mins(v.duration_s)];
    if (v.speakers && v.speakers.length) bits.push(v.speakers.slice(0, 4).join(', ') + (v.speakers.length > 4 ? ' and ' + (v.speakers.length - 4) + ' more' : ''));

    var sub = bits.filter(Boolean).join(' · ');

    var acts = '';
    if (v.status === 'filed' && v.transcript_id) acts += '<a href="#tx/' + E(v.transcript_id) + '">Open</a>';
    if (v.url) acts += '<a class="out" href="' + E(v.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Loom' + I('out') + '</a>';
    if (canFile && v.status !== 'filed') acts += '<button type="button" class="w162btn small" data-w162file="' + E(v.loom_id) + '">File it</button>';
    if (v.status === 'needs_look' || v.status === 'waiting' || v.status === 'error') acts += '<button type="button" class="w162btn ghost small" data-w162skip="' + E(v.loom_id) + '">Skip</button>';
    return '<div class="row" data-w162row="' + E(v.loom_id) + '"><div class="main"><div class="t">' + chip(v) + E(v.title || 'Untitled recording') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="s">' + E(sub) + '</div>'
      + (v.reason ? '<div class="s">' + E(where) + E(v.reason) + (v.status === 'waiting' || v.status === 'error' ? ' (tried ' + E(v.attempts || 1) + (v.attempts === 1 ? ' time)' : ' times)') : '') + '</div>' : '')
      + '</div><div class="acts">' + acts + '</div>'
      + (S.open[v.loom_id] ? fileForm(v) : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function html() {
    var d = S.data || {};
    var hl = headline(d);
    var h = '<div class="hd"><div><h3>' + I('loom') + 'Meetings from Loom</h3><p class="when' + (hl.cls ? ' ' + hl.cls : '') + '">' + hl.t + '</p></div>'
      + '<div class="acts2">' + (d.connected ? '' : '<button type="button" class="w162btn ghost" data-w162connect="1"' + (S.connecting ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.connecting ? 'Opening Atlassian…' : 'Connect Loom') + '</button> ')
      + '<button type="button" class="w162btn" data-w162pull="1"' + (S.pulling ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (S.pulling ? ' Pulling…' : ' Pull now') + '</button></div></div>';
    var c = d.counts || {};
    h += '<div class="tiles">'
      + '<div class="tile"><b>' + E(c.filed || 0) + '</b><span>filed since ' + E(day(d.since)) + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="tile"><b' + ((c.needs_look || 0) ? ' class="warm"' : '') + '>' + E(c.needs_look || 0) + '</b><span>' + ((c.needs_look || 0) === 1 ? 'needs' : 'need') + ' a look</span></div>'
      + '<div class="tile"><b>' + E((c.waiting || 0) + (c.error || 0)) + '</b><span>waiting for Loom</span></div>'
      + '<div class="tile"><b>' + E(c.skipped || 0) + '</b><span>skipped</span></div>'
      + '</div>';
    var look = d.look || [];
    if (look.length) h += '<h4>Needs a look</h4>' + look.map(function (v) { return row(v, true); }).join('');
    /* wave210b (Steel, 06/10/26: "there's 2 sections with the same list of meetings ... Can we combine the 2?"):
       a filed meeting is in the Archive below, so this card lists only what Loom sent that was not filed */
    var recent = (d.recent || []).filter(function (v) { return v.status !== 'filed'; });
    if (recent.length) {
      var shown = S.showAll ? recent : recent.slice(0, 6);
      h += '<h4>Not filed</h4>' + shown.map(function (v) { return row(v, v.status === 'skipped' || v.status === 'error'); }).join('');
      if (recent.length > 6) h += '<button type="button" class="more" data-w162more="1">' + (S.showAll ? 'Show fewer' : 'Show all ' + recent.length) + '</button>';
    }
    h += '<p class="s" style="margin:8px 0 0">Meetings filed from Loom are in the Archive below.</p>';
    h += '<div class="foot">The weekly team meetings are filed for everyone, leadership meetings for the leaders, one-on-ones for the people speaking in them and nobody else (you see yours only), and everything else as confidential to the people in it and the leaders. Board meetings, anything that may be sensitive and meetings whose team is unclear wait here. '
      + (d.archive_only ? plural(d.archive_only, 'older meeting is', 'older meetings are') + ' kept for the archive and Ask the Hub only (no tasks came from them). ' : '')
      + (c.personal ? plural(c.personal, 'personal recording was', 'personal recordings were') + ' left out; nothing about them is kept. ' : '')
      + 'The Hub only reads Loom, as hub@welfareavenue.com, and never changes anything there. '
      + (d.connected ? 'Connected through Atlassian' + (d.connected_at ? ' since ' + E(day(d.connected_at)) : '') + ' (<button type="button" class="more" data-w162connect="1" style="padding:0">connect again</button>).' : 'Not connected through Atlassian yet.')
      + '</div>';
    return h;
  }
  function onPage() { return /^#transcripts\b/.test(location.hash || '') || !!(document.getElementById('transcripts') && document.getElementById('transcripts').classList.contains('active')); }
  function paint() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('transcripts'); if (!sec) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w162loom');
    if (!isAdmin()) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!S.ok) { if (onPage()) (S.busy || load()).then(function () { if (S.ok) paint(); }); return; }
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w162loom'; el.__h = '';
      var mast = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
      if (mast) mast.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); else sec.insertBefore(el, sec.firstChild);
    }
    var x = html();
    if (el.__h !== x) {
      /* keep what an admin picked in an open form */
      var keep = {};
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-w162form]').forEach(function (f) { keep[f.getAttribute('data-w162form')] = { t: (f.querySelector('[data-w162team]') || {}).value, v: (f.querySelector('[data-w162vis]') || {}).value }; });
      el.innerHTML = x; el.__h = x;
      Object.keys(keep).forEach(function (id) { var f = el.querySelector('[data-w162form="' + id + '"]'); if (!f) return; if (keep[id].t !== undefined) f.querySelector('[data-w162team]').value = keep[id].t; if (keep[id].v !== undefined) f.querySelector('[data-w162vis]').value = keep[id].v; });
    }
  }
  function refreshArchive() { try { if (typeof loadTranscriptsData === 'function') Promise.resolve(loadTranscriptsData()).then(function () { try { if (typeof renderTranscripts === 'function') renderTranscripts(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function pollRun(n) {
    clearTimeout(S.poll);
    S.poll = setTimeout(async function () {
      await load(true);
      var last = S.data && S.data.last;
      if (last && last.status === 'running' && n < 12) { paint(); pollRun(n + 1); return; }
      S.pulling = false; paint();
      if (last && last.status !== 'running') { toastS(last.filed ? 'Loom: ' + plural(last.filed, 'meeting filed', 'meetings filed') : 'Loom: the pull is done'); if (last.filed) refreshArchive(); }
    }, n ? 15000 : 8000);
  }
  async function fnError(r) {
    try { if (r && r.error && r.error.context && typeof r.error.context.json === 'function') { var j = await r.error.context.json(); if (j && j.error) return j.error; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return (r && r.error && r.error.message) || (r && r.data && r.data.error) || '';
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162pull]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (S.pulling || el.disabled) return;
      S.pulling = true; paint();
      db.functions.invoke('loom-pull', { body: { action: 'sync' } }).then(async function (r) {
        if (r && !r.error && r.data && (r.data.accepted || r.data.run)) { toastS('Pulling from Loom. The list updates in a minute or two.'); pollRun(0); return; }
        var msg = await fnError(r);
        S.pulling = false; await load(true); paint();
        toastS(/already running/i.test(msg) ? 'A pull is already running' : 'The Loom pull could not start just now');
      }).catch(function () { S.pulling = false; paint(); toastS('The Loom pull could not start just now'); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162connect]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (S.connecting) return;
      S.connecting = true; paint();
      db.functions.invoke('loom-connect', { body: { action: 'start' } }).then(async function (r) {
        if (r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.url) { location.href = r.data.url; return; }
        var msg = await fnError(r);
        S.connecting = false; paint(); toastS('Atlassian could not be opened just now' + (msg ? ': ' + msg : ''));
      }).catch(function () { S.connecting = false; paint(); toastS('Atlassian could not be opened just now'); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162file]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var id = el.getAttribute('data-w162file'); S.open[id] = !S.open[id]; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162cancel]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.open[el.getAttribute('data-w162cancel')] = false; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162more]'))) { e.preventDefault(); S.showAll = !S.showAll; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162save]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (S.filing) return;
      var lid = el.getAttribute('data-w162save');
      var form = el.closest('[data-w162form]');
      var team = form && form.querySelector('[data-w162team]').value, vis = form && form.querySelector('[data-w162vis]').value;
      if (!team) { toastS('Pick the team it belongs to'); return; }
      if (team === 'Leadership meeting' && vis === 'private') vis = 'leadership';
      S.filing = lid; paint();
      db.functions.invoke('loom-pull', { body: { action: 'file', loom_id: lid, department: team, visibility: vis } }).then(async function (r) {
        var okd = r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.ok;
        var msg = okd ? '' : await fnError(r);
        S.filing = null;
        if (okd) { S.open[lid] = false; toastS(r.data.already ? 'It was already in the archive' : 'Filed. The team page updates in a few minutes.'); refreshArchive(); }
        else toastS(msg ? 'Not filed: ' + msg : 'It could not be filed just now');
        await load(true); paint();
      }).catch(function () { S.filing = null; paint(); toastS('It could not be filed just now'); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w162skip]'))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var sid = el.getAttribute('data-w162skip');
      db.rpc('loom_video_decide', { p_loom_id: sid, p_action: 'skip' }).then(async function (r) {
        if (r && r.error) { toastS('It could not be skipped: ' + (r.error.message || '')); return; }
        S.open[sid] = false; toastS('Skipped. File it later from the list if you change your mind.');
        await load(true); paint();
      });
    }
  });

  var mo = null;
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('transcripts'); if (!sec || mo) return;
    var pending = false;
    mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (pending || !S.ok || !isAdmin() || document.getElementById('w162loom')) return; pending = true; setTimeout(function () { pending = false; paint(); }, 60); });
    mo.observe(sec, { childList: true });
  }
  var tries = 0;
  function kick() {
    try {
      watch();
      if (!onPage()) { paint(); return; }
      if (isAdmin()) { tries = 0; load().then(paint); return; }
      paint();
      /* the access profile may still be on its way: look again a few times */
      if (!mine().role && tries < 8) { tries++; setTimeout(kick, 700); }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(kick, 120); });
  /* back from Atlassian's approval (loom-connect): ?loom=connected | cancelled | error&why=... */
  (function () {
    try {
      var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search || ''); var res = q.get('loom'); if (!res) return;
      var why = q.get('why') || '';
      q.delete('loom'); q.delete('why');
      history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + (q.toString() ? '?' + q.toString() : '') + (location.hash || '#transcripts'));
      setTimeout(function () {
        toastS(res === 'connected' ? 'Loom is connected. The next pull uses it.' : res === 'cancelled' ? 'Connecting Loom was cancelled' : 'Loom was not connected: ' + why);
        load(true).then(paint);
      }, 1800);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  })();
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa162 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa162.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(kick, 200); return out; };
  }

  /* the Transcript archive tour: a stop for the Loom card, after the masthead */
  function addStep() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T || !T.transcripts || !T.transcripts.steps || T.transcripts.__w162) return;
      var steps = T.transcripts.steps;
      var i = steps.map(function (s) { return s.s; }).indexOf('#transcripts > .w118mast');
      steps.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : 0, 0, { s: '#w162loom', t: 'Meetings from Loom', p: 'Every Monday, Wednesday and Friday at 7:30 the Hub pulls new Loom meetings: team meetings for everyone, leadership meetings for the leaders, the rest confidential to the people in them and the leaders. Anything that needs a look waits here for an admin to file or skip.', opt: 1 });
      T.transcripts.__w162 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addStep(); setTimeout(addStep, 1500);

  window.w162 = { load: load, state: function () { return S; }, paint: paint, html: html };
})();

(function () {
  if (window.__wa166) return; window.__wa166 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', MUTED = '#797773', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', LINE = '#E7E2D6', CREAM = '#FFFAF2';
  var S = { lead: null, loading: null, at: 0, err: null, busy: {}, open: {} };
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function todayIso() { try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); } catch (e) { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function fmtDay(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '';
    try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: 'UTC', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); }
  }
  function ago(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '';
    var d = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 86400000);
    if (!(d >= 0)) return '';
    return d === 0 ? 'today' : (d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d + ' days ago');
  }
  function initials(n) { return String(n || '?').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 2).map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase(); }).join(''); }
  /* wave168: a person's photo on the leads' card, only from the Hub's own storage (Slack and Google photos are copied there) */

  function meName() { try { var m = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null; return (m && m.name) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }

  var CSS = ''

    + '#w166lead .w166st.child{padding-left:32px;}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st:before{content:"";position:absolute;left:2px;top:7px;width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;border:1.5px solid ' + MUTED + ';}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st.child:before{left:16px;}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st.done{color:' + MUTED + ';}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st.done:before{background:' + GREEN + ';border-color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st.late{color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + '#w166lead .w166st em{font-style:normal;font-size:11px;color:' + TEAL + ';margin-left:6px;}'
    + '@media (max-width:700px){#w166lead .w166top{flex-wrap:wrap;}#w166lead .w166day{text-align:left;}}';
  if (!document.getElementById('wa166css')) { var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'wa166css'; st.textContent = CSS; document.head.appendChild(st); }

  /* ---------- Loom videos play in place ("Watch here") ----------
     In onboarding steps, the Academy's lessons and readings, the HR checklists and the leads' card, a Loom link gets a Watch
     here button; the video opens under the paragraph as a Loom embed (loaded only when asked) and closes again. A link next to
     a player that already shows the same video (the Academy's video sheet) is left alone, and so are the Loom card's meeting
     recordings (they are meetings, not trainings). */
  var LOOM_RE = /loom\.com\/(?:share|embed)\/([0-9a-f]{32})/i;
  var SCOPES = '#training, #w122sheet, .modal-back, #w166lead, #hrcentre';
  function loomIdOf(href) { var m = String(href || '').match(LOOM_RE); return m ? m[1].toLowerCase() : ''; }
  function playerNear(a, id) {
    var box = a.closest('.wrap, .w37step, .ds, .prose, .modal-back, .sh') || a.parentElement;
    if (!box) return false;
    return [].slice.call(box.querySelectorAll('iframe')).some(function (f) { return f.src && f.src.indexOf(id) >= 0 && !f.closest('.w166vidwrap'); });
  }
  function upgrade(root) {
    var n = 0;
    (root || document).querySelectorAll('a[href*="loom.com/share/"], a[href*="loom.com/embed/"]').forEach(function (a) {
      if (a.getAttribute('data-w166') || !a.closest(SCOPES) || a.closest('#w162loom') || a.closest('.w166vidwrap') || a.classList.contains('w122lnk')) return;
      var id = loomIdOf(a.getAttribute('href')); if (!id) return;
      a.setAttribute('data-w166', '1');
      if (playerNear(a, id)) return;
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w166play'; b.setAttribute('data-w166loom', id); b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      b.innerHTML = '&#9654; Watch here';
      a.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', b); n++;
    });
    return n;
  }
  function hostOf(b) {
    /* the block the player goes under: the paragraph, list item or step text holding the link */
    var h = b.closest('p, li, .ds, .w166ask, .w166ls') || b.parentElement;
    return h;
  }
  function toggle(b) {
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-w166loom'); var host = hostOf(b); if (!host) return;
    var next = host.nextElementSibling;
    if (next && next.classList.contains('w166vidwrap') && next.getAttribute('data-id') === id) {
      next.remove(); b.innerHTML = '&#9654; Watch here'; b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); return;
    }
    var w = document.createElement('div'); w.className = 'w166vidwrap'; w.setAttribute('data-id', id);
    w.innerHTML = '<iframe src="https://www.loom.com/embed/' + id + '?hide_owner=true&hide_share=true&hide_title=true&hideEmbedTopBar=true" title="Loom video" allow="autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture" allowfullscreen loading="lazy"></iframe>';
    host.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', w);
    b.innerHTML = 'Hide the video'; b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w166loom]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    try { toggle(b); } catch (err) { /* fine */ }
  }, true);
  var upT = null;
  function soon() { clearTimeout(upT); upT = setTimeout(function () { try { afterDom(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 120); }
  try { new MutationObserver(function (list) { for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) { if (list[i].addedNodes && list[i].addedNodes.length) { soon(); return; } } }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- the plan: Academy steps say so, show how far the person is, and open the module or the avenue ---------- */
  var REQ = { hub: 'Using the Hub', foundations: 'Welfare Avenue foundations', branding: 'Our brand' };
  function academy() { try { return (window.w122 && window.w122.A) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function myName() {
    var n = meName(); if (n) return n;
    try { var full = String((USER && USER.name) || ''), em = String((USER && USER.email) || ''); return full ? full.split(' ')[0] : em.split('@')[0]; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function personOf(onb) {
    try {
      var m = null;
      if (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' && onb) m = WA28_PEOPLE.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(onb.member_id) || String(p.name).toLowerCase() === String(onb.person_name || '').toLowerCase(); })[0] || null;
      return { name: (onb && onb.person_name) || (m && m.name) || '', email: (m && m.email) || '', dept: (m && m.department) || '' };
    } catch (e) { return { name: (onb && onb.person_name) || '', email: '', dept: '' }; }
  }
  /* the person's Academy in one pass (the same rules as wa166_path_state and wa166_training_done: the database ticks the
     step, this shows it at once): what they finished, and every avenue with how far they are */
  function ctxFor(who) {
    var A = academy(); if (!A || !A.loaded) return null;
    var n = String(who.name || '').toLowerCase(), em = String(who.email || '').toLowerCase(), done = {};
    (A.prog || []).forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.status === 'done' && (String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === n || (em && String(p.email || '').toLowerCase() === em))) done[String(p.training_id)] = true;
    });
    var byGroup = {};
    (A.trainings || []).forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.active === false || t.kind === 'tour' || (t.body && t.body.planned)) return;
      (byGroup[t.path_group] = byGroup[t.path_group] || []).push(t);
    });
    var all = (A.paths || []).filter(function (p) { return p.active !== false; }).map(function (p) {
      var items = byGroup[p.name] || [];
      var d = items.filter(function (t) { return done[String(t.id)]; }).length;
      return { key: p.key, title: p.title || p.name, dept: p.dept || null, required: !!p.required, sort: p.sort == null ? 1e9 : Number(p.sort),
        done: d, total: items.length, complete: items.length > 0 && d >= items.length, ratio: items.length ? d / items.length : 0 };
    });
    return { A: A, who: who, done: done, all: all };
  }
  function pick(list) {
    return list.slice().sort(function (x, y) {
      return (y.complete - x.complete) || (y.ratio - x.ratio) || (x.sort - y.sort) || String(x.key).localeCompare(String(y.key));
    })[0] || null;
  }
  /* 'team': the team's avenue they are furthest in (any avenue besides the three everyone does when the team has none);
     'any': an avenue besides the three everyone does and the one that counted for 'team' (one avenue never ticks both) */
  function pathState(cx, key) {
    var elig = cx.all.filter(function (p) { return !p.required && ['tours', 'firstweek', 'keela'].indexOf(p.key) < 0; });
    var team = elig.filter(function (p) { return p.dept && p.dept === cx.who.dept; });
    var teamPick = pick(team.length ? team : elig);
    var cand;
    if (key === 'team') cand = teamPick ? [teamPick] : [];
    else if (key === 'any') cand = elig.filter(function (p) { return !(teamPick && teamPick.complete && p.key === teamPick.key); });
    else cand = cx.all.filter(function (p) { return p.key === key; });
    var best = pick(cand);
    return best ? { key: best.key, title: best.title, done: best.done, total: best.total, complete: best.complete, teamHas: team.length > 0 }
      : { key: key, title: null, done: 0, total: 0, complete: false, teamHas: team.length > 0 };
  }
  function linkState(cx, t) {
    if (!cx) return null;
    if (t.training_id) {
      var tr = cx.A.byId ? cx.A.byId[String(t.training_id)] : null;
      return { kind: 'module', id: t.training_id, title: tr ? tr.title : 'the module', mins: tr ? (tr.minutes || null) : null, done: !!cx.done[String(t.training_id)] };
    }
    if (t.academy_path) {
      var s = pathState(cx, t.academy_path);
      return { kind: 'avenue', key: s.key, title: s.title || REQ[t.academy_path] || 'the avenue', done: s.complete, n: s.done, total: s.total, asked: t.academy_path, teamHas: s.teamHas };
    }
    return null;
  }
  function chipHtml(t, ls, onb) {
    if (/^role(60|90)$/.test(t.academy_path || '') && window.w279 && typeof window.w279.roleStep === 'function') return window.w279.roleStep(t, onb);   /* wave279: the role step lists the manager's picks */
    if (!t.training_id && !t.academy_path) return '';
    if (!ls) return '<div><span class="w166acad">Avenue Academy</span></div>';
    var open = (ls.asked === 'team' || ls.asked === 'any') && !ls.n && !ls.done;   /* nothing started yet: they choose */
    var what = ls.kind === 'module' ? 'Avenue Academy module' + (ls.mins ? ' &middot; ' + ls.mins + ' min' : '')
      : (open ? (ls.asked === 'team' && ls.teamHas ? 'Your team&rsquo;s Learning Avenues' : 'Learning Avenues')
        : (ls.title ? E(ls.title) : 'Learning Avenue') + (ls.total ? ' &middot; ' + ls.n + ' of ' + ls.total : ''));
    var go = ls.kind === 'module' ? '<button type="button" class="w166go" data-w166open="t:' + E(ls.id) + '">' + (ls.done ? 'Open it again' : 'Open it') + '</button>'
      : (!open && ls.key && ls.key !== 'team' && ls.key !== 'any' ? '<button type="button" class="w166go" data-w166open="p:' + E(ls.key) + '">' + (ls.done ? 'Open the avenue' : 'Go to the avenue') + '</button>'
        : '<button type="button" class="w166go" data-w166open="a:">Browse the avenues</button>');
    return '<div><span class="w166acad' + (ls.done ? ' ok' : '') + '">' + (ls.done ? '&#10003; ' : '') + what + '</span>' + go + '</div>';
  }
  /* the plan's steps with what the Academy already finished */
  function planStates(onb) {
    var cx = ctxFor(personOf(onb)), m = (typeof wa37StepsFor === 'function') ? wa37StepsFor(onb.id) : {};
    var weeks = (typeof wa37Weeks === 'function') ? wa37Weeks() : [], out = [];
    weeks.forEach(function (wk) {
      var list = wk.steps.filter(function (t) { return m[t.id]; }).map(function (t) {
        var ls = linkState(cx, t);
        return { t: t, s: m[t.id], ls: ls, done: !!(m[t.id].done || (ls && ls.done)) };
      });
      if (list.length) out.push({ name: wk.name, steps: list });
    });
    return out;
  }
  var __chk166 = (typeof wa37Checklist === 'function') ? wa37Checklist : null;
  function checklist(onb, canTick) {
    var today = todayIso(), html = '', firstOpen = false;
    planStates(onb).forEach(function (wk) {
      var d = wk.steps.filter(function (x) { return x.done; }).length, n = wk.steps.length;
      /* wave285: the week's count follows the one progress rule (real steps only; a header is done when its steps are; setup and Academy steps count) */
      var w285c = null; try { w285c = (window.w285 && typeof window.w285.secCount === 'function') ? window.w285.secCount(onb.id, wk.name) : null; } catch (e) { w285c = null; }
      if (w285c && w285c.total) { d = w285c.done; n = w285c.total; }
      var open = !firstOpen && d < n; if (open) firstOpen = true;
      html += '<details class="w37week"' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + E(wk.name)
        + ' <span class="w37bar" style="max-width:140px"><i style="width:' + Math.round(100 * d / Math.max(1, n)) + '%"></i></span>'
        + '<span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;font-weight:500">' + d + ' of ' + n + '</span></summary><div class="w37wbody">';
      wk.steps.forEach(function (x) {
        var t = x.t, s = x.s, ls = x.ls, done = x.done, acad = !!(t.training_id || t.academy_path);
        /* wave285: the rule's done (a header when all its steps are, a setup step setup covered, an Academy step): shown ticked and locked, with why */
        var w285w = null; try { w285w = (window.w285 && typeof window.w285.stepWhy === 'function') ? window.w285.stepWhy(onb.id, t.id) : null; } catch (e) { w285w = null; }
        if (w285w && w285w.done != null) done = w285w.done;
        var lock = !!(w285w && (w285w.header || (w285w.done && !s.done)));
        var why = !w285w || s.done ? 'done in the Avenue Academy' : w285w.header ? 'done when its steps are' : w285w.how === 'setup' ? 'done at setup' : 'done in the Avenue Academy';
        var due = '';
        if (!done && s.due_date) due = '<span class="w44due' + (String(s.due_date) < today ? ' late' : '') + '">due ' + E(String(s.due_date)) + '</span>';
        html += '<div class="w37step' + (t.parent_id ? ' child' : '') + (done ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w166t="' + E(t.id) + '" data-w57id="' + E(t.id) + '">'
          + '<input type="checkbox" data-w37chk="' + s.id + '"' + (done ? ' checked' : '') + (canTick && !(ls && ls.done) && !lock ? '' : ' disabled')
          + (acad ? ' title="' + (ls && ls.done ? 'Done in the Avenue Academy' : 'Ticks itself when it is finished in the Avenue Academy') + '"' : '') + ' />'
          + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="nm">' + E(t.name)
          + (t.priority === 'high' ? '<span class="pr">important</span>' : '') + due + '</div>'
          + chipHtml(t, ls, onb)
          + (t.description ? '<div class="ds">' + ((typeof wa37Md === 'function') ? wa37Md(t.description) : E(t.description)) + '</div>' : '')
          + (s.done && s.done_by ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.6">done by ' + E(s.done_by) + '</div>' : (done && !s.done ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.6">' + why + '</div>' : (w285w && w285w.header && !done ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.6">done when its steps are</div>' : '')))
          + '</div></div>';
      });
      html += '</div></details>';
    });
    return html;
  }
  if (__chk166) wa37Checklist = checklist;
  /* open a module or an avenue from the plan (the plan sheet closes behind it in the Academy's own routing) */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w166open]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();

    else location.hash = '#training';
  }, true);

  /* the plan sheet in the Academy: which onboarding it shows (the Academy's own choice: the person's, not cancelled) */
  function planOnb() {
    try {
      if (window.__w169view) return window.__w169view;   /* wave169: HR previews someone's plan, or a new person's */
      if (typeof WA37 === 'undefined' || !WA37.onbs) return null;
      var n = myName().toLowerCase();
      return WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status !== 'cancelled' && String(o.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === n; })[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  /* its counts include the steps finished in the Academy that the page has not reloaded yet */
  function syncHead() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh || !sh.querySelector('.onbwrap')) return;
    var o = planOnb(); if (!o) return;
    var total = 0, done = 0;
    planStates(o).forEach(function (wk) { wk.steps.forEach(function (x) { total++; if (x.done) done++; }); });
    try { var w285p = window.w285 && window.w285.plan ? window.w285.plan(o.id) : null; if (w285p && w285p.total) { total = w285p.total; done = w285p.done; } } catch (e) { /* the count above */ }   /* wave285: the one progress rule */
    if (!total) return;
    var kk = sh.querySelector('.hd .kk span:last-child'); if (kk && / steps$/.test(kk.textContent)) kk.textContent = done + ' of ' + total + ' steps';
    var bar = sh.querySelector(':scope > .pr > i'); if (bar) bar.style.width = Math.round(100 * done / total) + '%';
    var st = sh.querySelector('.ft .st'); if (st && !st.querySelector('.ok') && / done$/.test(st.textContent)) st.textContent = done + ' of ' + total + ' done';
  }
  /* draw the plan again in place (after the Academy ticked steps), keeping the weeks that were open and the scroll */
  function replan() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh) return;
    var wrap = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap'); if (!wrap) return;
    var o = planOnb(); if (!o) return;
    var open = [].map.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('details.w37week'), function (d) { return d.open; });
    var main = sh.querySelector('.main'), top = main ? main.scrollTop : 0;
    wrap.innerHTML = checklist(o, !window.__w169view);   /* wave169: a preview is read only */
    [].forEach.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('details.w37week'), function (d, i) { if (open[i] != null) d.open = open[i]; });
    if (main) main.scrollTop = top;
    syncHead();
    try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('w166replan')); } catch (e) { /* fine: wave167's journey listens */ }
  }
  /* the plan sheet: say how Academy steps work, and ask for help from here too */
  function decoratePlan() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh) return;
    var wrap = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap'); if (!wrap || wrap.getAttribute('data-w166')) return;
    wrap.setAttribute('data-w166', '1');
    var intro = sh.querySelector('.main .prose p');
    if (intro && intro.textContent.indexOf('Avenue Academy') < 0) intro.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', ' Steps marked Avenue Academy tick themselves when you finish the module or the avenue here, and the videos play inside the Hub.');
    var mine = planOnb(); if (mine && (mine.status !== 'active' || window.__w169view)) mine = null;   /* wave169: no help form in a preview */
    if (mine) {
      /* the Academy's sheet sits above every modal, so the form opens right here */
      var left = planStates(mine).reduce(function (a, wk) { return a.concat(wk.steps.filter(function (x) { return !x.done; }).map(function (x) { return x.t; })); }, []);
      wrap.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', '<div class="w166help" id="w166help"><button type="button" data-w166help="' + E(mine.id) + '">Stuck on a step? Ask for help</button> <span style="font-size:12px;color:#797773">HR and your lead see it in the Hub.</span>'
        + '<div class="w166hform">'
        + '<label style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39">Which step<select id="w166hs" style="display:block;width:100%;margin-top:4px">' + left.map(function (t) { return '<option>' + E(t.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option>Something else</option></select></label>'
        + '<label style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39">What is getting in the way<textarea id="w166hn" rows="3" style="display:block;width:100%;margin-top:4px" placeholder="A link that does not open, an invite that never came, a step that is unclear..."></textarea></label>'
        + '<div><button type="button" class="w122btn" data-w166hsend="' + E(mine.id) + '">Send it</button></div></div></div>');
    }
    syncHead();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w166hsend]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (b.disabled) return; b.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('onboarding_help').insert({ onboarding_id: Number(b.getAttribute('data-w166hsend')), person_name: myName() || 'A team member',
        step_name: (document.getElementById('w166hs') || {}).value || 'an onboarding step', note: ((document.getElementById('w166hn') || {}).value || '').trim() || null });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var box = document.getElementById('w166help'); if (box) box.innerHTML = '<span class="w166acad ok">&#10003; Sent</span> <span style="font-size:12.5px;color:#4a5551">HR and your lead see it in the Hub and will reach out.</span>';
    } catch (err) { b.disabled = false; T('Could not send it'); }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w166help]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    var f = b.parentElement && b.parentElement.querySelector('.w166hform'); if (f) { f.classList.toggle('on'); if (f.classList.contains('on')) { var ta = f.querySelector('textarea'); if (ta) ta.focus(); } }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- Home: the people a lead is onboarding, and where the lead comes in (onboarding_lead_view) ---------- */
  function load(force) {
    if (S.loading) return S.loading;
    if (S.lead && !force && Date.now() - S.at < 60000) return Promise.resolve(S.lead);
    S.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('onboarding_lead_view');
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        S.lead = (r && r.data) || { people: [] }; S.err = null;
      } catch (e) { S.err = String((e && e.message) || e); S.lead = S.lead || { people: [] }; }
      S.at = Date.now(); S.loading = null;
      return S.lead;
    })();
    return S.loading;
  }
  var SEC_SHORT = { 'WEEK 1': 'Week 1', 'WEEK 2': 'Week 2', 'WEEK 3': 'Week 3', 'WEEK 4': 'Week 4', '60 DAYS': '60 days', '90 DAYS': '90 days', '6 MONTHS': '6 months' };
  function weekOf(day) { if (day <= 7) return 'Week 1 of 4'; if (day <= 14) return 'Week 2 of 4'; if (day <= 21) return 'Week 3 of 4'; if (day <= 28) return 'Week 4 of 4'; if (day <= 60) return 'Up to 60 days'; if (day <= 90) return 'Up to 90 days'; return 'Past 90 days'; }   /* wave279: day is day one = 1, so 0 to 27 days in is Week n of 4, then 60 and 90 days */
  function leadStepsHtml(p) {
    var today = todayIso(), soonDay = new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    var steps = (p.lead_steps || []).slice();
    var open = steps.filter(function (s) { return !s.done; }), done = steps.filter(function (s) { return s.done; });
    var showOpen = open.filter(function (s) { return !s.due || String(s.due) <= soonDay; });
    var later = open.length - showOpen.length;
    var h = '';
    showOpen.forEach(function (s) {
      var late = s.due && String(s.due) < today;
      var extra = '';
      if (/quiz/i.test(s.name || '')) extra = (p.quizzes_done >= 2 ? 'Both quizzes are in: review them with them.' : (p.quizzes_done === 1 ? 'One of the two quizzes is in.' : 'Their quizzes come in week 4.'));
      h += '<label class="w166ls"><input type="checkbox" data-w166tick="' + E(s.id) + '"' + (S.busy['t' + s.id] ? ' disabled' : '') + ' /><span>' + E(s.name)
        + (s.due ? '<span class="w166due' + (late ? ' late' : (String(s.due) <= soonDay ? ' soon' : '')) + '">' + (late ? 'was due ' : 'by ') + E(fmtDay(s.due)) + '</span>' : '')
        + (s.description || extra ? '<small>' + E(extra || s.description) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></label>';
    });
    if (later) h += '<div class="w166note">Later: ' + later + ' more of yours, starting ' + E(fmtDay((open.filter(function (s) { return String(s.due || '') > soonDay; })[0] || {}).due)) + '.</div>';
    if (!showOpen.length && !later && done.length) h += '<div class="w166note">All your moments are done. Thank you for bringing them in.</div>';
    if (done.length && (showOpen.length || later)) h += '<div class="w166note" style="color:#797773">Done: ' + done.map(function (s) { return E(s.name); }).join(', ') + '.</div>';
    (p.help || []).forEach(function (q) {
      h += '<div class="w166ask"><b>Asked for help</b> on ' + E(q.step || 'a step') + (q.note ? ': \u201c' + E(q.note) + '\u201d' : '') + ' <span style="color:#8a6414">(' + E(ago(q.at)) + ')</span>'
        + '<button type="button" data-w166resolve="' + E(q.id) + '">Dealt with</button></div>';
    });
    if (p.late_count) {
      h += '<div class="w166note"><b style="color:#B3402F">Behind on ' + p.late_count + (p.late_count === 1 ? ' step' : ' steps') + '</b>: '
        + (p.late || []).slice(0, 3).map(function (l) { return E(l.name) + ' (due ' + E(fmtDay(l.due)) + ')'; }).join(', ')
        + (p.late_count > 3 ? ', and more' : '') + '. A quick message or ten minutes together usually unblocks it.</div>';
    }
    return h;
  }
  function planHtml(p) {
    var today = todayIso(), by = {}, order = [];
    (p.steps || []).forEach(function (s) { if (!by[s.section]) { by[s.section] = []; order.push(s.section); } by[s.section].push(s); });
    return order.map(function (sec) {
      return '<div class="w166sec"><b>' + E(SEC_SHORT[sec] || sec) + '</b>' + by[sec].map(function (s) {
        var late = !s.done && s.due && String(s.due) < today;
        return '<div class="w166st' + (s.child ? ' child' : '') + (s.done ? ' done' : '') + (late ? ' late' : '') + '">' + E(s.name) + (s.academy ? '<em>Avenue Academy</em>' : '') + (late ? ' <span style="font-size:11px">(due ' + E(fmtDay(s.due)) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function personHtml(p) {
    var pct = p.total ? Math.round(100 * p.done / p.total) : 0;
    var status = p.late_count ? '<span class="late">' + p.late_count + ' late</span>' : '<span class="ok">on pace</span>';
    var wks = (p.sections || []).map(function (s) {
      var cls = s.done >= s.total ? ' done' : (s.due && String(s.due) < todayIso() ? ' late' : '');
      return '<span class="w166wk' + cls + '">' + E(SEC_SHORT[s.name] || s.name) + ' ' + s.done + '/' + s.total + '</span>';
    }).join('');
    var av = (p.academy || []).filter(function (a) { return a && a.total; }).map(function (a) {
      if (a.asked === 'team' && !a.done && !a.complete) return '<span>Team avenue: not started</span>';
      return '<span class="' + (a.complete ? 'ok' : '') + '">' + (a.complete ? '&#10003; ' : '') + E(a.title || a.key) + ' ' + a.done + '/' + a.total + '</span>';
    }).join('');
    var sub = [p.title, teamName(p.department), p.via].filter(Boolean).map(E).join(' &middot; ');
    return '<div class="w166p" data-w166p="' + E(p.onboarding_id) + '">'
      + '<div class="w166top"><div class="w166av"' + (ownImg(p.avatar_url) ? ' style="background:' + GREEN + ' url(\'' + E(p.avatar_url) + '\') center/cover"' : '') + '>' + (ownImg(p.avatar_url) ? '' : E(initials(p.name))) + '</div><div class="w166who"><b>' + E(p.name) + '</b><span>' + sub + '</span></div>'
      + (p.starts_on ? '<div class="w166day">Starts on ' + E(fmtDay(p.starts_on)) + '</div></div>'   /* wave271: the call date can be ahead, and there is no day yet to count */
        : '<div class="w166day">Day ' + E(p.day) + '<span>' + E(weekOf(p.day)) + '</span></div></div>')
      + '<div class="w166bar" title="' + pct + '% of their plan"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>'
      + '<div class="w166sum">' + p.done + ' of ' + p.total + ' steps &middot; ' + status + (p.last_done_at ? ' &middot; last step ' + E(ago(p.last_done_at)) : ' &middot; no step ticked yet')
      + (p.next ? ' &middot; next: ' + E(p.next.name) : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w166wks">' + wks + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w166you"><div class="w166h">Where you come in</div>' + (leadStepsHtml(p) || '<div class="w166note">Nothing for you right now.</div>') + '</div>'
      + (av ? '<div class="w166av2"><b>Avenue Academy</b>' + av + '</div>' : '')
      + '<details class="w166plan"' + (S.open[p.onboarding_id] ? ' open' : '') + ' data-w166plan="' + E(p.onboarding_id) + '"><summary>See their plan</summary>' + planHtml(p) + '</details>'
      + '</div>';
  }
  function cardHtml(d) {
    var ppl = (d && d.people) || [];
    if (!ppl.length) return '';
    return '<h3>' + (ppl.length === 1 ? 'Your new person\u2019s onboarding' : 'Your people\u2019s onboarding (' + ppl.length + ')') + '</h3>'
      + '<p style="margin:0 0 10px;font-size:12.5px;color:#4a5551">How each person you are bringing in is doing, and the moments that are yours. Tick yours as you do them.</p>'
      + '<div class="w166ppl">' + ppl.map(personHtml).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody');
    var el = document.getElementById('w166lead');
    var html = cardHtml(S.lead);
    if (!body || !html) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w166lead'; el.className = 'mp-sec';
      var a = document.getElementById('w112wrap') || body.querySelector('.mp-online') || body.querySelector('.mp-chips') || body.querySelector('.mp-head');
      if (a && a.parentNode === body) body.insertBefore(el, a.nextSibling); else body.insertBefore(el, body.firstChild);
    }
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }
  function refresh(force) { return load(force).then(function () { paint(); }); }
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) {
    var d = e.target; if (!d || !d.getAttribute || !d.getAttribute('data-w166plan')) return;
    S.open[d.getAttribute('data-w166plan')] = d.open;
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
    var c = e.target; if (!c || !c.getAttribute || !c.getAttribute('data-w166tick')) return;
    var id = Number(c.getAttribute('data-w166tick')); S.busy['t' + id] = true; c.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('onboarding_lead_tick', { p_step: id, p_done: !!c.checked });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      T(c.checked ? 'Done. Thank you.' : 'Marked not done');
    } catch (err) { c.checked = !c.checked; T('Could not save that: ' + String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 80)); }
    delete S.busy['t' + id];
    await refresh(true);
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w166resolve]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); b.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('onboarding_help_resolve', { p_id: Number(b.getAttribute('data-w166resolve')) });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      T('Marked as dealt with');
    } catch (err) { b.disabled = false; T('Could not save that'); return; }
    await refresh(true);
  }, true);

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  /* one pass after the page changes (the body observer above calls it, debounced): the Watch here buttons, the plan sheet's

  var celSeen = false;
  function afterDom() {
    try { upgrade(document); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (document.querySelector('#w122sheet .onbwrap:not([data-w166])')) decoratePlan(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

      if (cel && !celSeen) { celSeen = true; afterComplete(); } else if (!cel) celSeen = false;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* a module or an avenue was finished: the database has ticked the plan's steps that go with it (trg_wa166_tp_onb) */
  var acQ = null;
  function afterComplete() {
    if (acQ) return acQ;
    acQ = (async function () {
      try { if (typeof wa37Load === 'function') await wa37Load(true); } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
      try { var st = window.w122 && window.w122.state ? window.w122.state() : null; if (st && st.R && st.R.mode === 'onb') replan(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (window.w122 && typeof window.w122.paint === 'function') window.w122.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (document.getElementById('w166lead')) await refresh(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      acQ = null;
    })();
    return acQ;
  }

  /* Home: the leads' card after every render of Home (cached for a minute; ticks and help refresh it at once) */
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm166 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm166.apply(this, arguments);
      try { paint(); refresh(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  try {
    if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && db.auth) db.auth.onAuthStateChange(function (evt) {
      if (evt === 'SIGNED_OUT') { S.lead = null; S.at = 0; S.open = {}; var el = document.getElementById('w166lead'); if (el) el.remove(); }
    });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- the tours: a stop for the leads' card on Home; the Academy's onboarding stop says how Academy steps work ---------- */
  function addTour() {
    try {
      var TT = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!TT) return;
      var hs = TT.home && TT.home.steps;
      if (hs && !hs.some(function (x) { return x.s === '#w166lead'; })) {

        hs.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : Math.min(2, hs.length), 0, { s: '#w166lead', t: 'Your people\u2019s onboarding',
          p: 'Leads see each person they are onboarding: how far they are, what is late, their help requests, and the moments that are yours (introduce them, first tasks, the two-week check-in, the quizzes, the 30-day evaluation). Tick yours here; See their plan opens the whole plan.', opt: 1 });
      }
      var as = TT.academy && TT.academy.steps;
      var st = as ? as.filter(function (x) { return x.s === '#w122 .w122onb'; })[0] : null;
      if (st && String(st.p).indexOf('journey') < 0) st.p = 'Your onboarding plan, the Hub first steps and the policies to sign, in one place at the top. The plan opens as a journey, week by week; steps marked Avenue Academy tick themselves when you finish the module or the avenue, and the videos play inside the Hub.';
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addTour(); setTimeout(addTour, 1500);

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w166 = {
    state: function () { return S; }, load: load, paint: paint, refresh: refresh, upgrade: upgrade, checklist: checklist,
    planStates: planStates, decoratePlan: decoratePlan, syncHead: syncHead, afterComplete: afterComplete,
    pathState: function (who, key) { var cx = ctxFor(who); return cx ? pathState(cx, key) : null; }
  };
})();

   WA v167 The onboarding journey (29/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (29/09/26): "Make the onboarding interactive with our branding highlighted everywhere. Use photos and make it the
   best most educational onboarding possible."

   The plan in the Avenue Academy (#training/onboarding, the sheet wave122 draws and wave166 fills) becomes a journey:
   - a welcome with a real photo, the logo and a doodle, the person's first name, their day, how far they are (a ring) and
     Continue (the next step: the week opens, the step shows, folded or not);
   - the avenue: the weeks as stops on a road (photo, title, how far), the E that moves standing at the week they are in;
     choosing a stop shows that week;
   - each week: its photo and due date, what you will learn and why it matters (app_config onboarding_journey, with the same
     values here as a fallback), the lesson that opens it (an Academy lesson with a knowledge check; its step ticks itself),
     then the week's steps (wave166's plan, one week at a time);
   - your people (your lead from the org chart, your team, our co-executive directors, with their photos), Welfare Avenue in
     numbers, a moment from our story (Did you know?), and Ask for help;
   - a celebration when a week is finished (and when the whole plan is).
   The lessons' photos: a 'photo' and a 'photos' block in wave122's lessons (122). Data: wa167_journey.
   ===================================================================== */

  function daysOf(name) {
    var u = String(name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), n = parseInt(u.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''), 10);
    if (/^WEEK\s+\d+$/.test(u)) return 7 * n;
    if (/^\d+\s+DAYS?$/.test(u)) return n;
    if (/^\d+\s+MONTHS?$/.test(u)) return 30 * n;
    return 9999;
  }
  function lab(name) {
    var u = String(name || '').trim(), m = /^WEEK\s+(\d+)$/i.exec(u);
    if (m) return 'Week ' + m[1];
    m = /^(\d+)\s+(DAYS?|MONTHS?)$/i.exec(u); if (m) return m[1] + ' ' + m[2].toLowerCase();
    return u.charAt(0) + u.slice(1).toLowerCase();

  function fmtDay(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: 'UTC', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); } }
