  /* ---------- what the journey reads: its settings, the people around the person, and a moment from our story ---------- */
  function loadCfg() {
    if (S.cfgAt && Date.now() - S.cfgAt < 600000) return Promise.resolve(S.cfg);
    S.cfgAt = Date.now();
    return Promise.resolve(db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'onboarding_journey')).then(function (r) {
      var v = r && r.data && r.data[0] && r.data[0].value;
      if (v) { try { S.cfg = typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : v; } catch (e) { /* the fallback stays */ } }
      return S.cfg;
    }).catch(function () { return S.cfg; });
  }
  function loadPeople() {
    if (S.pplAt && Date.now() - S.pplAt < 300000) return Promise.resolve(S.ppl);
    S.pplAt = Date.now();
    return Promise.all([
      db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,department,role,avatar_url').eq('active', true),
      db.from('org_positions').select('id,member_id,title,department,reports_to')
    ]).then(function (r) {
      S.ppl = { members: (r[0] && r[0].data) || [], seats: (r[1] && r[1].data) || [] };
      return S.ppl;
    }).catch(function () { return S.ppl; });
  }
  function loadStory() {
    if (S.story) return Promise.resolve(S.story);
    return Promise.resolve(db.from('wa_history').select('id,date_start,title,summary,highlight,visibility,status').eq('highlight', true)).then(function (r) {
      var rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x.title && x.summary && x.status !== 'hidden' && (!x.visibility || x.visibility === 'everyone'); });
      rows.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.date_start).localeCompare(String(b.date_start)); });
      S.story = rows; S.storyI = rows.length ? Math.floor(Math.random() * rows.length) : 0;
      return rows;
    }).catch(function () { S.story = []; return S.story; });
  }

  /* the person's own plan: the one the Academy's sheet shows */
  function myName() {
    try { var m = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null; if (m && m.name) return m.name; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { var full = String((USER && USER.name) || ''), em = String((USER && USER.email) || ''); return full ? full.split(' ')[0] : em.split('@')[0]; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function planOnb() {
    try {
      if (window.__w169view) return window.__w169view;   /* wave169: HR previews someone's journey, or a new person's */
      if (typeof WA37 === 'undefined' || !WA37.onbs) return null;
      var n = myName().toLowerCase();
      return WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status !== 'cancelled' && String(o.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === n; })[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  function seatOf(P, memberId) { return (P.seats || []).filter(function (s) { return String(s.member_id) === String(memberId); }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.id - b.id; })[0] || null; }
  function memberOf(P, id) { return (P.members || []).filter(function (m) { return String(m.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  /* the people around them: their lead (the org chart), their team, our co-executive directors */
  function peopleFor(o) {
    var P = S.ppl; if (!P || !o) return null;
    var me = memberOf(P, o.member_id) || (P.members || []).filter(function (m) { return String(m.name).toLowerCase() === String(o.person_name || '').toLowerCase(); })[0] || null;
    var seat = me ? seatOf(P, me.id) : null, lead = null, leadSeat = null;
    if (seat && seat.reports_to) { leadSeat = (P.seats || []).filter(function (s) { return String(s.id) === String(seat.reports_to); })[0] || null; if (leadSeat && leadSeat.member_id) lead = memberOf(P, leadSeat.member_id); }
    var dept = (me && me.department) || null;
    var team = (P.members || []).filter(function (m) { return dept && m.department === dept && (!me || m.id !== me.id) && (!lead || m.id !== lead.id); })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var eds = (P.seats || []).filter(function (s) { return /co-?ed\b|co-executive/i.test(String(s.title || '')) && s.member_id; }).map(function (s) { var m = memberOf(P, s.member_id); return m ? { m: m, title: s.title } : null; }).filter(Boolean)
      .filter(function (x) { return (!me || x.m.id !== me.id) && (!lead || x.m.id !== lead.id); });
    return { me: me, title: seat ? seat.title : null, dept: dept, lead: lead, leadTitle: leadSeat ? leadSeat.title : null, team: team, eds: eds };
  }

  /* the journey from the plan (wave166's planStates: each week's steps, with what the Academy already finished) */
  function journeyOf(o) {
    var W = window.w166; if (!W || typeof W.planStates !== 'function' || !o) return null;
    var weeks = W.planStates(o) || [], C = cfg(), total = 0, done = 0, next = null, nextWk = null;
    var byName = {};
    weeks.forEach(function (wk) {
      var d = 0, due = null;
      wk.steps.forEach(function (x) {
        total++; if (x.done) { done++; d++; }
        else { if (!next) { next = x; nextWk = wk.name; } if (x.s && x.s.due_date && (!due || String(x.s.due_date) < due)) due = String(x.s.due_date); }
      });
      var anyDue = null; wk.steps.forEach(function (x) { if (x.s && x.s.due_date && (!anyDue || String(x.s.due_date) > anyDue)) anyDue = String(x.s.due_date); });
      var meta = C.weeks[wk.name] || {};
      var lesson = meta.lesson ? wk.steps.filter(function (x) { return String(x.t.id) === String(meta.lesson); })[0] || null : null;
      byName[wk.name] = { name: wk.name, steps: wk.steps, done: d, total: wk.steps.length, complete: d >= wk.steps.length, due: due || anyDue, meta: meta, lesson: lesson };
    });
    var list = Object.keys(byName).sort(function (a, b) { return (daysOf(a) - daysOf(b)) || a.localeCompare(b); }).map(function (k) { return byName[k]; });   /* wave169: in time order, weeks HR adds included */
    return { o: o, weeks: list, byName: byName, total: total, done: done, pct: total ? Math.round(100 * done / total) : 0, next: next, nextWk: nextWk, day: dayN(o.started_at), complete: total > 0 && done >= total };
  }

  /* ---------- the journey's parts ---------- */
  function avHtml(m, cls) {
    var url = m && m.avatar_url ? String(m.avatar_url) : '';
    return '<span class="' + (cls || 'av') + '"' + (url ? ' style="background-image:url(\'' + E(url) + '\')"' : '') + '>' + (url ? '' : E(initials(m && m.name))) + '</span>';
  }
  function short(s, n) { s = String(s || ''); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '\u2026' : s; }
  function heroHtml(J) {
    var C = cfg(), h = C.hero || {}, o = J.o, pp = peopleFor(o);
    var nm = first(o.person_name) || 'friend';
    var who = pp && pp.title ? E(pp.title) + (pp.dept ? ', ' + E(teamName(pp.dept)) : '') + '. ' : (pp && pp.dept ? E(teamName(pp.dept)) + '. ' : '');
    var line = J.complete ? who + 'Every step of your plan is done. Welcome to the team, for good.'
      : who + 'Day ' + J.day + ' of your onboarding. Here is your road for the first 90 days, one stop at a time.';
    var go = '';
    if (J.next) {
      var t = J.next.t, lessonStep = /^wa167w/.test(String(t.id)) && t.training_id;
      go = lessonStep ? '<button type="button" class="w167btn" data-w166open="t:' + E(t.training_id) + '">Start here: ' + E(short(String(t.name).replace(/^Start here:\s*/i, ''), 40)) + '</button>'
        : '<button type="button" class="w167btn" data-w167go="next">Continue: ' + E(short(t.name, 40)) + '</button>';
    } else go = '<button type="button" class="w167btn" data-w166open="a:">Explore the Avenue Academy</button>';
    var lead = pp && pp.lead ? '<button type="button" class="w167btn ghost" data-w167person="' + E(pp.lead.id) + '">Your lead: ' + E(first(pp.lead.name)) + '</button>' : '';
    return '<section class="w167hero" style="background-image:url(\'' + E(photoUrl(h.photo)) + '\');background-position:' + bgPos(h) + '">'
      + '<img class="lg" src="' + BR.logo + '" alt="Welfare Avenue, building pathways in our community">'
      + '<img class="dd1" src="' + BR.star + '" alt="">'
      + '<div class="tx"><div class="ey">Your onboarding' + (J.complete ? ' &middot; complete' : ' &middot; day ' + J.day) + '</div>'
      + '<h1>Welcome, <span>' + E(nm) + '</span></h1>'
      + '<p>' + line + '</p>'
      + (h.tag ? '<div class="tag">' + E(h.tag) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="cta">' + go + lead + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="rg" title="' + J.done + ' of ' + J.total + ' steps done">' + ring(J.pct, 132, 12) + '<div class="in"><b>' + J.pct + '%</b><span>' + J.done + ' of ' + J.total + ' steps</span></div></div>'
      + '</section>';
  }
  function curWeek(J) { return J.nextWk || (J.weeks.length ? J.weeks[J.weeks.length - 1].name : null); }
  function roadHtml(J) {
    var cur = curWeek(J), n = J.weeks.length, ci = 0;
    J.weeks.forEach(function (w, i) { if (w.name === cur) ci = i; });
    var lane = n > 1 ? Math.round(100 * ci / (n - 1)) : 0;
    return '<nav class="w167road" aria-label="Your onboarding, week by week"><div class="lane"><i style="width:' + lane + '%"></i></div><div class="stops">'
      + J.weeks.map(function (w) {
        var m = w.meta || {}, pct = w.total ? Math.round(100 * w.done / w.total) : 0, lbl = lab(w.name);
        return '<button type="button" class="w167stop' + (w.complete ? ' done' : '') + (w.name === S.wk ? ' on' : '') + (w.name === cur && !J.complete ? ' cur' : '') + '" data-w167wk="' + E(w.name) + '" aria-pressed="' + (w.name === S.wk ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-label="' + E(lbl + ': ' + (m.title || '') + ', ' + w.done + ' of ' + w.total + ' done') + '">'
          + (w.name === cur && !J.complete ? '<img class="e" src="' + BR.e + '" alt="">' : '')
          + '<span class="pic" style="' + (m.photo ? 'background-image:url(\'' + E(photoUrl(m.photo)) + '\');background-position:' + bgPos(m) : '') + '"></span>'
          + '<span class="lb">' + E(lbl) + '</span><b>' + E(m.title || lbl) + '</b><span class="n">' + w.done + ' of ' + w.total + '</span>' + BAR(pct) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div><p class="hint">Choose a stop to see that week. You are here: the E that moves.</p></nav>';
  }
  function featHtml(w) {
    var x = w.lesson; if (!x || !x.t.training_id) return '';
    var ls = x.ls || {}, done = !!x.done, title = String(x.t.name).replace(/^Start here:\s*/i, ''), num = String(lab(w.name)).replace(/\D/g, '') || '&#9733;';
    return '<div class="w167feat' + (done ? ' ok' : '') + '"><div class="ic">' + (done ? '&#10003;' : num) + '</div><div class="tx"><span>' + (done ? 'Done: you passed its knowledge check' : 'Start here') + '</span><b>' + E(title) + '</b>'
      + '<em>' + (ls.mins ? ls.mins + '-minute lesson with photos' : 'A short lesson with photos') + ' &middot; a knowledge check at the end &middot; it ticks itself</em></div>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w167btn' + (done ? ' ghost' : '') + '" data-w166open="t:' + E(x.t.training_id) + '">' + (done ? 'Open it again' : 'Start the lesson') + '</button></div>';
  }
  function weekHeadHtml(J, w) {
    if (!w) return '';
    var m = w.meta || {}, lbl = lab(w.name), pct = w.total ? Math.round(100 * w.done / w.total) : 0;
    var k = lbl + (w.complete ? ' &middot; done' : (w.due ? ' &middot; due ' + E(fmtDay(w.due)) : ''));
    var li = function (s) { return '<li>' + E(s) + '</li>'; };
    return '<header class="w167ban" style="' + (m.photo ? 'background-image:url(\'' + E(photoUrl(m.photo)) + '\');background-position:' + bgPos(m) : '') + '"><img class="dd" src="' + BR.ast + '" alt="">'
      + '<div><div class="k">' + k + '</div><h2>' + E(m.title || lbl) + '</h2><p>' + w.done + ' of ' + w.total + ' steps done</p><div class="pb">' + BAR(pct) + '</div></div></header>'
      + ((m.learn && m.learn.length) || m.why ? '<div class="w167learn">' + (m.learn && m.learn.length ? '<div class="ob"><h3>By the end of this week you will</h3><ul>' + m.learn.map(li).join('') + '</ul></div>' : '')
        + (m.why ? '<div class="wy"><img src="' + BR.heart + '" alt=""><h3>Why it matters</h3><p>' + E(m.why) + '</p></div>' : '') + '</div>' : '')
      + featHtml(w)
      /* wave191p: Week 3, choose your servings (four a year; filled by wave191's onboarding module) */
      + (w.name === 'WEEK 3' ? '<div class="w191onbw"><h3><img src="' + BR.star + '" alt="">Choose your servings</h3><p>Every member volunteers four times a year. Pick your first ones: they are confirmed right away, and the operations lead hears from you.</p><div class="w191onb" data-w191onbat="journey"></div></div>' : '');
  }
  function pplHtml(J) {
    var pp = peopleFor(J.o);
    var h = '<h3><img src="' + BR.flowers + '" alt="">Your people</h3>';
    if (!pp) return h + '<div class="w167ppl"><div class="w167pc" style="cursor:default"><span class="av">&hellip;</span><div class="t"><b>Finding your people</b></div></div></div>';
    var cards = [];
    if (pp.lead) cards.push('<button type="button" class="w167pc" data-w167person="' + E(pp.lead.id) + '">' + avHtml(pp.lead) + '<div class="t"><em>Your lead</em><b>' + E(pp.lead.name) + '</b><span>' + E(pp.leadTitle || teamName(pp.lead.department)) + '. Checks in with you at two weeks and around day 30.</span></div></button>');
    pp.eds.forEach(function (x) { cards.push('<button type="button" class="w167pc" data-w167person="' + E(x.m.id) + '">' + avHtml(x.m) + '<div class="t"><em>Co-executive director</em><b>' + E(x.m.name) + '</b><span>Book 15 minutes with one of them in your first 60 days.</span></div></button>'); });
    var team = pp.team.slice(0, 12);
    return h + (cards.length ? '<div class="w167ppl">' + cards.join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + (team.length ? '<div class="w167team" aria-label="Your team">' + team.map(function (m) { return '<button type="button" data-w167person="' + E(m.id) + '" title="' + E(m.name + (m.role ? ', ' + m.role : '')) + '">' + avHtml(m) + '<span>' + E(first(m.name)) + '</span></button>'; }).join('')
        + (pp.team.length > team.length ? '<span style="align-self:center;font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + '">and ' + (pp.team.length - team.length) + ' more</span>' : '') + '</div>'
        + '<p style="margin:8px 0 0;font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + '">Your ' + E(teamName(pp.dept)) + ' team. Say hello in your team&rsquo;s channel; the org chart shows everyone else.</p>' : '');
  }
  function numHtml() {
    var C = cfg(); if (!C.numbers || !C.numbers.length) return '';
    return '<h3><img src="' + BR.star + '" alt="">Welfare Avenue in numbers</h3><div class="w167num"><img class="dd1" src="' + BR.blob + '" alt=""><img class="dd2" src="' + BR.arrow + '" alt="">'
      + '<div class="g">' + C.numbers.map(function (x) { return '<div><b>' + E(x.n) + '</b><span>' + E(x.l) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (C.numbers_note ? '<p class="nt">' + E(C.numbers_note) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function dykHtml() {
    var rows = S.story || []; if (!rows.length) return '';
    var x = rows[S.storyI % rows.length];
    return '<h3><img src="' + BR.heart + '" alt="">Did you know?</h3><div class="w167dyk"><div class="yr">' + E(String(x.date_start || '').slice(0, 4)) + '</div><div class="tx"><b>' + E(x.title) + '</b><p>' + E(short(x.summary, 320)) + '</p>'
      + '<div class="ac"><button type="button" class="w167btn green" data-w167dyk="1">Another moment</button><button type="button" class="w167lnk" data-w167story="1">All of our story</button></div></div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- building the journey around wave166's plan, in place, before the page paints ---------- */
  function part(box, key, html) {
    var el = box.querySelector('[data-w167p="' + key + '"]'); if (!el) return;
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }
  function paintParts(sh, J) {
    var box = sh.querySelector('.w167box'); if (!box) return;
    part(box, 'hero', heroHtml(J));
    part(box, 'road', roadHtml(J));
    part(box, 'wh', weekHeadHtml(J, J.byName[S.wk]));
    part(box, 'sh', (J.byName[S.wk] && J.byName[S.wk].lesson ? 'Then, this week' : 'This week') + '<span style="font-family:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-left:10px">Tick each step as you go; steps marked Avenue Academy tick themselves.</span>');
    part(box, 'ppl', pplHtml(J));
    part(box, 'num', numHtml());
    part(box, 'dyk', dykHtml());
  }
  function weekNameOf(d) { var s = d.querySelector('summary'); if (!s) return ''; var t = s.firstChild; return String(t && t.nodeType === 3 ? t.textContent : s.textContent).trim(); }
  function applyWeek(sh) {
    var wrap = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap'); if (!wrap) return;
    [].forEach.call(wrap.querySelectorAll('details.w37week'), function (d) {
      var on = weekNameOf(d) === S.wk;
      if (d.classList.contains('w167on') !== on) d.classList.toggle('w167on', on);
      if (on && !d.open) d.open = true;
    });
  }
  function build(sh, wrap) {
    var W = window.w166; if (!W || typeof W.planStates !== 'function') return;
    try { if (typeof W.decoratePlan === 'function') W.decoratePlan(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var o = planOnb(); if (!o) return;
    var J = journeyOf(o); if (!J || !J.weeks.length) return;
    var main = wrap.closest('.wrap'); if (!main) return;
    wrap.setAttribute('data-w167', '1');
    sh.classList.add('w167');
    main.classList.add('wide'); main.classList.add('w167j');
    if (S.onb !== o.id) { S.onb = o.id; S.wk = null; }
    if (!S.wk || !J.byName[S.wk]) S.wk = curWeek(J);
    var box = document.createElement('div'); box.className = 'w167box';
    box.innerHTML = '<div data-w167p="hero"></div><div data-w167p="road"></div>'
      + '<section class="w167wk"><div class="w167wh" data-w167p="wh"></div><div class="w167steps"><h3 data-w167p="sh"></h3></div></section>'
      + '<section class="w167sec" data-w167p="ppl"></section><section class="w167sec" data-w167p="num"></section><section class="w167sec" data-w167p="dyk"></section>';
    main.insertBefore(box, main.firstChild);
    var steps = box.querySelector('.w167steps'); steps.appendChild(wrap);
    var help = sh.querySelector('#w166help'); if (help) steps.appendChild(help);
    paintParts(sh, J);
    applyWeek(sh);
    try { if (typeof W.syncHead === 'function') W.syncHead(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    /* after a tick the Academy draws the sheet again: keep the place */
    var m = sh.querySelector('.main');
    if (m && S.restore && Date.now() < S.restore.until && S.restore.onb === o.id) m.scrollTop = S.restore.top;
    S.restore = null;
    celebrateCheck(J);
    Promise.all([loadCfg(), loadPeople(), loadStory()]).then(function () { refresh(); });
  }
  function refresh() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh.w167'); if (!sh || !sh.querySelector('.w167box')) return;
    var o = planOnb(); if (!o) return;
    var J = journeyOf(o); if (!J) return;
    if (!J.byName[S.wk]) S.wk = curWeek(J);
    paintParts(sh, J); applyWeek(sh); celebrateCheck(J);
  }
  function sync() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh'); if (!sh) return;
    var wrap = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap');
    if (!wrap) { if (sh.classList.contains('w167')) sh.classList.remove('w167'); return; }
    if (!wrap.getAttribute('data-w167')) { build(sh, wrap); return; }
    applyWeek(sh);
  }
  function select(wk, scroll) {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh.w167'); if (!sh) return;
    S.wk = wk; refresh();
    if (scroll) { var sec = sh.querySelector('.w167wk'); if (sec) try { sec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* Continue: open the week of the next step and show the step (unfolding it when it sits under a bigger step) */
  function continueNext() {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh.w167'); if (!sh) return;
    var o = planOnb(), J = o ? journeyOf(o) : null; if (!J || !J.next) return;
    S.wk = J.nextWk; refresh();
    var tid = String(J.next.t.id), row = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap [data-w166t="' + tid.replace(/"/g, '') + '"]');
    if (row && row.style.display === 'none' && J.next.t.parent_id) {
      var tg = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap [data-w57t="' + String(J.next.t.parent_id).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]'); if (tg) tg.click();
    }
    if (!row) return;
    try { row.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    row.classList.remove('w167flash'); void row.offsetWidth; row.classList.add('w167flash');
  }

  /* ---------- a week finished, or the whole plan ---------- */
  function celebrateCheck(J) {
    if (window.__w169view) return;   /* wave169: nothing is celebrated in a preview */
    var key = String(J.o.id), prev = S.counts[key], now = { __all: J.complete };
    J.weeks.forEach(function (w) { now[w.name] = w.complete; });
    S.counts[key] = now;
    if (!prev) return;
    if (J.complete && !prev.__all) { celebrate('all', J); return; }
    var w = J.weeks.filter(function (x) { return x.complete && prev[x.name] === false; })[0];
    if (w) celebrate(w.name, J);
  }
  function celebrate(kind, J) {
    var sh = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .sh.w167'); if (!sh || sh.querySelector('.w167cel')) return;
    var mark = 'w167cel:' + J.o.id + ':' + kind; if (lsGet(mark)) return; lsSet(mark, '1');
    var idx = -1; J.weeks.forEach(function (w, i) { if (w.name === kind) idx = i; });
    var w = J.byName[kind], nxt = idx >= 0 ? J.weeks.slice(idx + 1).filter(function (x) { return !x.complete; })[0] : null;
    var title = kind === 'all' ? 'You are onboarded!' : lab(kind) + ' done!';
    var text = kind === 'all' ? 'Every step of your plan is done. Thank you for the care you put into it; your lead and HR can see it too.'
      : 'Every step of ' + ((w && w.meta && w.meta.title) || lab(kind)) + ' is done.' + (nxt ? ' Next stop: ' + ((nxt.meta && nxt.meta.title) || lab(nxt.name)) + '.' : '');
    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'w167cel'; el.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); el.setAttribute('aria-label', title);
    el.innerHTML = '<canvas></canvas><div class="cd"><img class="fl" src="' + BR.flowers + '" alt=""><img class="e" src="' + BR.e + '" alt=""><h3>' + E(title) + '</h3><p>' + E(text) + '</p><div class="bt">'
      + (nxt ? '<button type="button" class="w167btn" data-w167wk="' + E(nxt.name) + '" data-w167celx="1">Next stop: ' + E((nxt.meta && nxt.meta.title) || lab(nxt.name)) + '</button>' : '')
      + '<button type="button" class="w167btn green" data-w167celx="1">' + (nxt ? 'Stay here' : 'Keep going') + '</button></div></div>';
    sh.appendChild(el);
    confetti(el.querySelector('canvas'));
  }
  function confetti(cv) {
    if (!cv || reduced()) return;
    var ctx = cv.getContext && cv.getContext('2d'); if (!ctx) return;
    var W0 = cv.width = cv.offsetWidth * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1), H0 = cv.height = cv.offsetHeight * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    var cols = [GOLD, PURPLE, CREAM, TEAL, '#B3A8F5'], ps = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < 140; i++) ps.push({ x: W0 / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * W0 * 0.3, y: H0 * 0.42, vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * W0 * 0.018, vy: -Math.random() * H0 * 0.022 - H0 * 0.006, r: 4 + Math.random() * 7, c: cols[i % cols.length], a: Math.random() * 6, va: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3, round: Math.random() < 0.35 });
    var t0 = Date.now();
    (function tick() {
      var t = Date.now() - t0; if (t > 3200 || !cv.isConnected) { try { ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W0, H0); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W0, H0);
      ps.forEach(function (p) {
        p.vy += H0 * 0.0006; p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.a += p.va;
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, 1 - t / 3200); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(p.a); ctx.fillStyle = p.c;
        if (p.round) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, p.r * 0.6, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill(); } else ctx.fillRect(-p.r / 2, -p.r / 4, p.r, p.r / 2);
        ctx.restore();
      });
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    })();
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target : null; if (!t) return;
    var b;
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167celx]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var cel = b.closest('.w167cel'); var wk = b.getAttribute('data-w167wk');
      if (cel) cel.remove();
      if (wk) select(wk, true);
      return;
    }
    if (t.closest('.w167cel') && !t.closest('.cd')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); t.closest('.w167cel').remove(); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167wk]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); select(b.getAttribute('data-w167wk'), true); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167go]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); continueNext(); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167person]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); location.hash = '#person/' + b.getAttribute('data-w167person'); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167dyk]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); S.storyI = (S.storyI + 1) % Math.max(1, (S.story || []).length); refresh(); return; }
    if ((b = t.closest('[data-w167story]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); location.hash = '#ourstory'; return; }
  }, true);
  /* Escape closes a celebration first (the Academy closes the sheet on Escape) */
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
    var cel = document.querySelector('#w122sheet .w167cel'); if (!cel) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); cel.remove();
  }, true);
  /* a tick in the plan: the Academy draws the sheet again after it; remember where the person was */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var c = e.target; if (!c || !c.getAttribute || !c.getAttribute('data-w37chk')) return;
    var sh = c.closest && c.closest('#w122sheet .sh.w167'); if (!sh) return;
    var m = sh.querySelector('.main'); S.restore = { top: m ? m.scrollTop : 0, until: Date.now() + 5000, onb: S.onb };
  }, true);
  /* wave166 draws the plan again in place after the Academy ticked steps: the journey follows */
  document.addEventListener('w166replan', function () { try { refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  function watchSheet() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w122sheet'); if (!el) return false;
    if (el.__w167) return true;
    el.__w167 = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { try { sync(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }).observe(el, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    sync();
    return true;
  }
  if (!watchSheet()) {
    try { var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (watchSheet()) mo.disconnect(); }); mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  loadCfg();

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  /* wave169: HR changed what people see for a week in the master onboarding: read the settings again and redraw */
  function reloadCfg(v) {
    if (v && typeof v === 'object') { S.cfg = v; S.cfgAt = Date.now(); refresh(); return Promise.resolve(S.cfg); }
    S.cfgAt = 0; return loadCfg().then(function () { refresh(); return S.cfg; });
  }
  window.w167 = { state: function () { return S; }, sync: sync, refresh: refresh, select: select, journeyOf: journeyOf, planOnb: planOnb, celebrate: celebrate, cfg: cfg, peopleFor: peopleFor, continueNext: continueNext, reloadCfg: reloadCfg };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v169 The master onboarding runs the Academy, and trainings are assigned to the organization (29/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (29/09/26): "the master onboarding in the hr center that controls the onboarding. I want to make that's synced to
   what people see on their Avenue Academy page. Also, I want HR to have the ability to assign trainings to the org through
   that same page in the hr center. Leaders should have that ability on the Avenue Academy page as well".

   What changed where:
   - The master onboarding (HR centre, #hrcentre/onboarding; 037, 044, 046, 047, 057 draw it): each week says what people
     see for it in the Academy (the journey's stop: its title, photo, what they will learn, why it matters and the lesson that
     opens it) and edits it in place (onboarding_journey_save into app_config.onboarding_journey, which the journey reads);
     each step says when it is done in the Avenue Academy (a module or a Learning Avenue: training_id, academy_path) and the
     step's editor sets it; "Preview what a new person sees" opens the journey as a new person sees it on day one, and each
     person on the board has "Journey", their own journey as they see it (read only).
   - The database (wa169_sync_assign): a step moved to another week takes that week's due date in every plan in progress; a
     step newly linked to the Academy ticks itself for those who already did it; the Learning Avenue Your onboarding is the
     master's own Academy modules in the master's order; weeks HR adds (90 DAYS, 6 MONTHS) get their own due dates.
   - Assigning trainings (academy_assignments, academy_assign): a module or a Learning Avenue, to everyone (the whole team,
