  function counts(roster, evId) {
    var list = (roster || []).filter(function (r) { return !isOff(r); });
    var c = { list: list.length, conf: 0, bytext: 0, decl: 0, texted: 0, waiting: 0, nophone: 0 };
    list.forEach(function (r) {
      if (isConf(r)) { c.conf++; if (r.confirm_status === 'confirmed') c.bytext++; }
      else if (isDecl(r)) c.decl++;
      else {
        c.waiting++;
        var t = evId ? lastText(evId, r.id) : null;
        if (t && t.status === 'sent') c.texted++;
        if (!phoneOf(r)) c.nophone++;
      }
    });
    return c;
  }
  /* the date the automatic confirmation goes (7 days before, 10 am) */
  function autoDay(ev) {
    var days = Number(S.cfg.sms_confirm_days || 7), hour = Number(S.cfg.sms_confirm_hour || 10);
    return { day: addDays(ev.event_date, -days), hour: hour };
  }

  /* ---------- the event page ---------- */
  function statusTag(r, evId) {
    if (isOff(r)) return '';
    var c = r.confirm || {};
    if (isConf(r)) {
      if (r.confirm_status === 'confirmed') return '<span class="w171tag ok">\u2713 ' + (c.via === 'reply' ? 'Replied YES' : c.via === 'staff' ? 'Said yes in a text' : 'Confirmed by text link') + (r.confirmed_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtWhen(r.confirmed_at)) : '') + '</span>';
      return '';
    }
    if (isDecl(r)) {
      if (r.confirm_status === 'declined') return '<span class="w171tag no">Can\u2019t make it ' + (c.via === 'reply' ? '(replied NO)' : c.via === 'staff' ? '(said so in a text)' : '(text link)') + (r.confirmed_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtWhen(r.confirmed_at)) : '') + '</span>';
      return '';
    }
    var t = lastText(evId, r.id);
    if (!t && r.signup_noticed_at) return '<span class="w171tag mute">Told they are signed up \u00b7 ' + E(fmtWhen(r.signup_noticed_at)) + '</span>';
    if (t && t.status === 'sent') return '<span class="w171tag wait">Texted ' + E(fmtWhen(t.sent_at || t.created_at)) + ' \u00b7 no answer yet</span>';
    if (t && t.status === 'queued') return '<span class="w171tag wait">Text queued</span>';
    if (t && t.status === 'failed') return '<span class="w171tag no" title="' + E(t.error || '') + '">The text did not go through</span>';
    if (!phoneOf(r)) return '<span class="w171tag mute">No phone number</span>';
    return '';
  }
  /* wave172: what the volunteer told us on their page (the recipe, donations, the car, how photos come back) and the survey */
  var SHARE = { album: 'the Google Photos album', drive: 'the Drive folder', email: 'email' };
  function recipeName(id) { var x = (S.recipes || []).filter(function (r) { return r.id === id; })[0]; return x ? x.name : 'recipe ' + id; }
  function detailTags(r, evId) {
    if (isOff(r) || isDecl(r)) return '';
    var c = r.confirm || {}, out = [];
    if (r.recipe_id && lower(r.role) === 'cook') out.push('Cooking: ' + recipeName(r.recipe_id));
    if (lower(r.role) === 'cook' && c.cook_location === 'home' && c.neighbourhood) out.push('Pickup: ' + c.neighbourhood + (c.address ? ' \u00b7 ' + c.address : ''));
    if (lower(r.role) === 'cook' && c.cook_location === 'drop off') out.push('Drops off' + (c.stay_to_serve === true ? ' \u00b7 staying to serve' : c.stay_to_serve === false ? ' \u00b7 not staying' : ''));
    if (c.donations) out.push('Bringing to donate: ' + c.donations);
    if (c.vehicle || c.model) out.push('Car: ' + [c.vehicle, c.model].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
    if (c.photos_share) out.push('Photos by ' + (SHARE[c.photos_share] || c.photos_share));
    if (lower(r.role) === 'community_debrief') out.push(c.briefed_at ? 'Briefing read ' + fmtWhen(c.briefed_at) : 'Briefing not read yet');   /* wave235 */
    var sv = (S.msgs[evId] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.roster_id === r.id && x.kind === 'survey' && x.direction === 'out' && x.status === 'sent'; })[0];
    if (sv) out.push('Survey texted ' + fmtWhen(sv.sent_at || sv.created_at));
    var iv = (S.msgs[evId] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.roster_id === r.id && x.kind === 'itinerary' && x.direction === 'out' && x.status === 'sent'; })[0];
    if (iv) out.push('Itinerary texted ' + fmtWhen(iv.sent_at || iv.created_at));
    return out.map(function (x) { return '<span class="w171tag mute">' + E(x) + '</span>'; }).join('');
  }
  function tagFor(r, evId) { var D = curD(); return statusTag(r, evId) + detailTags(r, evId) + (D ? w174Tags(r, evId, driverKinds(D)) : '') + w175Tags(r); }
  function decorateCards() {
    var D = curD(); var ev = curEv(); if (!D || !ev) return;
    var by = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { by[String(r.id)] = r; });
    document.querySelectorAll('#w119 [data-w119card]').forEach(function (el) {
      var r = by[el.getAttribute('data-w119card')]; if (!r) return;
      var html = tagFor(r, ev.id);
      var olds = el.querySelectorAll('.w171tag');
      var cur = Array.prototype.map.call(olds, function (x) { return x.outerHTML; }).join('');
      if (cur === html) return;
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(olds, function (x) { x.remove(); });
      if (html) el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    });
  }
  function decorateMast() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); var meta = document.querySelector('#w119 .mast .meta'); if (!ev || !D || !meta) return;
    var c = counts(D.roster, ev.id);
    var fmt = ev.format ? FORMATS[ev.format] || ev.format : '';
    var up = ev.event_date >= todayIso() && ev.status !== 'cancelled';
    var html = (up || fmt ? '<span class="w171pill' + (fmt ? '' : ' gold') + '"' + (staff() ? ' data-w171fmt="1" title="Set the format"' : '') + '>' + E(fmt || 'Format not set') + '</span>' : '')
      + (c.list ? '<span class="w171pill">' + c.conf + ' of ' + c.list + ' confirmed' + (c.bytext ? ' \u00b7 ' + c.bytext + ' by text' : '') + '</span>' : '');
    var box = meta.querySelector('.w171m1');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('span'); box.className = 'w171m1'; box.style.display = 'contents'; meta.appendChild(box); }
    if (box.__h !== html) { box.innerHTML = html; box.__h = html; }
  }
  function repHtml(m, D) {
    var r = m.roster_id ? (D.roster || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === m.roster_id; })[0] : null;
    var who = r ? first(r.person_name) + ' (' + one(r.role) + ')' : 'Someone not on this roster';
    var h = { confirmed: ['ok', 'Confirmed'], declined: ['no', 'Can\u2019t make it'], opted_out: ['no', 'Stopped texts'], opted_in: ['ok', 'Texts back on'], question: ['wait', 'Needs an answer'], answered: ['ok', 'Answered'], done: ['ok', 'No reply needed'], stored: ['mute', 'Kept (texts off)'], unmatched: ['mute', 'Unknown number'] }[m.handled] || ['mute', m.handled || ''];
    return '<div class="w171rep"><div class="h"><b>' + E(who) + '</b><span>' + E(fmtWhen(m.created_at)) + '</span><span class="w171tag ' + h[0] + '" style="display:inline-block;margin:0">' + E(h[1]) + '</span>' + (m.phone && typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff() ? '<button class="w171btn ghost" style="margin-left:auto;padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px" data-w191conv="' + E(m.phone) + '">' + (m.handled === 'question' ? 'Reply' : 'Open') + '</button>' : '') + '</div><p>' + E(m.body) + '</p></div>';
  }
  function cardHtml(ev, D, cn) {
    var c = counts(D.roster, ev.id);
    var mode = S.mode || 'off';
    var up = ev.event_date > todayIso() && ev.status !== 'cancelled' && ev.status !== 'completed';
    var ad = autoDay(ev);
    var note;
    if (!up) note = 'This serving is over, so no more confirmation texts go out.';
    else if (mode === 'off') note = 'Texts are off: nothing goes out until an admin switches them on in Text settings.';
    else if (mode === 'test') note = 'Test mode: only the test phone gets texts. Everyone else is held back.';
    else note = (ad.day > todayIso() ? 'Confirmation texts go automatically on ' + fmtDay(ad.day, true) + ' at ' + ad.hour + ' am' : 'Confirmation texts go automatically, 9 am to 7 pm') + ' to everyone not yet confirmed. Anyone added later gets theirs right away; someone added more than 7 days out first gets a text saying they are signed up, with a link to add it to their calendar.' + ' The day before, confirmed volunteers get their itinerary; after the serving, their survey' + (String(S.cfg.sms_survey_roles || '*').trim() === '*' ? '' : ' (' + String(S.cfg.sms_survey_roles).split(',').map(function (x) { return one(x.trim()).toLowerCase(); }).join(', ') + ')') + '.';
    var reps = replies(ev.id).slice(0, 6);
    return '<h4>Texts <span class="mode ' + E(mode) + '">' + E(MODES[mode] || mode) + '</span></h4>'
      + '<div class="w171nums"><div><b>' + c.conf + '</b><span>confirmed of ' + c.list + '</span></div><div><b>' + c.bytext + '</b><span>by text</span></div><div><b>' + c.waiting + '</b><span>waiting' + (c.texted ? ', ' + c.texted + ' texted' : '') + '</span></div><div><b>' + c.decl + '</b><span>can\u2019t make it</span></div></div>'
      + '<p class="w171note">' + E(note) + (c.nophone && up ? ' ' + c.nophone + ' on the list ' + (c.nophone === 1 ? 'has' : 'have') + ' no phone number.' : '') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="w171btns">'
      + (cn.text && up ? '<button class="w171btn" data-w171send="1"' + (mode === 'off' ? ' disabled title="Texts are off"' : '') + '>Text to confirm</button>' : '')
      + (cn.admin && up && (D.roster || []).length ? '<button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171test="1">Send me a test</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171set="1">Text settings</button></div>'
      + '<div class="w171h">Replies</div>' + (reps.length ? reps.map(function (m) { return repHtml(m, D); }).join('') : '<p class="w171note" style="margin:0">No texts back yet. YES and NO update the roster by themselves; anything else shows here for the team.</p>');
  }
  async function loadRecipes() {
    if (S.recipes) return S.recipes;
    try { var r = await db.from('recipes').select('id,name').order('sort'); S.recipes = (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { S.recipes = []; }
    return S.recipes;
  }
  async function paintCard() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); var rail = document.getElementById('w119rail');
    if (!ev || !D || !rail || !staff()) return;
    var cn = await can(); await loadCfg(false); await loadMsgs(ev.id, false); await loadRecipes(); await loadAccounts(false);
    if (curEv() !== ev) return;
    rail = document.getElementById('w119rail'); if (!rail) return;
    var box = document.getElementById('w171card');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w171card'; box.className = 'cardy w171card'; rail.insertBefore(box, rail.firstChild); }
    var html = cardHtml(ev, D, cn);
    if (box.__h !== html) { box.innerHTML = html; box.__h = html; }
    decorateCards(); decorateMast();
    paintW174();
  }

  /* ---------- Text to confirm ---------- */
  function sendModal() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D) return;
    var rows = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return !isOff(r) && !isConf(r) && !isDecl(r); });
    var html = '<div class="w171m"><h3>Text to confirm</h3><p class="sub">' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + ' \u00b7 each person gets their own link to confirm their spot, from the ops line.</p>';
    if (S.mode === 'test') html += '<div class="info">Test mode: only the test phone gets a text. The others are held back until texts are on.</div>';
    if (!rows.length) html += '<p>Everyone on this serving has answered. Nothing to send.</p>';
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var t = lastText(ev.id, r.id); var ph = phoneOf(r);
      var sent = t && (t.status === 'sent' || t.status === 'queued');
      html += '<label class="row"><input type="checkbox" data-w171pick="' + r.id + '"' + (ph && !sent ? ' checked' : '') + (ph ? '' : ' disabled') + '><span class="who">' + E(r.person_name || 'Unnamed') + '<small>' + E(one(r.role)) + ' \u00b7 ' + (ph ? (sent ? 'texted ' + E(fmtWhen(t.sent_at || t.created_at)) + ' (not sent twice)' : 'not texted yet') : 'no phone number') + '</small></span></label>';
    });
    if (rows.length) html += '<div class="prev">Hi <first name>, it\u2019s Welfare Avenue! You\u2019re on the list as a <role> for our serving on ' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + '. Please confirm here: hub.welfareavenue.com/c/\u2026 (or reply YES or NO). Reply STOP to stop texts.</div><p class="w171note">Someone whose contact says French gets it in French. Anyone already texted for this serving is not texted again.</p>';
    html += '<div class="foot"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171close="1">Cancel</button>' + (rows.length ? '<button class="w171btn" data-w171go="1">Send the texts</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    openModal(html);
  }
  async function sendGo(btn) {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev || S.busy) return;
    var ids = Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w171pick]:checked'), function (c) { return Number(c.getAttribute('data-w171pick')); });
    if (!ids.length) { T('Tick at least one person'); return; }
    S.busy = true; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa171_text_to_confirm', { p_event: ev.id, p_roster: ids });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r.data || {};
      var bits = [];
      if (d.queued) bits.push(d.queued + ' text' + (d.queued === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' on the way');
      if (d.held_by_test_mode) bits.push(d.held_by_test_mode + ' held back by test mode');
      if (d.already_texted) bits.push(d.already_texted + ' already texted');
      if (d.no_phone) bits.push(d.no_phone + ' with no phone');
      if (d.opted_out) bits.push(d.opted_out + ' stopped texts');
      if (d.reason) bits.push(d.reason);
      T(bits.join(', ') || 'Nothing to send');
      closeModal();
      setTimeout(function () { loadMsgs(ev.id, true).then(paintCard); }, 2500);
    } catch (e) { T('Could not send: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Send the texts'; }
    S.busy = false;
  }
  /* ---------- Send me a test: as any role, as anyone on the list ----------
     wave191k (Steel, 01/10/26: "test mode only lets me test with volunteer cooks"): the button used to send the first
     person on the list, and the first person on every serving is a cook. Now it opens a picker: the roles on this serving
     (with how many), the person, their text, and their page (?test=1, saves nothing). After a send, the exact text that went
     out shows in place and the role is ticked, so the next role can be tried straight away. Admins (quo-send test_confirm). */
  function testRoles() {
    var D = curD(); var by = {};
    ((D && D.roster) || []).filter(function (x) { return !isOff(x); }).forEach(function (r) { var k = lower(r.role) || 'volunteer'; (by[k] = by[k] || []).push(r); });
    var order = ROLES.filter(function (k) { return by[k]; }).concat(Object.keys(by).filter(function (k) { return ROLES.indexOf(k) < 0; }));
    return { by: by, order: order };
  }
  function placeWord(r) { return isConf(r) ? 'confirmed' : isDecl(r) ? 'can\u2019t make it' : 'waiting'; }
  function testModal() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D) return;
    var t = testRoles(); if (!t.order.length) { T('No one is on this serving yet'); return; }
    S.tested = S.tested || {};
    /* the last role tried, else servers (most of the list), else the first role on it */
    var role = S.testRole && t.by[S.testRole] ? S.testRole : t.by.server ? 'server' : t.order[0]; S.testRole = role;
    var people = t.by[role];
    var r = people.filter(function (x) { return x.id === S.testRid; })[0] || people[0]; S.testRid = r.id;
    var phone = String(S.cfg.sms_test_phones || '').split(',')[0].replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10);
    var sent = S.testSent && S.testSent.rid === r.id ? S.testSent : null;
    var art = /^[aeiou]/.test(one(role).toLowerCase()) ? 'an ' : 'a ';
    var text = sent && sent.text ? sent.text : 'Hi ' + (first(r.person_name) || 'there') + ', it\u2019s Welfare Avenue! You\u2019re on the list as ' + art + one(role).toLowerCase() + ' for our serving on ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)
      + '. Please confirm here: hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + String(r.confirm_token || '').slice(0, 6) + '\u2026?test=1 (or reply YES or NO). Reply STOP to stop texts.';
    var html = '<div class="w171m w171tm"><h3>Send me a test</h3><p class="sub">' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + ' \u00b7 pick whose confirmation text to try. It goes only to the test phone'
      + (phone.length === 10 ? ' (' + phone.slice(0, 3) + ') ' + phone.slice(3, 6) + '-' + phone.slice(6) : '') + '; its link opens their page as they would see it and saves nothing.</p>'
      + (phone ? '' : '<div class="warn">No test phone is set. Add one in Text settings first.</div>')
      + '<label class="f">As</label><div class="roles" role="radiogroup" aria-label="The role to test">' + t.order.map(function (k) {
        var n = t.by[k].length;
        return '<label class="' + (k === role ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w171trole" value="' + E(k) + '"' + (k === role ? ' checked' : '') + '><b>' + E(one(k)) + '</b><small>' + n + ' on the list</small>' + (S.tested[k] ? '<i title="Sent to the test phone this session">\u2713 sent</i>' : '') + '</label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<label class="f" for="w171tp2">Person</label><select id="w171tp2">' + people.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (x.id === r.id ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E((x.person_name || 'Unnamed') + ' \u00b7 ' + placeWord(x)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<div class="sms">' + E(text) + '</div>'
      + (sent ? '<p class="sent' + (sent.ok ? '' : ' no') + '">' + (sent.ok ? '\u2713 Sent to the test phone ' + E(fmtWhen(sent.at)) + '. Pick another role to try the next one.' : 'The test did not go through: ' + E(String(sent.err || '').slice(0, 120))) + '</p>'
        : '<p class="w171note" style="margin:2px 0 0">' + (lower(S.cfg.sms_mode || S.mode) === 'live' ? 'Texts are on, but a test still goes only to the test phone.' : 'This works in any mode: a test only ever goes to the test phone.') + '</p>')
      + '<div class="foot"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171close="1">Done</button>'
      + (r.confirm_token ? '<a class="w171btn ghost" style="text-decoration:none" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="https://hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + E(r.confirm_token) + '?test=1">Open their page</a>' : '')
      + '<button class="w171btn" data-w171tgo="1"' + (phone && !S.busy ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Sending\u2026' : sent && sent.ok ? 'Send again' : 'Send to the test phone') + '</button></div></div>';
    var mb = document.getElementById('modalBox'); if (mb && mb.querySelector('.w171tm')) mb.innerHTML = html; else openModal(html);
  }
  async function testGo(btn) {
    var ev = curEv(); var rid = S.testRid; if (!ev || !rid || S.busy) return;
    S.busy = true; if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026'; }
    var role = S.testRole;
    try {
      var res = await db.functions.invoke('quo-send', { body: { action: 'test_confirm', roster_id: rid } });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      var d = res.data || {};
      if (d.error) throw new Error(d.error);
      var okN = (d.results || []).filter(function (x) { return x.ok; }).length;
      S.testSent = { rid: rid, ok: okN > 0, text: d.text || '', at: new Date().toISOString(), err: okN ? '' : String(((d.results || [])[0] || {}).error || '') };
      if (okN) { S.tested = S.tested || {}; S.tested[role] = true; }
      T(okN ? 'Test sent to the test phone (as ' + one(role).toLowerCase() + ')' : 'The test did not go through');
      loadMsgs(ev.id, true);
    } catch (e) { S.testSent = { rid: rid, ok: false, err: String((e && e.message) || e) }; T('Could not send the test: ' + S.testSent.err.slice(0, 90)); }
    S.busy = false;
    if (document.querySelector('#modalBox .w171tm')) testModal();
  }

  /* ---------- the format ---------- */
  function fmtModal() {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev) return;
    var f = ev.format || '';
    var raw = S.evRaw && S.evRaw.id === ev.id ? S.evRaw : {};
    var html = '<div class="w171m"><h3>How the food is made</h3><p class="sub">' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + ' \u00b7 the confirm page and the itineraries follow this.</p>'
      + '<div class="modes">'
      + '<label class="' + (f === 'home_cooked' ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w171f" value="home_cooked"' + (f === 'home_cooked' ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>Home-cooked</b><small>Cooks cook at home; the pickup driver collects the food on the way (the standard since 2023).</small></span></label>'
      + '<label class="' + (f === 'community_kitchen' ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w171f" value="community_kitchen"' + (f === 'community_kitchen' ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>Community kitchen</b><small>Volunteers cook together at a kitchen in the morning and serve later.</small></span></label></div>'
      + '<div id="w171kit" style="' + (f === 'community_kitchen' ? '' : 'display:none') + '">'
      + '<label class="f">Kitchen (name and address)</label><input type="text" id="w171kp" value="' + E(raw.kitchen_place || '') + '" placeholder="La Poubelle Magnifique, 1221 Rue Crescent">'
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f">Cooking shift</label><input type="text" id="w171ks" value="' + E(raw.kitchen_shift || '') + '" placeholder="9:00 to 12:30"></div>'
      + '<div><label class="f">Kitchen contact (a name)</label><input type="text" id="w171kc" value="' + E(raw.kitchen_contact || '') + '" placeholder="Shima"></div></div>'
      + '<label class="f">Ingredients (when and where they arrive, fridge space)</label><input type="text" id="w171ki" value="' + E(raw.ingredients_note || '') + '"></div>'
      + '<div class="foot"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171close="1">Cancel</button><button class="w171btn" data-w171fsave="1">Save</button></div></div>';
    openModal(html);
  }
  async function loadRaw(ev) {
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,format,kitchen_place,kitchen_shift,kitchen_contact,ingredients_note').eq('id', ev.id).maybeSingle(); S.evRaw = (r && r.data) || null; } catch (e) { S.evRaw = null; }
  }
  async function fmtSave(btn) {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev) return;
    var pick = document.querySelector('input[name="w171f"]:checked');
    if (!pick) { T('Choose home-cooked or community kitchen'); return; }
    var v = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value.trim() || null : null; };
    var row = { format: pick.value };
    if (pick.value === 'community_kitchen') { row.kitchen_place = v('w171kp'); row.kitchen_shift = v('w171ks'); row.kitchen_contact = v('w171kc'); row.ingredients_note = v('w171ki'); }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update(row).eq('id', ev.id);
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      ev.format = row.format; if (S.evRaw && S.evRaw.id === ev.id) Object.assign(S.evRaw, row);
      T('Saved: ' + FORMATS[row.format]);
      closeModal(); decorateMast();
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); btn.disabled = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- the page refreshes itself while it is open, so a tap on a phone shows up here ---------- */
  function rosterSig(rows) { return (rows || []).map(function (r) { return r.id + ':' + (r.status || '') + ':' + (r.confirm_status || '') + ':' + (r.confirmed_at || '') + ':' + (r.recipe_id || '') + ':' + JSON.stringify(r.confirm || {}); }).sort().join('|'); }
  async function tick() {
    if (document.hidden || !onEvent() || S.busy) return;
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('event_roster').select('id,status,confirm_status,confirmed_at,confirm,recipe_id,signup_noticed_at').eq('event_id', ev.id);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      await loadMsgs(ev.id, true); await loadResp(ev.id, true);
      if (rosterSig(rows) !== rosterSig(D.roster)) {
        var by = {}; rows.forEach(function (x) { by[x.id] = x; });
        var changed = false;
        D.roster.forEach(function (x) { var n = by[x.id]; if (n && rosterSig([n]) !== rosterSig([x])) { Object.assign(x, n); changed = true; } });
        if (rows.length !== D.roster.length) { if (typeof waVolLoadEvent === 'function') { await waVolLoadEvent(ev.id); changed = true; } }
        if (changed) {
          ev.roster_confirmed = (curD().roster || []).filter(function (x) { return lower(x.status) === 'confirmed'; }).length;
          if (typeof waVolRenderEvent === 'function') waVolRenderEvent();
          return;
        }
      }
      paintCard();
    } catch (e) { /* offline: try again next time */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Text settings: the mode, the test phone, what volunteers see per role ---------- */
  async function loadGuides() {
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_role_guides').select('*').order('role'); S.guides = (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { S.guides = S.guides || []; }
    return S.guides;
  }
  async function loadPhotos() {
    if (S.photos) return S.photos;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_photos').select('id,title,path_thumb,path_web,orientation,scene').eq('status', 'ready').eq('usage', 'ready').not('path_web', 'is', null).order('id', { ascending: true }).limit(400);
      S.photos = (r && r.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { S.photos = []; }
    return S.photos;
  }
  async function myEmail() { try { var u = await db.auth.getUser(); return (u && u.data && u.data.user && u.data.user.email) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function guideOf(role) { return (S.guides || []).filter(function (g) { return g.role === role; })[0] || null; }
  /* wave172: the survey after the serving (the Volunteer Survey from the master SOP) */
  var SV_ROLES = ['server', 'cook', 'driver', 'photographer', 'videographer', 'team_leader', 'community_debrief', 'dishwasher', 'volunteer'];
  function surveyHtml(cn) {
    var all = String(S.cfg.sms_survey_roles || '*').trim() === '*';
    var on = all ? SV_ROLES.slice() : String(S.cfg.sms_survey_roles || '').split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); });
    var dis = cn.admin ? '' : ' disabled';
    return '<div class="w171h" style="margin-top:18px">The survey after the serving</div>'
      + '<p class="w171note">A thank-you text with the volunteer\u2019s own link goes to the confirmed volunteers in these roles, from this hour on the day of the serving (or the next day if it was missed). The survey is on their page, tailored to their role (the questions are under each role below); the answers show on the event.</p>'
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f">Who gets it</label>' + SV_ROLES.map(function (r) { return '<label style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font-size:13.5px;margin:3px 0"><input type="checkbox" data-w171svr="' + r + '"' + (on.indexOf(r) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + dis + '> ' + E(one(r)) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div><label class="f">From (hour, 24 h, Toronto)</label><input type="text" id="w171svh" value="' + E(S.cfg.sms_survey_hour || '18') + '"' + dis + '></div></div>'
      + (cn.admin ? '<div class="foot" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171svsave="1">Save the survey settings</button></div>' : '');
  }
  async function surveySave(btn) {
    var picked = Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w171svr]:checked'), function (c) { return c.getAttribute('data-w171svr'); });
    var roles = picked.length === SV_ROLES.length ? '*' : picked.join(',');
    var hour = String(parseInt((document.getElementById('w171svh') || {}).value, 10));
    if (!(Number(hour) >= 8 && Number(hour) <= 19)) { T('The hour is between 8 and 19'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var now = new Date().toISOString();
      var rows = [['sms_survey_roles', roles], ['sms_survey_hour', hour]];
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var r = await db.from('app_config').update({ value: rows[i][1], updated_at: now }).eq('key', rows[i][0]); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.cfg[rows[i][0]] = rows[i][1]; }
      T('Survey settings saved');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  /* wave172: the role's forms and guides, one per line: when | English | French | link */
  function linksText(g) { return ((g && g.links) || []).map(function (l) { return [l.when || 'before', l.label_en || '', l.label_fr || '', l.url || ''].join(' | '); }).join('\n'); }
  function linksParse(txt) {
    var out = [], bad = 0;
    String(txt || '').split('\n').forEach(function (line) {
      if (!line.trim()) return;
      var p = line.split('|').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); });
      var when = lower(p[0]);
      if (['before', 'during', 'after'].indexOf(when) < 0) { when = 'before'; p.unshift('before'); }
      var url = p[p.length - 1];
      if (p.length < 3 || !/^(https:\/\/|mailto:)/i.test(url)) { bad++; return; }
      out.push({ when: when, label_en: p[1], label_fr: p.length >= 4 ? p[2] : p[1], url: url });
    });
    return { links: out, bad: bad };
  }
  /* wave173: where drivers pick up cooks' food, and the drop-off for everyone else (staff edit) */
  function cookHtml() {
    return '<div class="w171h" style="margin-top:18px">Cooks: pickup areas and the drop-off</div>'
      + '<p class="w171note">Cooks choose pickup from home only in these neighbourhoods; anyone else drops their food off. From the old website.</p>'
      + '<label class="f">Pickup areas, one per line</label><textarea id="w171areas" style="min-height:120px">' + E(S.cfg.cook_pickup_areas || '') + '</textarea>'
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f">Drop-off place</label><input type="text" id="w171dplace" value="' + E(S.cfg.cook_dropoff_place || '') + '"></div>'
      + '<div><label class="f">Drop off by (24 h, for example 12:00)</label><input type="text" id="w171dtime" value="' + E(S.cfg.cook_dropoff_time || '') + '"></div></div>'
      + '<div class="foot" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171cooksave="1">Save the pickup areas</button></div>';
  }
  async function cookSave(btn) {
    var areas = ((document.getElementById('w171areas') || {}).value || '').split('\n').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    var place = ((document.getElementById('w171dplace') || {}).value || '').trim();
    var time = ((document.getElementById('w171dtime') || {}).value || '').trim();
    if (!areas || !place) { T('The areas and the drop-off place are needed'); return; }
    if (!/^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(time)) { T('The time looks like 12:00'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var now = new Date().toISOString();
      var rows = [['cook_pickup_areas', areas], ['cook_dropoff_place', place], ['cook_dropoff_time', time]];
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var r = await db.from('app_config').update({ value: rows[i][1], updated_at: now }).eq('key', rows[i][0]); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.cfg[rows[i][0]] = rows[i][1]; }
      T('Pickup areas saved');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  function modeHtml(cn) {

      + opt('live', 'On', 'Confirmation texts go to volunteers ' + (S.cfg.sms_confirm_days || 10) + ' days before each serving (then the Monday reminder and the Thursday check-in, see Setup), and YES, NO and STOP replies update the roster.')
      + '</div>' + (cn.admin ? '<div id="w171mconf"></div>' : '<p class="w171note">Only an admin can switch texts on or off.</p>');

'on' : '') + '" style="background-image:url(&quot;' + E(ST + (p.path_thumb || p.path_web)) + '&quot;)"></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f">What to expect (English, one per line)</label><textarea id="w171een">' + E((g.expect_en || []).join('\n')) + '</textarea></div><div><label class="f">What to expect (French)</label><textarea id="w171efr">' + E((g.expect_fr || []).join('\n')) + '</textarea></div></div>'

    var mb = document.getElementById('modalBox'), back = document.getElementById('modalBack');

  async function saveGuide(btn) {
    var g = guideOf(S.set.role); if (!g) return;
    var v = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value : ''; };
    var lines = function (id) { return v(id).split('\n').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); };
    var lk = linksParse(v('w171lnk'));
    if (lk.bad) { T(lk.bad + ' link line' + (lk.bad === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' need a link starting with https:// or mailto:'); return; }
    var row = { links: lk.links, label_en: v('w171len').trim() || g.label_en, label_fr: v('w171lfr').trim() || g.label_fr, photo_id: S.set.photo || null,
      expect_en: lines('w171een'), expect_fr: lines('w171efr'), bring_en: v('w171ben').trim() || null, bring_fr: v('w171bfr').trim() || null,
      arrive_en: v('w171aen').trim() || null, arrive_fr: v('w171afr').trim() || null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: await myEmail() };
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_role_guides').update(row).eq('role', g.role);
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      Object.assign(g, row); T('Saved: what a ' + row.label_en.toLowerCase() + ' sees');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
  function modeAsk(v) {
    var box = document.getElementById('w171mconf'); if (!box) return;
    if (v === S.mode) { box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    var what = { off: 'Switch texts off? Anything still queued is not sent.', test: 'Switch to test mode? Only the test phone gets texts.', live: 'Switch texts on? Volunteers on upcoming servings get their confirmation text 7 days before (anyone already inside 7 days gets it at the next quarter hour, between 9 am and 7 pm).' }[v];
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="' + (v === 'live' ? 'warn' : 'info') + '">' + E(what) + '<div class="foot" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171mno="1">Keep ' + E((MODES[S.mode] || S.mode).toLowerCase()) + '</button><button class="w171btn" data-w171mset="' + v + '">Yes, ' + (v === 'live' ? 'switch texts on' : v === 'test' ? 'use test mode' : 'switch them off') + '</button></div></div>';
  }
  async function modeSet(v, btn) {

    } catch (e) { T('Could not switch: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); btn.disabled = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- the Volunteering calendar: the format on each upcoming serving ---------- */
  function decorateCalendar() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    var today = todayIso();
    sec.querySelectorAll('.vl-ev[data-vlev]').forEach(function (el) {
      var e = ((typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === el.getAttribute('data-vlev'); })[0];
      if (!e || (e.kind || 'service') !== 'service' || e.event_date < today || e.status === 'cancelled') return;
      var want = e.format ? '<span class="w171fmt cal">' + E(FORMATS[e.format] || e.format) + '</span>' : '';
      var old = el.querySelector('.w171fmt'); if (old && old.outerHTML === want) { /* same */ } else { if (old) old.remove(); if (want) el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', want); }
      var gaps = el.querySelector('.w119gaps');
      if (gaps) {
        var g = gaps.querySelector('.w171gap');
        if (!e.format && !g) gaps.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w171gap">format not set</span>');
        if (e.format && g) g.remove();
        var byl = gaps.querySelector('.w171byl');
        var n = Number(e.roster_by_link || 0);
        if (n && !byl) gaps.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="ok w171byl">' + n + ' by text</span>');
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- Home and the Executive dashboard: the next serving's confirmations (staff) ---------- */
  async function loadNext(force) {
    if (!force && S.next && Date.now() - S.nextAt < 60000) return S.next;
    try {
      var today = todayIso();
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_event_summary').select('id,code,event_date,title,location,status,kind,format,roster_count,roster_confirmed,roster_by_link,roster_declined,roster_texted').eq('kind', 'service').gte('event_date', today).neq('status', 'cancelled').order('event_date', { ascending: true }).limit(8);
      var evs = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (e) { return e.event_date >= today && e.status !== 'cancelled' && (e.kind || 'service') === 'service' && (!e.location || lower(e.location).indexOf('toronto') < 0); });
      evs.sort(function (a, b) { return a.event_date < b.event_date ? -1 : a.event_date > b.event_date ? 1 : 0; });
      var ev = evs[0] || null;
      var rows = [];
      if (ev) { var rr = await db.from('event_roster').select('id,person_name,role,status,confirm_status,confirmed_at').eq('event_id', ev.id); rows = (rr && rr.data) || []; }
      await loadCfg(false);
      S.next = ev ? { ev: ev, rows: rows } : { ev: null, rows: [] }; S.nextAt = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { S.next = S.next || { ev: null, rows: [] }; }
    return S.next;
  }
  function nextHtml(n) {
    if (!n || !n.ev) return '';
    var ev = n.ev, rows = n.rows.filter(function (r) { return !isOff(r); });
    var conf = rows.filter(isConf), decl = rows.filter(isDecl), wait = rows.filter(function (r) { return !isConf(r) && !isDecl(r); });
    var bytext = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.confirm_status === 'confirmed'; }).length;
    var pct = rows.length ? Math.round(conf.length / rows.length * 100) : 0;
    var d = Math.round((new Date(ev.event_date + 'T12:00:00Z') - new Date(todayIso() + 'T12:00:00Z')) / 86400000);
    var when = d === 0 ? 'today' : d === 1 ? 'tomorrow' : 'in ' + d + ' days';
    var names = wait.slice(0, 6).map(function (r) { return first(r.person_name) + ' (' + one(r.role).toLowerCase() + ')'; }).join(', ') + (wait.length > 6 ? ' and ' + (wait.length - 6) + ' more' : '');
    var mode = S.mode || 'off';
    return '<div class="k">Next serving \u00b7 ' + E(when) + (ev.format ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(FORMATS[ev.format] || ev.format) : '') + '</div>'
      + '<h3>' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + '</h3>'
      + '<p><b style="color:' + GOLD + '">' + conf.length + ' of ' + rows.length + '</b> confirmed' + (bytext ? ' \u00b7 ' + bytext + ' by text' : '') + (decl.length ? ' \u00b7 ' + decl.length + ' can\u2019t make it' : '') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>'
      + (wait.length ? '<p>Still waiting: ' + E(names) + '.</p>' : (rows.length ? '<p>Everyone on the list has answered.</p>' : '<p>No one is on the roster yet.</p>'))
      + '<p style="font-size:12px;opacity:.8">' + (mode === 'live' ? 'Confirmation texts go 7 days before.' : mode === 'test' ? 'Texts are in test mode.' : 'Texts are off.') + '</p>'
      + '<button class="go" data-w171open="' + ev.id + '">Open the serving</button>';
  }
  function placeNext(host, anchor, before) {
    var n = S.next; var html = nextHtml(n);
    var el = document.getElementById('w171next');
    if (!html || !host) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w171next'; }
    if (el.parentNode !== host) { if (anchor && anchor.parentNode === host) host.insertBefore(el, before ? anchor : anchor.nextSibling); else host.insertBefore(el, host.firstChild); }
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }
  async function paintNext(force) {
    if (!staff()) { var x = document.getElementById('w171next'); if (x) x.remove(); return; }
    var home = document.getElementById('mypage'), ov = document.getElementById('overview');
    var onHome = home && home.classList.contains('active'), onOv = ov && ov.classList.contains('active');
    if (!onHome && !onOv) return;
    await loadNext(force);
    if (onHome) {
      var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'); if (!body) return;
      var a = document.getElementById('w166lead') || document.getElementById('w112wrap') || body.querySelector('.mp-online') || body.querySelector('.mp-chips') || body.querySelector('.mp-head');
      placeNext(body, a, false);
    } else if (onOv) {
      var w = document.getElementById('w128'); if (!w) return;
      var k = w.querySelector(':scope > .w128kpis') || w.querySelector(':scope > .w128brief');
      if (!k) return;
      placeNext(w, k, false);
    }
  }
  /* =====================================================================
     wave174 (30/09/26): surveys for every role, the serving's time, and the itineraries by role.
     On the event page: a "Survey and itinerary" card under the Texts card (the start time, each role's arrival, the
     driver parts, previews of each itinerary and survey as a test link, who answered the survey, what they said:
     overall, the needs people told us about, what slowed the serving, the meal, and every answer in full); each roster
     card says "Survey answered" and, for a driver, main or pickup (leaders click it to switch). Text settings gains the
     itinerary settings (the meeting spot, the storage unit and its access, the dishwashing, the notes, the pickup lead
     time, the hour of the day-before text) and the survey questions per role (switch one off).
     ===================================================================== */
  var W4 = { resp: {}, respAt: {}, qs: null, itin: null, times: {} };
  var KIND = { main: 'Main driver', pickup: 'Pickup driver', both: 'Only driver' };
  var RUN_ADDR = [['storage_address', 'Storage unit address (for the map)'], ['serving_address', 'Serving address (for the map)'], ['dish_address', 'Dishwashing address after the serving (for the map; empty for none)']];
  var RUN_MIN = [['run_cook_min', 'Minutes at each cook', '7'], ['run_storage_min', 'Minutes loading at the storage unit', '15'], ['run_setup_min', 'At the serving this many minutes before it starts', '30'],
    ['run_serve_min', 'The serving lasts (minutes)', '105'], ['run_dish_min', 'Minutes for the dishes', '45'], ['run_close_min', 'Minutes putting away at the storage unit', '20'], ['run_drive_factor', 'Traffic factor on driving times', '1.25']];
  var ITIN_KEYS = [['meet_spot', 'Meeting spot at the serving'], ['storage_place', 'Storage unit (address and how to find it)'], ['storage_access', 'Storage access (door and locker codes; team leaders and drivers only)'],
    ['dish_place', 'Dishwashing after the serving'], ['pickup_lead_min', 'Pickup driver starts this many minutes before the serving (only until the run is worked out)'], ['audit_url', 'Audit and restock form (team leaders)']];
  var CSS4 = ''
    + '.w174card .t{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:4px 0 8px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w174card .t input{width:92px;font:14px ' + BODY + ';border:1.5px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:10px;padding:5px 8px;background:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + '.w174roles{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:3px 10px;font-size:12.5px;margin:4px 0 8px;}'
    + '.w174roles b{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:15px;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '.w174bars{margin:4px 0 8px;}'
    + '.w174bars div{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 34px;gap:6px;align-items:center;font-size:12px;margin:3px 0;}'
    + '.w174bars i{display:block;height:7px;border-radius:7px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);overflow:hidden;grid-column:1/-1;margin-top:-2px;}'
    + '.w174bars i s{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GOLD + ';text-decoration:none;}'
    + '.w174bars span:last-child{text-align:right;font-weight:700;}'
    + '.w174ans{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:10px 0;font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w174ans .h{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:baseline;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 4px;}'
    + '.w174ans dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1.2fr);gap:3px 12px;margin:0;}'
    + '.w174ans dt{color:' + MUTED + ';}.w174ans dd{margin:0;font-weight:600;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;}'
    + '@media (max-width:620px){.w174ans dl{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w174ans dd{margin-bottom:4px;}}'
    + '.w171tag[data-w174drv]{cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w174q{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;padding:5px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w174q small{display:block;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:11.5px;}'
    + '.w174rmin{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:8px 12px;margin:8px 0 4px;}.w174rmin label{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';}.w174rmin input{max-width:120px;}';
  if (!document.getElementById('wa174css')) { var st4 = document.createElement('style'); st4.id = 'wa174css'; st4.textContent = CSS4; document.head.appendChild(st4); }

  function hm12(s) { var m = String(s || '').match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); if (!m) return String(s || ''); var h = Number(m[1]); return (h % 12 || 12) + ':' + m[2] + (h < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm'); }
  function addMin(s, n) { var m = String(s || '12:00').match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); var x = (Number(m[1]) * 60 + Number(m[2]) + n + 1440) % 1440; return ('0' + Math.floor(x / 60)).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + (x % 60)).slice(-2); }
  async function loadResp(evId, force) {
    if (!evId) return [];
    if (!force && W4.resp[evId] && Date.now() - (W4.respAt[evId] || 0) < 25000) return W4.resp[evId];
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_survey_responses').select('id,roster_id,role,lang,answers,submitted_at,updated_at').eq('event_id', evId); W4.resp[evId] = (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { W4.resp[evId] = W4.resp[evId] || []; }
    W4.respAt[evId] = Date.now();
    return W4.resp[evId];
  }
  async function loadQs(force) {
    if (W4.qs && !force) return W4.qs;
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_survey_questions').select('*').order('sort').order('id'); W4.qs = (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { W4.qs = W4.qs || []; }
    return W4.qs;
  }
  async function loadStart(ev) {
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,start_time').eq('id', ev.id).maybeSingle(); if (r && r.data) ev.start_time = String(r.data.start_time || '12:00').slice(0, 5); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return ev.start_time || '12:00';
  }
  /* a driver's part, as the database decides it (wa174_driver_kind): set by hand, or the first driver main and the next pickup */
  function driverKinds(D) {
    var drv = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return lower(r.role) === 'driver' && !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r); });
    var rank = function (r) { var k = (r.detail || {}).driver_role; return k === 'main' || k === 'both' ? 0 : k === 'pickup' ? 1 : 2; };
    drv.sort(function (a, b) { return rank(a) - rank(b) || (a.sort == null ? 1e9 : a.sort) - (b.sort == null ? 1e9 : b.sort) || a.id - b.id; });
    var out = {};
    drv.forEach(function (r, i) { var k = (r.detail || {}).driver_role; out[r.id] = ['main', 'pickup', 'both'].indexOf(k) >= 0 ? k : drv.length === 1 ? 'both' : i === 0 ? 'main' : 'pickup'; });
    return out;
  }
  function qOf(role, key) { return (W4.qs || []).filter(function (q) { return q.qkey === key && (q.role === role || q.role === '*'); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.role === '*') - (b.role === '*'); })[0]; }
  function optLabel(q, v) {
    if (v && typeof v === 'object') return 'Other: ' + v.other;
    var o = ((q && q.options) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.v === v; })[0]; return o ? o.en : String(v);
  }
  function ansText(q, v) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(function (x) { return optLabel(q, x); }).join(', ');
    if (q && q.kind === 'choice') return optLabel(q, v);
    if (q && q.kind === 'scale') return v + ' of ' + (q.max_v == null ? 5 : q.max_v);
    return String(v);
  }
  function tally(rows, key, qRole) {
    var c = {}, n = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (x) { var v = (x.answers || {})[key]; if (v == null) return; n++; (Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]).forEach(function (y) { var k = y && typeof y === 'object' ? 'Other: ' + y.other : optLabel(qOf(qRole || x.role, key), y); c[k] = (c[k] || 0) + 1; }); });
    return { n: n, list: Object.keys(c).map(function (k) { return [k, c[k]]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; }) };
  }
  function barsHtml(title, tl, max) {
    if (!tl.n) return '';
    return '<div class="w171h">' + E(title) + ' <span style="font-weight:600;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0">(' + tl.n + ' answered)</span></div><div class="w174bars">' + tl.list.slice(0, max || 6).map(function (p) {
      return '<div><span>' + E(p[0]) + '</span><span>' + p[1] + '</span><i><s style="width:' + Math.round(100 * p[1] / tl.n) + '%"></s></i></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function avg(rows, key) { var v = rows.map(function (x) { return (x.answers || {})[key]; }).filter(function (x) { return typeof x === 'number'; }); return v.length ? Math.round(10 * v.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / v.length) / 10 : null; }
  function w174Html(ev, D, cn, rows) {
    var st = ev.start_time || '12:00';
    var up = ev.event_date >= todayIso() && ev.status !== 'cancelled';
    var dk = driverKinds(D);
    var guides = S.guides || [];
    var g = function (r) { return guides.filter(function (x) { return x.role === r; })[0] || {}; };
    var lead = Number((W4.itin || {}).pickup_lead_min || 75);
    var roleRow = function (label, from, to) { return '<b>' + E(hm12(from)) + '</b><span>' + E(label) + (to ? ' (to ' + E(hm12(to)) + ')' : '') + '</span>'; };
    /* wave191m: with a worked-out run (the run card), the drivers' and the team leader's starts come from it */
    var run = (window.WA191RUN && typeof WA191RUN.get === 'function') ? WA191RUN.get(ev.id) : null;
    var runAt = function (r) { var f = (r.stops || [])[0] || {}; return r.at === 'storage' ? 'at the storage unit' : r.at === 'serving' ? 'at the serving' : f.k === 'kitchen' ? 'at the kitchen' : 'at the first pickup'; };
    var times = run && (run.runs || []).length ? (run.runs || []).map(function (r) {
      var who = (r.names || []).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
      return roleRow((r.kind === 'none' ? 'The driver (none yet)' : (KIND[r.kind] || 'Driver') + (who ? ' ' + who : '')) + (r.with_tl ? ' and the team leader' : '') + ' ' + runAt(r), r.start, r.end);
    }).join('')
      : roleRow('Team leader and main driver at the storage unit', addMin(st, g('team_leader').arrive_offset_min == null ? -60 : g('team_leader').arrive_offset_min))
      + roleRow('Pickup driver starts the pickups', addMin(st, -lead));
    times = times
      + roleRow('Community lead (clothing count)', addMin(st, g('community_debrief').arrive_offset_min == null ? -15 : g('community_debrief').arrive_offset_min))
      + roleRow('Serving: servers, photographers', st, addMin(st, g('server').duration_min || 120));
    var list = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r); });
    var conf = list.filter(isConf);
    var byId = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { byId[r.id] = r; });
    var answered = rows.filter(function (x) { return byId[x.roster_id]; });
    /* previews: the first person on the list per itinerary kind, as a test link (saves nothing) */
    var pv = [];
    var seen = {};
    list.forEach(function (r) {
      var role = lower(r.role); if (!r.confirm_token) return;
      var kind = role === 'driver' ? 'driver ' + (dk[r.id] || '') : role;
      if (seen[kind]) return; seen[kind] = 1;
      pv.push('<a class="w171btn ghost" style="text-decoration:none" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="https://hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + E(r.confirm_token) + '?test=1">' + E(role === 'driver' ? (KIND[dk[r.id]] || 'Driver') : one(role)) + '</a>');
    });
    var srv = answered.filter(function (x) { return ['server', 'team_leader', 'community_debrief', 'community_talker'].indexOf(x.role) >= 0; });
    var mealAvg = avg(answered, 'meal_rating');
    var h = '<h4>Survey and itinerary</h4>'
      + '<div class="t">Serving starts at ' + (cn.text || staff() ? '<input type="time" id="w174st" value="' + E(st) + '"' + (up ? '' : ' disabled') + '> <button class="w171btn ghost" data-w174st="1"' + (up ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Save</button>' : '<b>' + E(hm12(st)) + '</b>') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w174roles">' + times + '</div>'
      + '<p class="w171note">Confirmed volunteers see their itinerary on their page from the day before (team leaders and drivers, servers and photographers, the Community lead; cooks have none), and get a text with it at ' + E(hm12(((S.cfg.sms_itinerary_hour || '17') + ':00'))) + ' when texts are on. After the serving, everyone confirmed gets their own survey. Previews (test links, nothing is saved):</p>'
      + (pv.length ? '<div class="w171btns">' + pv.join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w171note">No one on the list yet.</p>')
      + '<div class="w171h">Survey</div>'
      + '<div class="w171nums" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)"><div><b>' + answered.length + '</b><span>answered of ' + conf.length + ' confirmed</span></div>'
      + '<div><b>' + (mealAvg == null ? '\u2013' : mealAvg) + '</b><span>meal rating (0 to 5)</span></div>'
      + '<div><b>' + (avg(answered, 'meals') == null ? '\u2013' : avg(answered, 'meals')) + '</b><span>meals served (team leader)</span></div></div>'
      + barsHtml('Overall', tally(answered, 'overall', '*'))
      + barsHtml('Needs people told us about', tally(srv, 'needs'), 9)
      + barsHtml('What slowed the serving', tally(answered.filter(function (x) { return (x.answers || {}).barriers; }), 'barriers'))
      + barsHtml('The meal, as servers saw it', tally(answered, 'meal_reaction', 'server'))
      + (answered.length ? '<div class="w171btns"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w174read="1">Read every answer</button></div>' : '<p class="w171note" style="margin:0">' + (ev.event_date > todayIso() ? 'The survey opens an hour after the serving starts.' : 'No answers yet.') + '</p>');
    return h;
  }
  async function paintW174() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); var rail = document.getElementById('w119rail');
    if (!ev || !D || !rail || !staff()) return;
    var cn = await can();
    await Promise.all([loadResp(ev.id, false), loadQs(false), S.guides ? null : loadGuides(), W4.itin ? null : loadItin(), ev.start_time ? null : loadStart(ev)]);
    if (curEv() !== ev) return;
    rail = document.getElementById('w119rail'); if (!rail) return;
    var box = document.getElementById('w174card');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w174card'; box.className = 'cardy w174card'; var after = document.getElementById('w171card'); if (after && after.parentNode === rail) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box); else rail.insertBefore(box, rail.firstChild); }
    var html = w174Html(ev, D, cn, W4.resp[ev.id] || []);
    /* wave191: two cards: the itinerary (Prepare) and the survey (Wrap up), so the serving page can place each in its stage */
    var mark = '<div class="w171h">Survey</div>'; var at = html.indexOf(mark);
    var h1 = at > 0 ? html.slice(0, at).replace('<h4>Survey and itinerary</h4>', '<h4>Itinerary</h4>') : html;
    var h2 = at > 0 ? '<h4>Survey</h4>' + html.slice(at + mark.length) : '';
    if (box.__h !== h1 && !(document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w174st')) { box.innerHTML = h1; box.__h = h1; }
    var box2 = document.getElementById('w174survey');
    if (!box2) { box2 = document.createElement('div'); box2.id = 'w174survey'; box2.className = 'cardy w174card w174survey'; box.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box2); }
    if (box2.__h !== h2) { box2.innerHTML = h2; box2.__h = h2; box2.style.display = h2 ? '' : 'none'; }
  }
  /* the roster cards: survey answered, a driver's part */
  function w174Tags(r, evId, dk) {
    if (isOff(r) || isDecl(r)) return '';
    var out = '';
    if (lower(r.role) === 'driver' && dk[r.id]) out += '<span class="w171tag mute" data-w174drv="' + r.id + '" title="' + (staff() ? 'Click to switch main or pickup' : '') + '">' + E(KIND[dk[r.id]]) + '</span>';
    var a = (W4.resp[evId] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.roster_id === r.id; })[0];
    if (a) out += '<span class="w171tag ok">Survey answered \u00b7 ' + E(fmtWhen(a.updated_at || a.submitted_at)) + '</span>';
    return out;
  }
  async function saveStart(btn) {
    var ev = curEv(); var el = document.getElementById('w174st'); if (!ev || !el) return;
    var v = String(el.value || '').slice(0, 5);
    if (!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)) { T('The time looks like 12:00'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update({ start_time: v }).eq('id', ev.id); if (r.error) throw r.error; ev.start_time = v; T('The serving starts at ' + hm12(v)); document.getElementById('w174card').__h = ''; paintW174(); }
    catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); btn.disabled = false; }
  }
  async function switchDriver(rid) {
    var D = curD(); if (!D) return;
    var r = (D.roster || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === rid; })[0]; if (!r) return;
    var dk = driverKinds(D); var next = dk[rid] === 'main' ? 'pickup' : dk[rid] === 'pickup' ? 'both' : 'main';
    var detail = Object.assign({}, r.detail || {}, { driver_role: next });
    try { var res = await db.from('event_roster').update({ detail: detail }).eq('id', rid); if (res.error) throw res.error; r.detail = detail; T(first(r.person_name) + ': ' + KIND[next].toLowerCase()); decorateCards(); document.getElementById('w174card').__h = ''; paintW174(); }
    catch (e) { T('Could not switch: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
  }
  function readModal() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D) return;
    var byId = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { byId[r.id] = r; });
    var rows = (W4.resp[ev.id] || []).filter(function (x) { return byId[x.roster_id]; });
    var html = '<div class="w171m"><h3>Survey answers</h3><p class="sub">' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + ' \u00b7 ' + rows.length + ' answered. Staff only.</p>';
    rows.forEach(function (x) {
      var r = byId[x.roster_id]; var a = x.answers || {};
      var qs = (W4.qs || []).filter(function (q) { return (q.role === '*' || q.role === x.role) && a[q.qkey] != null; }).sort(function (p, q) { return p.sort - q.sort; });
      html += '<div class="w174ans"><div class="h"><b>' + E(r.person_name || 'Unnamed') + '</b><span class="w171tag mute" style="display:inline-block;margin:0">' + E(one(x.role)) + '</span><span style="color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:11.5px">' + E(fmtWhen(x.updated_at || x.submitted_at)) + (x.lang === 'fr' ? ' \u00b7 in French' : '') + '</span></div><dl>'
        + qs.map(function (q) { return '<dt>' + E(q.label_en) + '</dt><dd>' + E(ansText(q, a[q.qkey])) + '</dd>'; }).join('') + '</dl></div>';
    });
    html += '<div class="foot"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w171close="1">Close</button></div></div>';
    openModal(html);
  }
  /* ---------- Text settings: the itineraries and the survey questions ---------- */
  async function loadItin() {
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_itinerary_settings').select('key,value'); var o = {}; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { o[x.key] = x.value; }); W4.itin = o; } catch (e) { W4.itin = W4.itin || {}; }
    return W4.itin;
  }
  function itinSetHtml(cn) {
    var o = W4.itin || {};
    return '<div class="w171h" style="margin-top:18px">Itineraries</div>'
      + '<p class="w171note">What volunteers see on their page from the day before. The storage access shows only to team leaders and drivers.</p>'
      + ITIN_KEYS.map(function (k) { return '<label class="f">' + E(k[1]) + '</label><input type="text" data-w174it="' + k[0] + '" value="' + E(o[k[0]] || '') + '">'; }).join('')
      /* wave191m: what the drivers' run is worked out from (the serving-run function; the cron works every serving of the next 14 days out again when these change) */
      + '<div class="w171h" style="margin-top:14px">The drivers\u2019 run</div><p class="w171note">The Hub works out each driver\u2019s stops and times from these, with OpenStreetMap driving times. Addresses as Google Maps would find them; no door codes here.</p>'
      + RUN_ADDR.map(function (k) { return '<label class="f">' + E(k[1]) + '</label><input type="text" data-w174it="' + k[0] + '" value="' + E(o[k[0]] || '') + '">'; }).join('')
      + '<div class="w174rmin">' + RUN_MIN.map(function (k) { return '<label><span>' + E(k[1]) + '</span><input type="number" min="0" max="' + (k[0] === 'run_drive_factor' ? 3 : 240) + '" step="' + (k[0] === 'run_drive_factor' ? '0.05' : '1') + '" data-w174it="' + k[0] + '" value="' + E(o[k[0]] || '') + '" placeholder="' + E(k[2]) + '"></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="two"><div><label class="f">Important notes (English, one per line)</label><textarea data-w174it="notes_en">' + E(o.notes_en || '') + '</textarea></div><div><label class="f">Important notes (French)</label><textarea data-w174it="notes_fr">' + E(o.notes_fr || '') + '</textarea></div></div>'
      + '<label class="f">The itinerary text goes the day before at (hour, 24 h, Toronto)</label><input type="text" id="w174ih" value="' + E(S.cfg.sms_itinerary_hour || '17') + '"' + (cn.admin ? '' : ' disabled') + ' style="max-width:120px">'
      + '<div class="foot" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="w171btn ghost" data-w174itsave="1">Save the itineraries</button></div>';
  }
  async function itinSave(btn) {
    var rows = Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w174it]'), function (el) { return { key: el.getAttribute('data-w174it'), value: el.value.trim() || null }; });
    var lead = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.key === 'pickup_lead_min'; })[0];
    if (lead && lead.value && !(Number(lead.value) >= 0 && Number(lead.value) <= 240)) { T('The pickup lead time is minutes, 0 to 240'); return; }
    var badMin = rows.filter(function (x) { return /^run_/.test(x.key) && x.key !== 'run_drive_factor' && x.value != null && !(/^\d+$/.test(x.value) && Number(x.value) <= 240); })[0];
    if (badMin) { T('The run\u2019s minutes are whole numbers, 0 to 240'); return; }
    var fac = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.key === 'run_drive_factor'; })[0];
    if (fac && fac.value != null && !(Number(fac.value) >= 0.8 && Number(fac.value) <= 3)) { T('The traffic factor is between 0.8 and 3 (1.25 adds a quarter)'); return; }
    var au = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.key === 'audit_url'; })[0];
    if (au && au.value && !/^https:\/\//.test(au.value)) { T('The audit form link must start with https://'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var who = await myEmail(), now = new Date().toISOString();
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_itinerary_settings').upsert(rows.map(function (x) { return Object.assign(x, { updated_at: now, updated_by: who }); }));
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      rows.forEach(function (x) { W4.itin[x.key] = x.value; });
      var hEl = document.getElementById('w174ih');

      }

  async function refCheck() {
    var v = String((document.getElementById('w175ref') || {}).value || '').trim().toUpperCase(); var out = document.getElementById('w175refout'); if (!out) return;
    if (!/^WA-VH-[0-9A-F]{6}$/.test(v)) { out.textContent = 'A reference looks like WA-VH-89C29E.'; return; }
    try {
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_hour_letters').select('*').eq('ref', v).maybeSingle();
      var x = r && r.data;
      out.innerHTML = x ? '<b>Real letter.</b> ' + E(x.full_name) + ': ' + E(x.servings) + ' servings, about ' + E(x.hours) + ' hours, ' + E(fmtDay(x.first_date)) + ' to ' + E(fmtDay(x.last_date)) + '. Issued ' + E(fmtWhen(x.issued_at)) + '.' : '<b style="color:' + FLAG + '">No letter has this reference.</b>';
    } catch (e) { out.textContent = 'Could not check: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 80); }
  }
  async function signupSave(btn) {
    var roles = String((document.getElementById('w175roles') || {}).value || '').split(',').map(function (x) { return lower(x); }).filter(Boolean);
    var bad = roles.filter(function (x) { return ROLES.indexOf(x) < 0; });
    if (bad.length) { T('Not a role: ' + bad.join(', ') + '. Roles: ' + ROLES.join(', ')); return; }
    var days = String(parseInt((document.getElementById('w175days') || {}).value, 10));
    if (!(Number(days) >= 7 && Number(days) <= 365)) { T('Days ahead: 7 to 365'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var now = new Date().toISOString(), rows = [['vol_signup_roles', roles.join(',')], ['vol_signup_days', days]];
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var r = await db.from('app_config').update({ value: rows[i][1], updated_at: now }).eq('key', rows[i][0]); if (r.error) throw r.error; S.cfg[rows[i][0]] = rows[i][1]; }
      T('Sign-up settings saved');
    } catch (e) { T('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    btn.disabled = false;
  }

  function seasonOf(iso) { var m = Number(String(iso || '').slice(5, 7)); return m === 12 || m <= 2 ? 'winter' : m <= 5 ? 'spring' : m <= 8 ? 'summer' : 'autumn'; }
  function seasonName(s) { return { winter: 'Winter', spring: 'Spring', summer: 'Summer', autumn: 'Autumn' }[s] || s; }
  function hasItin(role) { var g = (S.guides || []).filter(function (x) { return x.role === lower(role); })[0]; return !!(g && g.itinerary); }
  function photoById(id) { return (S.photos || []).filter(function (p) { return p.id === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  async function loadMailMode() {
    if (W8.mail && Date.now() - W8.mailAt < 30000) return W8.mail;
    try { var r = await db.from('app_config').select('key,value').in('key', ['mail_mode', 'mail_test_recipients']); var c = {}; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { c[x.key] = x.value; }); W8.mail = c.mail_mode || 'test'; W8.tests = c.mail_test_recipients || ''; } catch (e) { W8.mail = W8.mail || 'test'; }
    W8.mailAt = Date.now(); return W8.mail;
  }
  async function loadSends(evId, force) {
    if (!evId) return [];
    if (!force && W8.sends[evId] && Date.now() - (W8.sendsAt[evId] || 0) < 25000) return W8.sends[evId];
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa182_itinerary_status', { p_event: evId }); W8.sends[evId] = (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { W8.sends[evId] = W8.sends[evId] || []; }
    W8.sendsAt[evId] = Date.now(); return W8.sends[evId];
  }
  async function loadEv8(ev, force) {
    if (!force && W8.ev[ev.id]) return W8.ev[ev.id];
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,event_date,whatsapp_url,itinerary_photo_id,itinerary_auto,itinerary_channels').eq('id', ev.id).maybeSingle(); W8.ev[ev.id] = (r && r.data) || { id: ev.id }; } catch (e) { W8.ev[ev.id] = W8.ev[ev.id] || { id: ev.id }; }
    try { var lk = await db.rpc('wa182_look', { p_event: ev.id }); W8.ev[ev.id].look = (lk && lk.data) || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return W8.ev[ev.id];
  }
  /* the latest send per person and channel that went (or will go) */
  function lastSend(evId, rid, ch) {
    return (W8.sends[evId] || []).filter(function (s) { return s.roster_id === rid && (!ch || s.channel === ch) && s.status !== 'cancelled'; })[0] || null;
  }
  function sendWord(s) {
    if (!s) return '';
    var ch = s.channel === 'email' ? 'Emailed' : 'Texted';
    if (s.status === 'scheduled') return (s.channel === 'email' ? 'Email' : 'Text') + ' scheduled ' + fmtWhen(s.send_at);
    if (s.status === 'queued') return (s.channel === 'email' ? 'Email' : 'Text') + ' on its way';
    if (s.status === 'sent') return ch + ' ' + fmtWhen(s.created_at) + (s.opened_at ? ' \u00b7 opened' : '');
    if (s.status === 'skipped') return (s.channel === 'email' ? 'Email' : 'Text') + ' skipped: ' + (s.reason || '');
    if (s.status === 'failed') return (s.channel === 'email' ? 'Email' : 'Text') + ' did not go through';
    return s.status;
  }
  function personCh(r) { var em = !!(r.__email); return { text: !!phoneOf(r), email: em }; }

  /* ---------- the block on the Survey and itinerary card ---------- */
  function blockHtml(ev, D, cn) {
    var x = W8.ev[ev.id] || {}; var look = x.look || {};
    var list = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r) && hasItin(r.role); });
    var sent = list.filter(function (r) { var s = lastSend(ev.id, r.id); return s && (s.status === 'sent' || s.status === 'queued'); }).length;
    var sch = (W8.sends[ev.id] || []).filter(function (s) { return s.status === 'scheduled'; });
    var up = ev.event_date >= todayIso() && ev.status !== 'cancelled';
    var autoTxt = x.itinerary_auto === false ? 'Not sent automatically.' : 'Sent automatically the day before at ' + hm12((S.cfg.sms_itinerary_hour || '17') + ':00') + ' to everyone confirmed who has not had it, by ' + ({ text: 'text', email: 'email', both: 'text and email' }[x.itinerary_channels || 'both']) + '.';
    return '<div class="w182blk" id="w182blk">' + (look.hero && look.hero.path ? '<div class="bgp" style="background-image:url(&quot;' + E(ST + look.hero.path) + '&quot;)"></div>' : '')
      + '<div class="ov">Itineraries \u00b7 ' + E(seasonName(look.season || seasonOf(ev.event_date))) + ' look</div><h5>' + sent + ' of ' + list.length + ' sent</h5>'
      + '<div class="w182st"><span class="' + (S.mode === 'live' ? 'ok' : 'wait') + '">Texts ' + E({ off: 'off', test: 'in test mode', live: 'on' }[S.mode] || S.mode) + '</span><span class="' + (W8.mail === 'live' ? 'ok' : 'wait') + '">Email ' + E(W8.mail === 'live' ? 'on' : 'in test mode') + '</span>'
      + (sch.length ? '<span>' + sch.length + ' scheduled</span>' : '') + (x.whatsapp_url ? '<span class="ok">WhatsApp group linked</span>' : '<span>No WhatsApp group yet</span>') + '</div>'
      + '<p>' + E(autoTxt) + ' Team leaders, drivers, servers, photographers and the Community lead get one; cooks don\u2019t.</p>'
      + '<div class="w171btns">' + (cn.text && up ? '<button class="w171btn" data-w182send="1">Send itineraries</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w171btn ghost" data-w182hist="1">Sent and scheduled</button>' + (cn.text || staff() ? '<button class="w171btn ghost" data-w182ev="1">This serving\u2019s itinerary</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  /* wave174's card, with the block under the start time; the previews open the itinerary itself */
  var __w174Html = w174Html;
  w174Html = function (ev, D, cn, rows) {
    var h = __w174Html(ev, D, cn, rows).replace(/\?test=1"/g, '?test=1&amp;it=1"');
    var at = h.indexOf('<div class="w171h">Survey</div>');
